<file path="desktop/src/frontend/ui/confirm_dialog.py">
 1: """
 2: Module: confirm_dialog.py
 3: Purpose: Reusable confirmation modal dialog for DearPyGui.
 4: """
 5: 
 6: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 7: from ..styling import theme as T
 8: from ..styling.fonts import styled_text, LABEL
 9: from .widgets import add_primary_button, add_danger_button
10: 
11: _dialog_counter = 0
12: 
13: 
14: def confirm(message: str, on_confirm, title: str = "Confirm",
15:             confirm_label: str = "Confirm", danger: bool = False):
16:     """Show a modal confirmation dialog.
17: 
18:     Args:
19:         message:       Text to display in the dialog body.
20:         on_confirm:    Callable invoked (no args) when user clicks confirm.
21:         title:         Window title.
22:         confirm_label: Label for the confirm button.
23:         danger:        If True, the confirm button uses the danger theme.
24:     """
25:     global _dialog_counter
26:     _dialog_counter += 1
27:     tag = f"_confirm_dialog_{_dialog_counter}"
28: 
29:     def _on_confirm():
30:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
31:             dpg.delete_item(tag)
32:         on_confirm()
33: 
34:     def _on_cancel():
35:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
36:             dpg.delete_item(tag)
37: 
38:     # Center in viewport
39:     vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
40:     vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
41:     win_w, win_h = 320, 120
42:     pos = [(vp_w - win_w) // 2, (vp_h - win_h) // 2]
43: 
44:     with dpg.window(
45:         tag=tag, label=title, modal=True,
46:         no_resize=True, no_collapse=True,
47:         width=win_w, height=win_h,
48:         pos=pos,
49:         on_close=_on_cancel,
50:     ):
51:         dpg.add_text(message, wrap=win_w - 30)
52:         dpg.add_spacer(height=8)
53:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
54:             dpg.add_spacer(width=win_w - 200)
55:             dpg.add_button(label="Cancel", width=80, callback=_on_cancel)
56:             dpg.add_spacer(width=4)
57:             if danger:
58:                 btn = add_danger_button(confirm_label, width=80, callback=_on_confirm)
59:             else:
60:                 btn = add_primary_button(confirm_label, width=80, callback=_on_confirm)

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/ui/toast.py">
  1: """
  2: Module: toast.py
  3: Purpose: Non-blocking toast notification overlay for DearPyGui.
  4: 
  5: Creates a small floating window at the bottom-right of the viewport that
  6: auto-dismisses after a configurable duration.  Supports success, error,
  7: info, and warning severity levels with distinct styling.
  8: """
  9: 
 10: import time
 11: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 12: 
 13: from ..styling import theme as T
 14: 
 15: # ── Severity presets ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 16: _SEVERITY = {
 17:     "success": {"bg": (11, 110, 79, 230), "border": (52, 211, 153, 255), "icon": "\u2713"},
 18:     "error":   {"bg": (127, 29, 29, 230),  "border": (239, 68, 68, 255),  "icon": "\u2717"},
 19:     "info":    {"bg": (30, 41, 59, 230),   "border": (129, 140, 248, 255),"icon": "\u25cf"},
 20:     "warning": {"bg": (120, 83, 9, 230),   "border": (251, 191, 36, 255), "icon": "\u26a0"},
 21: }
 22: 
 23: # Track active toasts for stacking
 24: _active_toasts: list[dict] = []
 25: _toast_counter = 0
 26: 
 27: _TOAST_WIDTH = 300
 28: _TOAST_HEIGHT = 50
 29: _TOAST_MARGIN = 12
 30: _TOAST_GAP = 6
 31: 
 32: 
 33: def _build_toast_theme(severity: str):
 34:     """Create or return a cached DPG theme for the given severity."""
 35:     tag = f"_toast_theme_{severity}"
 36:     if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 37:         return tag
 38:     preset = _SEVERITY.get(severity, _SEVERITY["info"])
 39:     with dpg.theme(tag=tag):
 40:         with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvAll):
 41:             dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_WindowBg, preset["bg"])
 42:             dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Border, preset["border"])
 43:             dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, 1)
 44:             dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowRounding, 8)
 45:             dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowPadding, 10, 8)
 46:     return tag
 47: 
 48: 
 49: def _reposition_toasts():
 50:     """Stack active toasts from bottom-right of viewport."""
 51:     vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
 52:     vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
 53:     y_offset = _TOAST_MARGIN
 54:     for toast in reversed(_active_toasts):
 55:         tag = toast["tag"]
 56:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 57:             x = vp_w - _TOAST_WIDTH - _TOAST_MARGIN - 16
 58:             y = vp_h - _TOAST_HEIGHT - y_offset - 40
 59:             dpg.configure_item(tag, pos=[x, y])
 60:             y_offset += _TOAST_HEIGHT + _TOAST_GAP
 61: 
 62: 
 63: def _dismiss_toast(tag: str):
 64:     """Remove a toast by tag."""
 65:     global _active_toasts
 66:     _active_toasts = [t for t in _active_toasts if t["tag"] != tag]
 67:     if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 68:         dpg.delete_item(tag)
 69:     _reposition_toasts()
 70: 
 71: 
 72: def tick_toasts():
 73:     """Call once per frame (from App.process_queue / render loop) to auto-dismiss expired toasts."""
 74:     now = time.time()
 75:     expired = [t for t in _active_toasts if t["duration"] > 0 and now - t["time"] >= t["duration"]]
 76:     for t in expired:
 77:         _dismiss_toast(t["tag"])
 78: 
 79: 
 80: def show_toast(message: str, severity: str = "info", duration: float = 3.0):
 81:     """Show a toast notification.
 82: 
 83:     Args:
 84:         message:  Text to display.
 85:         severity: One of 'success', 'error', 'info', 'warning'.
 86:         duration: Seconds before auto-dismiss (0 = sticky).
 87:     """
 88:     global _toast_counter
 89:     _toast_counter += 1
 90:     tag = f"_toast_win_{_toast_counter}"
 91:     theme_tag = _build_toast_theme(severity)
 92:     preset = _SEVERITY.get(severity, _SEVERITY["info"])
 93: 
 94:     # Cap active toasts at 5
 95:     while len(_active_toasts) >= 5:
 96:         oldest = _active_toasts.pop(0)
 97:         if dpg.does_item_exist(oldest["tag"]):
 98:             dpg.delete_item(oldest["tag"])
 99: 
100:     vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
101:     vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
102: 
103:     with dpg.window(
104:         tag=tag,
105:         label="",
106:         no_title_bar=True,
107:         no_resize=True,
108:         no_move=True,
109:         no_collapse=True,
110:         no_scrollbar=True,
111:         no_focus_on_appearing=True,
112:         width=_TOAST_WIDTH,
113:         height=_TOAST_HEIGHT,
114:         pos=[vp_w - _TOAST_WIDTH - _TOAST_MARGIN - 16,
115:              vp_h - _TOAST_HEIGHT - _TOAST_MARGIN - 40],
116:     ):
117:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
118:             dpg.add_text(preset["icon"])
119:             dpg.add_spacer(width=4)
120:             # Truncate long messages
121:             display = message if len(message) <= 40 else message[:37] + "..."
122:             dpg.add_text(display, wrap=_TOAST_WIDTH - 60)
123: 
124:     dpg.bind_item_theme(tag, theme_tag)
125: 
126:     toast_entry = {"tag": tag, "time": time.time(), "duration": duration}
127:     _active_toasts.append(toast_entry)
128:     _reposition_toasts()
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/data_manager.py">
  1: import json
  2: import logging
  3: import os
  4: import threading
  5: 
  6: import yaml
  7: 
  8: log = logging.getLogger("data")
  9: 
 10: 
 11: class DataMixin:
 12:     """Handles all persistence — server-first with local YAML/JSON fallback."""
 13: 
 14:     # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 15: 
 16:     def _discord_id(self) -> str:
 17:         """Return the signed-in Discord user's ID, or empty string."""
 18:         oauth = getattr(self, "_oauth", None)
 19:         if oauth and oauth.user_info:
 20:             return str(oauth.user_info.get("id", ""))
 21:         return ""
 22: 
 23:     # ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 24: 
 25:     def load_data(self):
 26:         """Load library + events from server (fallback to local files)."""
 27:         discord_id = self._discord_id()
 28:         server_data = {}
 29: 
 30:         # Try fetching everything from the server in one call
 31:         if discord_id and getattr(self, "api", None):
 32:             try:
 33:                 server_data = self.api.get_all_user_data(discord_id)
 34:             except Exception as exc:
 35:                 log.warning("Could not fetch cloud data: %s", exc)
 36: 
 37:         # ── Library ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 38:         lib = server_data.get("library", {})
 39:         if not lib:
 40:             # Fallback to local YAML
 41:             for src in [self.LIBRARY_FILE, "lineup_data.yaml"]:
 42:                 if os.path.exists(src):
 43:                     try:
 44:                         with open(src, "r") as f:
 45:                             lib = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
 46:                         break
 47:                     except Exception as e:
 48:                         print(f"Error loading {src}: {e}")
 49: 
 50:         self.saved_titles = lib.get("titles", [])
 51: 
 52:         raw_djs = lib.get("djs", []) or []
 53:         self.saved_djs = []
 54:         for _d in raw_djs:
 55:             if not isinstance(_d, dict):
 56:                 name = _d or ""
 57:                 if not name:
 58:                     continue
 59:                 self.saved_djs.append({"name": name, "stream": ""})
 60:             elif "stream" not in _d:
 61:                 name = _d.get("name") or ""
 62:                 if not name:
 63:                     continue
 64:                 self.saved_djs.append({
 65:                     "name": name,
 66:                     "stream": _d.get("goggles", "") or _d.get("link", "") or "",
 67:                 })
 68:             else:
 69:                 name = _d.get("name") or ""
 70:                 if not name:
 71:                     continue
 72:                 self.saved_djs.append(_d)
 73: 
 74:         self.saved_genres = lib.get("genres", [])
 75: 
 76:         # ── Events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 77:         evts = server_data.get("events", {})
 78:         if not evts:
 79:             for src in [self.EVENTS_FILE, "lineup_data.yaml"]:
 80:                 if os.path.exists(src):
 81:                     try:
 82:                         with open(src, "r") as f:
 83:                             evts = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
 84:                         break
 85:                     except Exception as e:
 86:                         print(f"Error loading {src}: {e}")
 87: 
 88:         raw_events = evts.get("events", []) if isinstance(evts, dict) else []
 89:         self.saved_events = sorted(
 90:             raw_events, key=lambda e: e.get("created_at", ""), reverse=True
 91:         )
 92: 
 93:     def get_dj_names(self):
 94:         return [d["name"] for d in self.saved_djs if d.get("name")]
 95: 
 96:     # ── Persistence ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 97: 
 98:     def save_data(self):
 99:         self._save_library()
100:         self._save_events()
101: 
102:     def _save_library(self):
103:         data = {
104:             "titles": self.saved_titles,
105:             "djs": self.saved_djs,
106:             "genres": self.saved_genres,
107:         }
108:         # Local file
109:         try:
110:             with open(self.LIBRARY_FILE, "w") as f:
111:                 yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True)
112:         except Exception as e:
113:             print(f"Error saving {self.LIBRARY_FILE}: {e}")
114:         # Push to server
115:         self._push_to_server("library", data)
116: 
117:     def _save_events(self):
118:         data = {"events": self.saved_events}
119:         # Local file
120:         try:
121:             with open(self.EVENTS_FILE, "w") as f:
122:                 yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True)
123:         except Exception as e:
124:             print(f"Error saving {self.EVENTS_FILE}: {e}")
125:         # Push to server
126:         self._push_to_server("events", data)
127: 
128:     def _push_to_server(self, key: str, value):
129:         """Push a data blob to the server in a background thread."""
130:         discord_id = self._discord_id()
131:         api = getattr(self, "api", None)
132:         if not discord_id or not api:
133:             return
134: 
135:         def _push():
136:             try:
137:                 api.put_user_data(discord_id, key, value)
138:             except Exception as exc:
139:                 log.warning("Cloud save (%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
140: 
141:         threading.Thread(target=_push, daemon=True).start()
142: 
143:     # ── Auto-save (crash recovery) ────────────────────────────────────────
144: 
145:     def _save_auto_state(self):
146:         """Persist the current working session to auto_save.json for crash recovery."""
147:         state = {
148:             "title": self.event_title_var.get(),
149:             "vol": self.event_vol_var.get(),
150:             "group_name": self.group_name_var.get(),
151:             "collab": bool(self.collab_with_var.get().strip()),
152:             "collab_with": self.collab_with_var.get(),
153:             "timestamp": self.event_timestamp.get(),
154:             "genres": list(self.active_genres),
155:             "names_only": self.names_only.get(),
156:             "social_links": dict(getattr(self, "social_links", {})),
157:             "slots": [
158:                 {
159:                     "name": s.name_var.get().strip(),
160:                     "genre": s.genre_var.get().strip(),
161:                     "duration": s.duration_var.get(),
162:                 }
163:                 for s in self.slots
164:             ],
165:         }
166:         try:
167:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "w") as f:
168:                 json.dump(state, f)
169:         except Exception as e:
170:             print(f"Auto-save error: {e}")
171: 
172:     def _check_auto_save(self):
173:         """On startup, detect an unclean exit and offer to restore the session."""
174:         if not os.path.exists(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE):
175:             return
176:         try:
177:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "r") as f:
178:                 state = json.load(f)
179:         except Exception:
180:             return
181:         # Clean-exit flag written by _on_close — no restore needed
182:         if state.get("clean_close"):
183:             return
184:         # Skip if essentially empty (no title and all slots are blank)
185:         title = state.get("title", "").strip()
186:         slots = state.get("slots", [])
187:         has_content = title or any(s.get("name") or s.get("genre") for s in slots)
188:         if not has_content:
189:             return
190:         # Show a DPG confirm dialog to let user decide whether to restore
191:         self._work_queue.put(lambda s=state: self._ask_restore_modal(s))
192: 
193:     def _ask_restore_modal(self, state):
194:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
195:         win_tag = "restore_session_win"
196:         if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
197:             return
198:         title = state.get("title", "").strip()
199:         label = f"An unsaved lineup was found{(' — ' + title) if title else ''}.\nRestore it?"
200:         vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
201:         vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
202:         with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Restore Unsaved Session", modal=True,
203:                         autosize=True, no_resize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
204:                         pos=[max(0, vp_w // 2 - 180), max(0, vp_h // 2 - 60)]):
205:             dpg.add_text(label, wrap=340)
206:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
207:                 restore_btn = dpg.add_button(
208:                     label="\u21BB Restore", width=140,
209:                     user_data=state,
210:                     callback=lambda s, a, u: (
211:                         self.load_event_lineup(u),
212:                         dpg.delete_item(win_tag) if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag) else None,
213:                     ))
214:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(restore_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
215:                 dpg.add_button(
216:                     label="\u00D7 Discard", width=140,
217:                     user_data=win_tag,
218:                     callback=lambda s, a, u: dpg.delete_item(u) if dpg.does_item_exist(u) else None)
219: 
220:     # ── Window state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
221: 
222:     def _restore_window_state(self):
223:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
224:         try:
225:             if os.path.exists(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE):
226:                 with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE, "r") as f:
227:                     state = json.load(f)
228:                 geo = state.get("geometry")
229:                 if geo:
230:                     # Parse "WxH+X+Y"
231:                     try:
232:                         size, pos = geo.split("+", 1)
233:                         w, h = map(int, size.split("x"))
234:                         x, y = map(int, pos.split("+"))
235:                         dpg.set_viewport_width(w)
236:                         dpg.set_viewport_height(h)
237:                         dpg.set_viewport_pos([x, y])
238:                     except Exception:
239:                         pass
240:         except Exception:
241:             pass
242: 
243:     def _on_close(self):
244:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
245:         try:
246:             w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
247:             h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
248:             pos = dpg.get_viewport_pos()
249:             x, y = pos[0], pos[1]
250:             geo = f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}"
251:             with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
252:                 json.dump({"geometry": geo, "maximized": False}, f)
253:         except Exception:
254:             pass
255:         # Mark clean exit so auto-save won't prompt on next launch
256:         try:
257:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "w") as f:
258:                 json.dump({"clean_close": True}, f)
259:         except Exception:
260:             pass
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/debounce.py">
  1: import queue
  2: import threading
  3: 
  4: 
  5: class DebounceMixin:
  6:     """Thread-safe debounce helpers using threading.Timer + a per-frame work queue."""
  7: 
  8:     def _init_debounce(self):
  9:         self._update_job       = None
 10:         self._roster_job       = None
 11:         self._save_lib_job     = None
 12:         self._auto_save_job    = None
 13:         self._auto_event_save_job = None
 14:         self._work_queue: queue.SimpleQueue = queue.SimpleQueue()
 15: 
 16:     def _queue_on_main(self, fn):
 17:         """Push *fn* onto the main-thread queue; executed in process_queue()."""
 18:         self._work_queue.put(fn)
 19: 
 20:     def process_queue(self):
 21:         """Call once per DPG frame from the main render loop."""
 22:         while not self._work_queue.empty():
 23:             try:
 24:                 self._work_queue.get_nowait()()
 25:             except Exception:
 26:                 pass
 27: 
 28:     # ── Timer helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 29: 
 30:     def _cancel(self, attr: str):
 31:         job = getattr(self, attr, None)
 32:         if job is not None:
 33:             job.cancel()
 34:             setattr(self, attr, None)
 35: 
 36:     def _timer(self, attr: str, delay_s: float, target):
 37:         self._cancel(attr)
 38:         t = threading.Timer(delay_s, lambda: self._queue_on_main(target))
 39:         t.daemon = True
 40:         t.start()
 41:         setattr(self, attr, t)
 42: 
 43:     # ── Public debounce gates ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 44: 
 45:     def _schedule_update(self, delay: int = 150):
 46:         self._timer("_update_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_update)
 47: 
 48:     def _run_scheduled_update(self):
 49:         self._update_job = None
 50:         self.update_output()
 51:         self._schedule_auto_save()
 52:         self._schedule_auto_event_save(delay=1500)
 53: 
 54:     def _schedule_roster_refresh(self, delay: int = 120):
 55:         self._timer("_roster_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_roster_refresh)
 56: 
 57:     def _run_scheduled_roster_refresh(self):
 58:         self._roster_job = None
 59:         self.refresh_dj_roster_ui()
 60: 
 61:     def _schedule_genre_refresh(self, delay: int = 120):
 62:         self._timer("_genre_refresh_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_genre_refresh)
 63: 
 64:     def _run_scheduled_genre_refresh(self):
 65:         self._genre_refresh_job = None
 66:         self.refresh_genre_tags()
 67: 
 68:     def _schedule_save_library(self, delay: int = 500):
 69:         self._timer("_save_lib_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_save_library)
 70: 
 71:     def _run_scheduled_save_library(self):
 72:         self._save_lib_job = None
 73:         self._save_library()
 74: 
 75:     def _schedule_auto_save(self, delay: int = 5000):
 76:         self._timer("_auto_save_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_auto_save)
 77: 
 78:     def _run_scheduled_auto_save(self):
 79:         self._auto_save_job = None
 80:         self._save_auto_state()
 81: 
 82:     def _schedule_auto_event_save(self, delay: int = 1500):
 83:         self._timer("_auto_event_save_job", delay / 1000,
 84:                     self._run_scheduled_auto_event_save)
 85: 
 86:     def _run_scheduled_auto_event_save(self):
 87:         self._auto_event_save_job = None
 88:         self._auto_event_save()
 89: 
 90:     def _refresh_slot_combos(self):
 91:         """Update all slot name-entry dropdowns."""
 92:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 93:         names = self.get_dj_names()
 94:         for slot in self.slots:
 95:             try:
 96:                 tag = f"slot_{slot.id}_name"
 97:                 if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 98:                     dpg.configure_item(tag, items=names)
 99:             except Exception:
100:                 pass
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/models/event_bus.py">
 1: """Lightweight publish-subscribe event bus for decoupled communication.
 2: 
 3: Usage
 4: -----
 5:     bus = EventBus()
 6:     bus.subscribe("model_changed", my_callback)
 7:     bus.publish("model_changed", sender=some_obj)
 8: 
 9: Subscribers receive ``(event_name, **kwargs)`` so they can inspect context
10: without being coupled to the publisher's type.
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: from collections import defaultdict
16: from typing import Any, Callable
17: 
18: 
19: class EventBus:
20:     """A simple, synchronous pub-sub hub.
21: 
22:     * **subscribe(event, callback)** — register a listener.
23:     * **unsubscribe(event, callback)** — remove a listener.
24:     * **publish(event, **kwargs)** — notify all listeners for *event*.
25:     """
26: 
27:     def __init__(self) -> None:
28:         self._subscribers: dict[str, list[Callable[..., Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
29: 
30:     # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
31: 
32:     def subscribe(self, event: str, callback: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
33:         """Register *callback* for *event*.  Duplicates are silently ignored."""
34:         if callback not in self._subscribers[event]:
35:             self._subscribers[event].append(callback)
36: 
37:     def unsubscribe(self, event: str, callback: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
38:         """Remove *callback* from *event*.  No-op if not registered."""
39:         try:
40:             self._subscribers[event].remove(callback)
41:         except ValueError:
42:             pass
43: 
44:     def publish(self, event: str, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
45:         """Call every subscriber of *event* with the supplied keyword args."""
46:         for cb in self._subscribers.get(event, []):
47:             cb(event, **kwargs)
48: 
49:     def clear(self) -> None:
50:         """Remove **all** subscriptions (useful for tests / teardown)."""
51:         self._subscribers.clear()
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/models/lineup_model.py">
  1: """Pure-Python data model for a lineup event.
  2: 
  3: This module defines plain dataclasses that capture the *complete* state of a
  4: lineup — titles, timestamps, slots, open-decks config, and DJ metadata — with
  5: **zero** dependency on Tkinter or any GUI framework.
  6: 
  7: The model is the single source of truth.  UI widgets read from / write to
  8: instance attributes, and the ``EventBus`` broadcasts changes so other
  9: subsystems (output generation, auto-save, etc.) can react without coupling.
 10: 
 11: Typical flow
 12: ------------
 13: 1. User types in a ``CTkEntry`` → UI handler calls ``model.set_title("…")``.
 14: 2. The setter publishes ``"model_changed"`` on the bus.
 15: 3. ``OutputGenerator`` (subscribed) rebuilds the preview text.
 16: 4. The output panel (subscribed) writes the new text to the textbox.
 17: """
 18: 
 19: from __future__ import annotations
 20: 
 21: import datetime
 22: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 23: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
 24: 
 25: from .types import DJInfo, EventSnapshot, SlotData
 26: 
 27: if TYPE_CHECKING:
 28:     from .event_bus import EventBus
 29: 
 30: 
 31: # ── Re-export for backward compatibility ──────────────────────────────────
 32: __all__ = ["SlotData", "DJInfo", "EventSnapshot", "LineupModel"]
 33: 
 34: 
 35: # ── Live mutable model ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 36: 
 37: class LineupModel:
 38:     """The runtime model that backs the editor UI.
 39: 
 40:     Every mutating setter publishes ``"model_changed"`` on the bus so
 41:     subscribers (output panel, auto-save timer, etc.) can react.
 42: 
 43:     The model is deliberately *not* a dataclass itself because it manages
 44:     change-notification logic and provides ``snapshot()`` for consumers
 45:     that need a frozen view.
 46:     """
 47: 
 48:     def __init__(self, bus: EventBus) -> None:
 49:         self._bus = bus
 50: 
 51:         self.title: str = ""
 52:         self.vol: str = ""
 53:         self.timestamp: str = ""
 54:         self.genres: list[str] = []
 55:         self.slots: list[SlotData] = []
 56:         self.names_only: bool = False
 57:         self.output_format: str = "discord"
 58:         self.saved_djs: list[DJInfo] = []
 59: 
 60:     # ── Snapshot ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 61: 
 62:     def snapshot(self) -> EventSnapshot:
 63:         """Return a frozen copy suitable for ``OutputGenerator``."""
 64:         return EventSnapshot(
 65:             title=self.title,
 66:             vol=self.vol,
 67:             timestamp=self.timestamp,
 68:             genres=list(self.genres),
 69:             slots=[SlotData(s.name, s.genre, s.duration) for s in self.slots],
 70:             names_only=self.names_only,
 71:             output_format=self.output_format,
 72:             saved_djs=[DJInfo(d.name, d.stream, d.exact_link) for d in self.saved_djs],
 73:         )
 74: 
 75:     # ── Bulk load (e.g. loading a saved event) ────────────────────────
 76: 
 77:     def load_from_dict(self, data: dict) -> None:
 78:         """Populate the model from a plain dict (event save format)."""
 79:         self.title = data.get("title", "")
 80:         self.vol = data.get("vol", "")
 81:         self.timestamp = data.get("timestamp", "")
 82:         self.genres = list(data.get("genres", []))
 83:         self.names_only = bool(data.get("names_only", False))
 84:         self.slots = [
 85:             SlotData(
 86:                 name=s.get("name", ""),
 87:                 genre=s.get("genre", ""),
 88:                 duration=int(s.get("duration", 60)),
 89:             )
 90:             for s in data.get("slots", [])
 91:         ]
 92:         self._notify()
 93: 
 94:     def to_dict(self) -> dict:
 95:         """Serialize to the same plain-dict format used for persistence."""
 96:         return {
 97:             "title": self.title,
 98:             "vol": self.vol,
 99:             "timestamp": self.timestamp,
100:             "genres": list(self.genres),
101:             "names_only": self.names_only,
102:             "slots": [
103:                 {"name": s.name, "genre": s.genre, "duration": str(s.duration)}
104:                 for s in self.slots
105:             ],
106:         }
107: 
108:     # ── Change notification ───────────────────────────────────────────
109: 
110:     def notify(self) -> None:
111:         """Publicly trigger a model-changed broadcast.
112: 
113:         Useful when the UI has mutated a ``SlotData`` in-place and needs
114:         to tell subscribers about it.
115:         """
116:         self._notify()
117: 
118:     def _notify(self) -> None:
119:         self._bus.publish("model_changed")
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/models/types.py">
 1: """Pure-Python value objects for lineup events.
 2: 
 3: These dataclasses capture the complete state of a lineup — slots, DJ metadata,
 4: and a frozen snapshot for output generation — with **zero** dependency on any
 5: GUI framework.
 6: """
 7: 
 8: from __future__ import annotations
 9: 
10: import datetime
11: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
12: 
13: 
14: @dataclass
15: class SlotData:
16:     """One performer slot — pure data, no widgets."""
17:     name: str = ""
18:     genre: str = ""
19:     duration: int = 60
20: 
21: 
22: @dataclass
23: class DJInfo:
24:     """Saved DJ metadata from the library."""
25:     name: str = ""
26:     stream: str = ""
27:     exact_link: bool = False
28: 
29: 
30: @dataclass
31: class EventSnapshot:
32:     """An immutable snapshot of the full event state.
33: 
34:     ``OutputGenerator`` works exclusively with this — it never touches
35:     GUI widgets or the live model.
36:     """
37:     title: str = ""
38:     vol: str = ""
39:     group_name: str = ""
40:     collab: bool = False
41:     collab_with: str = ""
42:     timestamp: str = ""            # "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM"
43:     genres: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
44:     slots: list[SlotData] = field(default_factory=list)
45:     names_only: bool = False
46:     output_format: str = "discord"  # "discord" | "local"
47:     stream_link_format: str = ""    # "" | "quest" | "pc"
48:     saved_djs: list[DJInfo] = field(default_factory=list)
49:     social_links: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
50:     role_mentions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
51: 
52:     @property
53:     def start_datetime(self) -> datetime.datetime:
54:         """Parse ``self.timestamp`` into a ``datetime``, falling back to *now*."""
55:         try:
56:             return datetime.datetime.strptime(self.timestamp, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
57:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
58:             return datetime.datetime.now()
59: 
60:     @property
61:     def full_title(self) -> str:
62:         base = f"{self.title} VOL.{self.vol}" if self.vol.isdigit() else self.title
63:         if self.collab and self.collab_with:
64:             base += f" x {self.collab_with}"
65:         return base

<file path="desktop/backend/output/output_builder.py">
  1: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
  2: 
  3: from ..models.types import DJInfo, EventSnapshot, SlotData
  4: from .output_generator import OutputGenerator
  5: 
  6: 
  7: class OutputMixin:
  8:     """Bridges the Tkinter UI with the pure-Python OutputGenerator.
  9: 
 10:     Reads widget state → builds an ``EventSnapshot`` → delegates text
 11:     generation to ``OutputGenerator`` → writes the result back to the
 12:     output textbox.  Copy helpers and format-button state remain here
 13:     because they are inherently UI concerns.
 14:     """
 15: 
 16:     # ── VRCDN link conversion (thin wrapper kept for backward compat) ─────
 17: 
 18:     @staticmethod
 19:     def _vrcdn_convert(link: str, fmt: str) -> str:
 20:         """Delegate to ``OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert``."""
 21:         return OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(link, fmt)
 22: 
 23:     # ── Snapshot builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 24: 
 25:     def _build_snapshot(self) -> EventSnapshot:
 26:         """Harvest the current UI state into an ``EventSnapshot``."""
 27:         slots = []
 28:         for s in self.slots:
 29:             try:
 30:                 dur = int(s.duration_var.get())
 31:             except (ValueError, AttributeError):
 32:                 dur = 0
 33:             slots.append(SlotData(
 34:                 name=s.name_var.get().strip(),
 35:                 genre=s.genre_var.get().strip(),
 36:                 duration=dur,
 37:             ))
 38: 
 39:         dj_list = [
 40:             DJInfo(
 41:                 name=d.get("name", ""),
 42:                 stream=d.get("stream", ""),
 43:                 exact_link=bool(d.get("exact_link")),
 44:             )
 45:             for d in self.saved_djs
 46:         ]
 47: 
 48:         # Read social links directly from widgets for real-time values
 49:         merged_social = {}
 50:         for label, _ in getattr(self, "_SOCIAL_FIELDS", []):
 51:             tag = f"social_input_{label.replace(' ', '_')}"
 52:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 53:                 val = (dpg.get_value(tag) or "").strip()
 54:                 if val:
 55:                     merged_social[label] = val
 56:             elif label in getattr(self, "social_links", {}):
 57:                 val = self.social_links[label].strip()
 58:                 if val:
 59:                     merged_social[label] = val
 60:         for key, val in getattr(self, "persistent_links", {}).items():
 61:             if isinstance(val, dict) and val.get("enabled") and val.get("link", "").strip():
 62:                 merged_social[key] = val["link"].strip()
 63:         # Also read club link widgets directly for real-time values
 64:         for label, _ in getattr(self, "_CLUB_LINK_FIELDS", []):
 65:             tag = f"group_link_{label.replace(' ', '_')}"
 66:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 67:                 val = (dpg.get_value(tag) or "").strip()
 68:                 if val:
 69:                     merged_social[label] = val
 70: 
 71:         collab_with = self.collab_with_var.get().strip()
 72: 
 73:         return EventSnapshot(
 74:             title=self.event_title_var.get().strip(),
 75:             vol=self.event_vol_var.get().strip(),
 76:             group_name=self.group_name_var.get().strip(),
 77:             collab=bool(collab_with),
 78:             collab_with=collab_with,
 79:             timestamp=self.event_timestamp.get(),
 80:             genres=list(self.active_genres),
 81:             slots=slots,
 82:             names_only=self.names_only.get(),
 83:             output_format=self.output_format.get(),
 84:             stream_link_format=self.stream_link_format.get(),
 85:             saved_djs=dj_list,
 86:             social_links=merged_social,
 87:             role_mentions=list(getattr(self, "discord_role_mentions", [])),
 88:         )
 89: 
 90:     # ── Main output builder ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 91: 
 92:     def update_output(self):
 93:         snap = self._build_snapshot()
 94: 
 95:         # Always refresh slot start-time labels regardless of output format
 96:         slot_times = OutputGenerator.compute_slot_times(snap)
 97:         for _slot, time_str in zip(self.slots, slot_times):
 98:             tag = f"slot_time_{_slot._id}"
 99:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
100:                 dpg.set_value(tag, time_str)
101: 
102:         # Sync button themes for formats, stream links, and times toggle
103:         if hasattr(self, "output_format") and hasattr(self, "names_only"):
104:             fmt = self.output_format.get()
105:             slf = self.stream_link_format.get()
106:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_discord"):
107:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_discord", "success_btn_theme" if fmt == "discord" else "secondary_btn_theme")
108:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_plain"):
109:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_plain", "success_btn_theme" if fmt == "local" else "secondary_btn_theme")
110:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_quest"):
111:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_quest", "success_btn_theme" if slf == "quest" else "secondary_btn_theme")
112:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_pc"):
113:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_pc", "success_btn_theme" if slf == "pc" else "secondary_btn_theme")
114:                 
115:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_times"):
116:                 times_on = not self.names_only.get()
117:                 dpg.configure_item("fmt_times", label="Times on" if times_on else "Times off")
118:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_times", "success_btn_theme" if times_on else "secondary_btn_theme")
119: 
120:         # Delegate the heavy lifting to the pure-Python generator
121:         import pathlib as _pl
122:         _dbg_lines = []
123:         for _lbl, _ in getattr(self, "_SOCIAL_FIELDS", []):
124:             _tag = f"social_input_{_lbl.replace(' ', '_')}"
125:             _exists = dpg.does_item_exist(_tag)
126:             _val = dpg.get_value(_tag) if _exists else "N/A"
127:             _dbg_lines.append(f"  {_lbl}: exists={_exists}, val={_val!r}")
128:         import traceback as _tb
129:         _stack = "".join(_tb.format_stack()[-5:-1])
130:         _pl.Path("debug_social.txt").write_text(
131:             f"=== update_output call ===\n"
132:             f"caller stack:\n{_stack}\n"
133:             f"_SOCIAL_FIELDS widget state:\n" + "\n".join(_dbg_lines) + "\n"
134:             f"social_links dict: {getattr(self, 'social_links', 'MISSING')}\n"
135:             f"merged_social in snapshot: {snap.social_links!r}\n"
136:             f"output_format: {snap.output_format}\n"
137:         )
138:         body = OutputGenerator.generate(snap)
139: 
140:         if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text"):
141:             # Only auto-generate if 'Auto-update' is checked (prevents overwriting manual edits)
142:             if dpg.does_item_exist("auto_update_output"):
143:                 if dpg.get_value("auto_update_output"):
144:                     dpg.set_value("output_text", body)
145: 
146:     # ── Copy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
147: 
148:     def copy_template(self):
149:         import threading
150:         text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
151:         dpg.set_clipboard_text(text)
152: 
153:     def copy_quest_links(self):
154:         self.stream_link_format.set("quest")
155:         self.update_output()
156:         self._copy_output_to_clipboard()
157: 
158:     def copy_pc_links(self):
159:         self.stream_link_format.set("pc")
160:         self.update_output()
161:         self._copy_output_to_clipboard()
162: 
163:     def _copy_output_to_clipboard(self):
164:         text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
165:         dpg.set_clipboard_text(text)
166: 
167:     # ── Format-button state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
168: 
169:     def toggle_format(self):
170:         self.output_format.set("discord")
171:         self.update_output()
172: 
173:     def set_plain_text(self):
174:         self.output_format.set("local")
175:         self.update_output()
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/output/output_generator.py">
  1: """Standalone text generator — **no Tkinter dependency**.
  2: 
  3: This module takes an ``EventSnapshot`` (a frozen, pure-Python data object)
  4: and returns fully-formatted output strings for every supported format:
  5: 
  6: * **Discord** — ``#`` headers, ``<t:UNIX:t>`` timestamps, ``**bold**`` names.
  7: * **Plain text (local)** — ``HH:MM`` times with local timezone abbreviation.
  8: * **Quest / PC** — VRCDN stream links only.
  9: 
 10: Because the generator is a pure function of data, it is trivially unit-testable
 11: without ever instantiating a Tkinter root window.
 12: 
 13: Example
 14: -------
 15:     from src.backend.types import EventSnapshot, SlotData
 16:     from src.backend.output_generator import OutputGenerator
 17: 
 18:     snap = EventSnapshot(
 19:         title="Friday Night Beats", vol="3",
 20:         timestamp="2025-06-01 20:00",
 21:         genres=["House", "Techno"],
 22:         slots=[SlotData("DJ Alpha", "House", 60)],
 23:         output_format="discord",
 24:     )
 25:     print(OutputGenerator.generate(snap))
 26: """
 27: 
 28: from __future__ import annotations
 29: 
 30: import datetime
 31: import re
 32: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
 33: 
 34: if TYPE_CHECKING:
 35:     from ..models.types import EventSnapshot
 36: 
 37: 
 38: class OutputGenerator:
 39:     """Pure-function text builder.  Every public method is ``@staticmethod``."""
 40: 
 41:     # ── Public entry point ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 42: 
 43:     @staticmethod
 44:     def generate(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
 45:         """Return the formatted output string for *snap*'s ``output_format``."""
 46:         fmt = snap.output_format
 47:         if fmt == "local":
 48:             return OutputGenerator._generate_plain(snap)
 49:         return OutputGenerator._generate_discord(snap)
 50: 
 51:     # ── Slot start-time labels (HH:MM) ────────────────────────────────────
 52: 
 53:     @staticmethod
 54:     def compute_slot_times(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> list[str]:
 55:         """Return a list of ``HH:MM`` strings — one per slot — for UI labels.
 56: 
 57:         This lets the UI update its time labels without parsing the output text.
 58:         """
 59:         start = snap.start_datetime
 60:         result: list[str] = []
 61:         ptr = start
 62:         for slot in snap.slots:
 63:             result.append(ptr.strftime("%H:%M"))
 64:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
 65:         return result
 66: 
 67:     # ── VRCDN link conversion ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 68: 
 69:     @staticmethod
 70:     def vrcdn_convert(link: str, fmt: str) -> str:
 71:         """Return a VRCDN link reformatted for *fmt* (``'quest'`` or ``'pc'``).
 72: 
 73:         Non-VRCDN links are returned unchanged.
 74:         """
 75:         if not link:
 76:             return link
 77:         m = re.match(
 78:             r"(?:https://stream\.vrcdn\.live/live/|rtspt://stream\.vrcdn\.live/live/)"
 79:             r"(.+?)(?:\.live\.ts)?$",
 80:             link,
 81:         )
 82:         if not m:
 83:             return link
 84:         key = m.group(1)
 85:         if fmt == "quest":
 86:             return f"https://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}.live.ts"
 87:         return f"rtspt://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}"
 88: 
 89:     # ── Private builders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 90: 
 91:     @staticmethod
 92:     def _generate_discord(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
 93:         lines: list[str] = []
 94: 
 95:         # Role mentions
 96:         if snap.role_mentions:
 97:             mentions = " ".join(f"<@&{rid}>" for rid in snap.role_mentions)
 98:             lines.append(mentions)
 99:             lines.append("")
100: 
101:         # Title
102:         if snap.title:
103:             lines.append(f"# {snap.full_title}")
104: 
105:         # Club name
106:         if snap.group_name:
107:             lines.append(f"## {snap.group_name}")
108: 
109:         # Timestamp
110:         start = snap.start_datetime
111:         unix = int(start.timestamp())
112:         lines.append(f"# <t:{unix}:F> (<t:{unix}:R>)")
113: 
114:         # Genres
115:         if snap.genres:
116:             lines.append(f"## {' // '.join(snap.genres)}")
117: 
118:         # Lineup header
119:         lines.append("### LINEUP")
120: 
121:         # Build DJ lookup for stream links
122:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs} if snap.stream_link_format else {}
123:         slf = snap.stream_link_format
124: 
125:         ptr = start
126:         for idx, slot in enumerate(snap.slots, start=1):
127:             name = slot.name or str(idx)
128:             if snap.names_only:
129:                 lines.append(name)
130:             else:
131:                 genre_str = f" ({slot.genre})" if slot.genre else ""
132:                 ts = int(ptr.timestamp())
133:                 lines.append(f"<t:{ts}:t> | **{name}**{genre_str}")
134: 
135:             # Interleave stream link after each slot
136:             if slf and slot.name.strip():
137:                 dj = dj_lookup.get(slot.name.strip())
138:                 if dj and dj.stream:
139:                     link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, slf)
140:                     lines.append(f"```\n{link}\n```")
141: 
142:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
143: 
144:         # Social links (bottom of lineup)
145:         _LINK_ORDER = ["TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"]
146:         if snap.social_links:
147:             parts = [
148:                 f"[{label}]({snap.social_links[label]})"
149:                 for label in _LINK_ORDER
150:                 if snap.social_links.get(label, "").strip()
151:             ]
152:             if parts:
153:                 lines.append("")
154:                 lines.append(" | ".join(parts))
155: 
156:         return "\n".join(lines)
157: 
158:     @staticmethod
159:     def _generate_plain(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
160:         lines: list[str] = []
161: 
162:         # Title
163:         if snap.title:
164:             lines.append(snap.full_title)
165: 
166:         # Club name
167:         if snap.group_name:
168:             lines.append(snap.group_name)
169: 
170:         # Timestamp + timezone
171:         start = snap.start_datetime
172:         local_tz = datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().tzname()
173:         if sum(1 for c in local_tz if c.isupper()) >= 3:
174:             local_tz = "".join(c for c in local_tz if c.isupper())
175:         lines.append(f"{start.strftime('%Y-%m-%d @ %H:%M')} ({local_tz})")
176: 
177:         # Genres
178:         if snap.genres:
179:             lines.append(" // ".join(snap.genres))
180: 
181:         # Lineup header
182:         lines.append("LINEUP")
183: 
184:         # Build DJ lookup for stream links
185:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs} if snap.stream_link_format else {}
186:         slf = snap.stream_link_format
187: 
188:         ptr = start
189:         for idx, slot in enumerate(snap.slots, start=1):
190:             name = slot.name or str(idx)
191:             if snap.names_only:
192:                 lines.append(name)
193:             else:
194:                 genre_str = f" ({slot.genre})" if slot.genre else ""
195:                 lines.append(f"{ptr.strftime('%H:%M')} | {name}{genre_str}")
196: 
197:             # Interleave stream link after each slot
198:             if slf and slot.name.strip():
199:                 dj = dj_lookup.get(slot.name.strip())
200:                 if dj and dj.stream:
201:                     link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, slf)
202:                     lines.append(link)
203: 
204:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
205: 
206:         # Social links (bottom of lineup)
207:         _LINK_ORDER = ["TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"]
208:         if snap.social_links:
209:             parts = [
210:                 f"{label}: {snap.social_links[label]}"
211:                 for label in _LINK_ORDER
212:                 if snap.social_links.get(label, "").strip()
213:             ]
214:             if parts:
215:                 lines.append("")
216:                 lines.append(" | ".join(parts))
217: 
218:         return "\n".join(lines)
219: 
220:     @staticmethod
221:     def _generate_stream_links(snap: "EventSnapshot", fmt: str) -> str:
222:         """Build Quest / PC stream-link-only output."""
223:         links: list[str] = []
224:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs}
225: 
226:         for slot in snap.slots:
227:             name = slot.name.strip()
228:             if not name:
229:                 continue
230:             dj = dj_lookup.get(name)
231:             if dj and dj.stream:
232:                 link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, fmt)
233:                 links.append(link)
234: 
235:         if not links:
236:             return ""
237:         return "\n".join(f"```\n{link}\n```" for link in links)
</file>

<file path="desktop/backend/services/__init__.py">

<file path="desktop/backend/services/discord_service.py">
  1: """
  2: Pure-Python Discord bot service — no GUI imports.
  3: Runs a discord.py client on a background thread and exposes helpers
  4: to send messages to channels by ID.
  5: """
  6: 
  7: import asyncio
  8: import threading
  9: from typing import Callable
 10: 
 11: import discord
 12: 
 13: 
 14: class DiscordService:
 15:     """Manages a Discord bot connection on a daemon thread."""
 16: 
 17:     def __init__(self):
 18:         self._token: str = ""
 19:         self._client: discord.Client | None = None
 20:         self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
 21:         self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
 22:         self._ready = threading.Event()
 23:         self._on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None  # status callback
 24: 
 25:     # ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 26: 
 27:     @property
 28:     def is_running(self) -> bool:
 29:         return (
 30:             self._client is not None
 31:             and self._loop is not None
 32:             and self._ready.is_set()
 33:         )
 34: 
 35:     def start(self, token: str, *, on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None):
 36:         """Start the bot in a background thread. No-op if already running."""
 37:         if self.is_running:
 38:             return
 39:         if not token.strip():
 40:             if on_status:
 41:                 on_status("No bot token provided.")
 42:             return
 43: 
 44:         self._token = token.strip()
 45:         self._on_status = on_status
 46:         self._ready.clear()
 47: 
 48:         self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
 49:         self._thread.start()
 50: 
 51:     def stop(self):
 52:         """Gracefully shut down the bot."""
 53:         if self._client and self._loop and not self._loop.is_closed():
 54:             asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._client.close(), self._loop)
 55:         self._ready.clear()
 56:         self._client = None
 57:         self._loop = None
 58:         self._thread = None
 59:         if self._on_status:
 60:             self._on_status("Disconnected")
 61: 
 62:     # ── Fetching metadata ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 63: 
 64:     def get_guilds(
 65:         self,
 66:         *,
 67:         on_result: Callable[[list[dict]], None] | None = None,
 68:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
 69:     ):
 70:         """Fetch all guilds (servers) the bot is in."""
 71:         if not self.is_running:
 72:             if on_error:
 73:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
 74:             return
 75: 
 76:         async def _fetch():
 77:             try:
 78:                 results = []
 79:                 for g in self._client.guilds:
 80:                     results.append({"id": str(g.id), "name": g.name})
 81:                 results.sort(key=lambda g: g["name"].lower())
 82:                 if on_result:
 83:                     on_result(results)
 84:             except Exception as exc:
 85:                 if on_error:
 86:                     on_error(str(exc))
 87: 
 88:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
 89: 
 90:     def get_guild_details(
 91:         self,
 92:         guild_id: str,
 93:         *,
 94:         on_result: Callable[[dict], None] | None = None,
 95:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
 96:     ):
 97:         """Fetch channels and roles for a specific guild."""
 98:         if not self.is_running:
 99:             if on_error:
100:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
101:             return
102: 
103:         async def _fetch():
104:             try:
105:                 g = self._client.get_guild(int(guild_id))
106:                 if not g:
107:                     g = await self._client.fetch_guild(int(guild_id))
108:                 
109:                 channels = []
110:                 for ch in g.text_channels:
111:                     channels.append({"id": str(ch.id), "name": ch.name})
112:                 channels.sort(key=lambda c: c["name"].lower())
113: 
114:                 roles = []
115:                 for r in g.roles:
116:                     if r.is_default():  # skip @everyone
117:                         continue
118:                     # Convert color to hex
119:                     hex_color = f"#{r.color.value:06x}" if r.color.value else ""
120:                     roles.append({"id": str(r.id), "name": r.name, "color": hex_color})
121:                 roles.sort(key=lambda r: r["name"].lower())
122: 
123:                 if on_result:
124:                     on_result({"channels": channels, "roles": roles})
125:             except Exception as exc:
126:                 if on_error:
127:                     on_error(str(exc))
128: 
129:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
130: 
131:     # ── Send helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
132: 
133:     def get_text_channels(
134:         self,
135:         *,
136:         on_result: Callable[[list[tuple[str, str, int]]], None] | None = None,
137:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
138:     ):
139:         """Fetch all visible text channels from the bot's guilds.
140: 
141:         Calls *on_result* with a list of ``(guild_name, channel_name, channel_id)``
142:         tuples, sorted by guild then channel name.
143:         """
144:         if not self.is_running:
145:             if on_error:
146:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
147:             return
148: 
149:         async def _fetch():
150:             try:
151:                 results: list[tuple[str, str, int]] = []
152:                 for guild in self._client.guilds:
153:                     for ch in guild.text_channels:
154:                         results.append((guild.name, ch.name, ch.id))
155:                 results.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0].lower(), t[1].lower()))
156:                 if on_result:
157:                     on_result(results)
158:             except Exception as exc:
159:                 if on_error:
160:                     on_error(str(exc))
161: 
162:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
163: 
164:     def send_message(
165:         self,
166:         channel_id: int,
167:         content: str,
168:         *,
169:         on_success: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
170:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
171:     ):
172:         """Queue a message send.  Callbacks fire on the bot's event loop."""
173:         if not self.is_running:
174:             if on_error:
175:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
176:             return
177: 
178:         async def _send():
179:             try:
180:                 channel = self._client.get_channel(channel_id)
181:                 if channel is None:
182:                     channel = await self._client.fetch_channel(channel_id)
183:                 # Split into 2000-char chunks for Discord's limit
184:                 for i in range(0, len(content), 2000):
185:                     await channel.send(content[i : i + 2000])
186:                 if on_success:
187:                     on_success()
188:             except Exception as exc:
189:                 if on_error:
190:                     on_error(str(exc))
191: 
192:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_send(), self._loop)
193: 
194:     def send_embed(
195:         self,
196:         channel_id: int,
197:         embed: discord.Embed | None = None,
198:         *,
199:         content: str | None = None,
200:         file: discord.File | None = None,
201:         on_success: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
202:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
203:     ):
204:         """Queue an embed message send, optionally with a file attachment."""
205:         if not self.is_running:
206:             if on_error:
207:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
208:             return
209: 
210:         async def _send():
211:             try:
212:                 channel = self._client.get_channel(channel_id)
213:                 if channel is None:
214:                     channel = await self._client.fetch_channel(channel_id)
215:                 kwargs: dict = {}
216:                 if embed:
217:                     kwargs["embed"] = embed
218:                 if content:
219:                     kwargs["content"] = content
220:                 if file is not None:
221:                     kwargs["file"] = file
222:                 await channel.send(**kwargs)
223:                 if on_success:
224:                     on_success()
225:             except Exception as exc:
226:                 if on_error:
227:                     on_error(str(exc))
228: 
229:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_send(), self._loop)
230: 
231:     # ── Internal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
232: 
233:     def _run(self):
234:         """Entry point for the background thread."""
235:         intents = discord.Intents.default()
236:         intents.message_content = True
237:         self._client = discord.Client(intents=intents)
238: 
239:         @self._client.event
240:         async def on_ready():
241:             self._ready.set()
242:             if self._on_status:
243:                 self._on_status(f"Connected as {self._client.user}")
244: 
245:         self._loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
246:         asyncio.set_event_loop(self._loop)
247:         try:
248:             self._loop.run_until_complete(self._client.start(self._token))
249:         except Exception as exc:
250:             if self._on_status:
251:                 self._on_status(f"Bot error: {exc}")
252:         finally:
253:             self._ready.clear()
</file>

 6: def main():
 7:     # Set Windows AppUserModelID so the taskbar shows our icon, not Python's
 8:     if sys.platform == "win32":
 9:         import ctypes
10:         ctypes.windll.shell32.SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID(
11:             "baebu.lineupbuilder"
12:         )
13:         
14:     import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
15:     from src.frontend.ui.widgets import add_context_menu
16: 
17:     # Monkeypatch dpg.add_input_text to automatically append our copy/paste right-click menu
18:     _original_add_input_text = dpg.add_input_text
19:     def _patched_add_input_text(*args, **kwargs):
20:         item = _original_add_input_text(*args, **kwargs)
21:         if item:
22:             add_context_menu(item)
23:         return item
24:     dpg.add_input_text = _patched_add_input_text
25: 
26:     app = App()
27:     app.run()

<file path="desktop/README.md">
  1: # Lineup Builder — Desktop App
  2: 
  3: Desktop GUI application for DJs and event organizers. Streamlines scheduling sets, managing performer rosters, and generating formatted output for Discord, plain text, and VRCDN-based VR platforms (Quest/PC).
  4: 
  5: Built on **DearPyGui** with a mixin-composition architecture — the entire backend is pure Python and fully unit-testable with no GUI dependency.
  6: 
  7: ---
  8: 
  9: ## Screenshots
 10: 
 11: ![Screenshot 1](../assets/Screenshots/Screenshot%202026-03-07%20025936.png)
 12: ![Screenshot 2](../assets/Screenshots/Screenshot%202026-03-07%20025953.png)
 13: ![Screenshot 3](../assets/Screenshots/Screenshot%202026-03-07%20030001.png)
 14: ![Screenshot 4](../assets/Screenshots/Screenshot%202026-03-07%20030012.png)
 15: 
 16: ---
 17: 
 18: ## Features
 19: 
 20: ### Event Orchestration
 21: 
 22: - **Smart Header** — Set event titles and volume numbers. Volume auto-increments when you save a new event.
 23: - **Integrated Date/Time Picker** — Custom-styled calendar and time selector (modal pop-up). Use **Arrow Up / Arrow Down** on the timestamp field to shift time ±15 minutes; hold **Shift** for ±24 hours.
 24: - **Automated Import** — Paste an existing lineup from Discord or plain text. The parser extracts titles, timestamps, genres, and DJ slots automatically.
 25: - **Genre Library** — Persistent global genre list. Toggle genres on/off per event or add new tags on the fly.
 26: 
 27: ### DJ Roster & Lineup Management
 28: 
 29: - **Persistent Roster** — Store frequent performers alongside their stream links (VRCDN, Twitch, YouTube, SoundCloud, Kick, or any custom URL).
 30: - **Exact Link Mode** — Per-DJ toggle to skip VRCDN conversion and pass the stream URL through as-is.
 31: - **Drag-and-Drop** — Drag DJs directly from your roster into a lineup slot.
 32: - **Slot Reordering** — Reorder slots using drag handles. Start times recalculate in real-time as durations change.
 33: - **Open Decks** — Configurable open-deck slots with adjustable counts and durations.
 34: - **Events History** — Save, restore, duplicate, and delete past event lineups from a persistent YAML-backed library.
 35: 
 36: ### Discord Bot Integration
 37: 
 38: - **Built-in Discord Bot** — Connect a Discord bot directly from the app.
 39: - **Rich Embed Posting** — Lineups posted as styled Discord embeds with event title, timestamps, genre tags, full lineup, and social links. Embed color matches your active theme accent.
 40: - **Embed Image** — Attach a poster image by URL or local file.
 41: - **Channel Picker** — Fetches your server's text channels for posting.
 42: - **Scheduled Posting** — Schedule posts for future delivery. Posts persist across app restarts.
 43: 
 44: ### Social Links
 45: 
 46: - **Configurable Social Links** — Timeline, VRCPop, X (Twitter), Instagram, Discord, and VRC Group links appear at the bottom of lineup output and Discord embeds.
 47: - **Persistent Links** — Mark Discord and VRC Group links as persistent so they carry across all events.
 48: 
 49: ### Multi-Format Output
 50: 
 51: Real-time previews for four output formats:
 52: 
 53: | Format | Style | Description |
 54: |--------|-------|-------------|
 55: | **Discord** | Markdown | `#` headers, `<t:UNIX:t>` timestamps, `**bold**` DJ names |
 56: | **Local** | Plain Text | `HH:MM` times with local timezone abbreviation |
 57: | **Quest** | HLS Links | `https://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}.live.ts` in a code block |
 58: | **PC** | RTSP Links | `rtspt://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}` in a code block |
 59: 
 60: ### Personalization & Reliability
 61: 
 62: - **Theme Engine** — 8 built-in dark presets (Slate, Midnight Blue, OLED Black, Crimson, Amber, Forest, Ocean, Violet) plus unlimited user-saved custom presets.
 63: - **Crash Recovery** — Auto-save every 5 seconds. On next launch, the app offers to restore the previous session.
 64: - **Window Persistence** — Remembers size, position, and panel widths across sessions.
 65: - **Windows Title Bar Coloring** — Dynamically colors the native title bar to match the active theme (Windows 10/11).
 66: - **Resizable Panels** — Left/right panel split is user-adjustable with a drag handle.
 67: 
 68: ---
 69: 
 70: ## Input Shortcuts
 71: 
 72: | Input | Action |
 73: |-------|--------|
 74: | **↑ / ↓** on timestamp field | Shift event time ±15 minutes |
 75: | **Shift + ↑ / ↓** on timestamp field | Shift event time ±24 hours |
 76: | **Mouse wheel** on combo / spinners | Cycle values up or down |
 77: 
 78: ---
 79: 
 80: ## Setup
 81: 
 82: ### Prerequisites
 83: 
 84: - **Python 3.11+**
 85: - **Windows 10/11** (required for native title bar coloring; runs on other platforms without it)
 86: 
 87: ### Install
 88: 
 89: ```bash
 90: cd desktop
 91: python -m venv .venv
 92: .venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1
 93: pip install -r requirements.txt
 94: ```
 95: 
 96: ### Run
 97: 
 98: ```bash
 99: # From the project root
100: python main.py          # production
101: python dev.py           # auto-restart on code changes (watchdog)
102: ```
103: 
104: On Windows you can also use `dev.bat` which activates the venv and launches `dev.py`.
105: 
106: ### Test
107: 
108: ```bash
109: pytest tests/ -v
110: ```
111: 
112: The backend is fully testable without a GUI — no DearPyGui imports in `src/backend/`.
113: 
114: ### Build Executable
115: 
116: ```bash
117: pip install pyinstaller
118: python -m PyInstaller lineup_builder.spec --clean
119: ```
120: 
121: Output: `dist/LineupBuilder.exe`
122: 
123: ---
124: 
125: ## Architecture
126: 
127: Mixin-composition pattern. A single `App` class inherits **11 functional mixins**, keeping GUI code cleanly separated from business logic.
128: 
129: ```
130: desktop/src/
131: ├── backend/              # Pure Python — zero DearPyGui imports
132: │   ├── api_client.py           REST client for the server API
133: │   ├── data_manager.py         DataMixin: YAML/JSON file I/O
134: │   ├── debounce.py             DebounceMixin: timer helpers + frame work queue
135: │   ├── discord_oauth.py        OAuth2 local callback server
136: │   ├── discord_service.py      DiscordService: bot lifecycle, embed posting
137: │   ├── event_bus.py            EventBus pub/sub hub
138: │   ├── lineup_model.py         LineupModel + SlotData / DJInfo dataclasses
139: │   ├── output_builder.py       OutputMixin: bridges UI state → OutputGenerator
140: │   ├── output_generator.py     Pure text generation (discord/local/quest/pc)
141: │   └── types.py                Shared dataclasses (EventSnapshot)
142: │
143: └── frontend/             # DearPyGui widgets — no business logic
144:     ├── app.py                  App class composing all 11 mixins; owns DPG lifecycle
145:     ├── ui_builder.py           UISetupMixin: full window/panel/widget layout
146:     ├── theme.py                Hex color constants + reusable style dicts
147:     ├── fonts.py                Icon class (Material Symbols), styled_text()
148:     ├── settings_manager.py     SettingsMixin: settings.json + apply_theme()
149:     ├── slot_ui.py              DPGVar, DPGBoolVar, SlotState, build_slot_row()
150:     ├── slot_manager.py         SlotMixin: add/delete/reorder slots
151:     ├── roster.py               RosterMixin: roster CRUD + drag payloads
152:     ├── genre_manager.py        GenreMixin: genre tag management
153:     ├── events_manager.py       EventsMixin: save/load/delete event lineups
154:     ├── drag_drop.py            DragDropMixin: slot reordering + DJ drop targets
155:     ├── date_time_picker.py     Calendar + time picker modal
156:     ├── import_parser.py        ImportMixin: Discord/plain-text import
157:     ├── widgets.py              Themed widget factory helpers
158:     └── utils.py                get_data_dir(), get_icon_path()
159: ```
160: 
161: ### State Flow
162: 
163: ```
164: UI widgets
165:   → DPGVar / DPGBoolVar wrappers
166:   → debounced callback (_schedule_update / _schedule_save_library)
167:   → OutputMixin._build_snapshot()  →  EventSnapshot (immutable)
168:   → OutputGenerator.generate(snap) →  formatted string
169:   → dpg.set_value(preview_tag, text)
170: ```
171: 
172: Cross-module communication uses `EventBus` pub/sub. `LineupModel` publishes `"model_changed"` on every setter.
173: 
174: ### Debounce Timings
175: 
176: | Method | Delay | Purpose |
177: |--------|-------|---------|
178: | `_schedule_update()` | 150 ms | Refresh output preview |
179: | `_schedule_roster_refresh()` | 120 ms | Redraw DJ roster panel |
180: | `_schedule_genre_refresh()` | 120 ms | Redraw genre tags |
181: | `_schedule_save_library()` | 500 ms | Persist DJ/genre library to YAML |
182: | `_schedule_auto_save()` | 5 000 ms | Write crash-recovery state |
183: | `_schedule_auto_event_save()` | 1 500 ms | Write current event snapshot |
184: 
185: ---
186: 
187: ## Data Files
188: 
189: All files live in the application directory (resolved by `get_data_dir()` — the executable's directory when frozen, the project root during development):
190: 
191: | File | Format | Contents |
192: |------|--------|----------|
193: | `lineup_library.yaml` | YAML | DJ roster, genre library, saved titles |
194: | `lineup_events.yaml` | YAML | Saved event lineups |
195: | `settings.json` | JSON | Theme colors, UI scale, user presets |
196: | `window_state.json` | JSON | Window geometry and panel widths |
197: | `auto_save.json` | JSON | Transient lineup state for crash recovery |
198: 
199: ---
200: 
201: ## Theme System
202: 
203: Themes are defined as dictionaries of hex color tokens and applied by `SettingsMixin.apply_theme()`, which builds a global DPG theme covering 30+ color slots and rounded-corner styles.
204: 
205: ### Built-in Presets
206: 
207: | Name | Description |
208: |------|-------------|
209: | **Slate** (Default) | Dark slate blue |
210: | **Midnight Blue** | Deep midnight blue with blue accents |
211: | **OLED Black** | Near-pure-black for OLED displays |
212: | **Crimson** | Deep red/rose tones |
213: | **Amber** | Warm amber/gold tones |
214: | **Forest** | Dark emerald green |
215: | **Ocean** | Deep teal/cyan |
216: | **Violet** | Rich purple |
217: 
218: ### Color Tokens
219: 
220: `primary_color`, `primary_hover`, `secondary_color`, `secondary_hover`, `success_color`, `success_hover`, `danger_color`, `danger_hover`, `accent_color`, `panel_bg`, `card_bg`, `border_color`, `hover_color`, `scrollbar_color`, `text_primary`, `text_secondary`
221: 
222: ---
223: 
224: ## Output Formats
225: 
226: `OutputGenerator.generate(snapshot)` is a pure static method — no GUI, no side effects.
227: 
228: ### Discord

232: ## House // Techno
233: ### LINEUP
234: <t:1234567890:t> | **DJ Alpha** (House)
235: <t:1234567950:t> | **DJ Beta** (Techno)

237:

<file path="desktop/src/backend/debounce.py">
  1: import queue
  2: import threading
  3: 
  4: 
  5: class DebounceMixin:
  6:     """Thread-safe debounce helpers using threading.Timer + a per-frame work queue."""
  7: 
  8:     def _init_debounce(self):
  9:         self._update_job       = None
 10:         self._roster_job       = None
 11:         self._save_lib_job     = None
 12:         self._auto_save_job    = None
 13:         self._auto_event_save_job = None
 14:         self._work_queue: queue.SimpleQueue = queue.SimpleQueue()
 15: 
 16:     def _queue_on_main(self, fn):
 17:         """Push *fn* onto the main-thread queue; executed in process_queue()."""
 18:         self._work_queue.put(fn)
 19: 
 20:     def process_queue(self):
 21:         """Call once per DPG frame from the main render loop."""
 22:         while not self._work_queue.empty():
 23:             try:
 24:                 self._work_queue.get_nowait()()
 25:             except Exception:
 26:                 pass
 27: 
 28:     # ── Timer helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 29: 
 30:     def _cancel(self, attr: str):
 31:         job = getattr(self, attr, None)
 32:         if job is not None:
 33:             job.cancel()
 34:             setattr(self, attr, None)
 35: 
 36:     def _timer(self, attr: str, delay_s: float, target):
 37:         self._cancel(attr)
 38:         t = threading.Timer(delay_s, lambda: self._queue_on_main(target))
 39:         t.daemon = True
 40:         t.start()
 41:         setattr(self, attr, t)
 42: 
 43:     # ── Public debounce gates ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 44: 
 45:     def _schedule_update(self, delay: int = 150):
 46:         self._timer("_update_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_update)
 47: 
 48:     def _run_scheduled_update(self):
 49:         self._update_job = None
 50:         self.update_output()
 51:         self._schedule_auto_save()
 52:         self._schedule_auto_event_save(delay=1500)
 53: 
 54:     def _schedule_roster_refresh(self, delay: int = 120):
 55:         self._timer("_roster_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_roster_refresh)
 56: 
 57:     def _run_scheduled_roster_refresh(self):
 58:         self._roster_job = None
 59:         self.refresh_dj_roster_ui()
 60: 
 61:     def _schedule_genre_refresh(self, delay: int = 120):
 62:         self._timer("_genre_refresh_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_genre_refresh)
 63: 
 64:     def _run_scheduled_genre_refresh(self):
 65:         self._genre_refresh_job = None
 66:         self.refresh_genre_tags()
 67: 
 68:     def _schedule_save_library(self, delay: int = 500):
 69:         self._timer("_save_lib_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_save_library)
 70: 
 71:     def _run_scheduled_save_library(self):
 72:         self._save_lib_job = None
 73:         self._save_library()
 74: 
 75:     def _schedule_auto_save(self, delay: int = 5000):
 76:         self._timer("_auto_save_job", delay / 1000, self._run_scheduled_auto_save)
 77: 
 78:     def _run_scheduled_auto_save(self):
 79:         self._auto_save_job = None
 80:         self._save_auto_state()
 81: 
 82:     def _schedule_auto_event_save(self, delay: int = 1500):
 83:         self._timer("_auto_event_save_job", delay / 1000,
 84:                     self._run_scheduled_auto_event_save)
 85: 
 86:     def _run_scheduled_auto_event_save(self):
 87:         self._auto_event_save_job = None
 88:         self._auto_event_save()
 89: 
 90:     def _refresh_slot_combos(self):
 91:         """Update all slot name-entry dropdowns."""
 92:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 93:         names = self.get_dj_names()
 94:         for slot in self.slots:
 95:             try:
 96:                 tag = f"slot_{slot.id}_name"
 97:                 if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 98:                     dpg.configure_item(tag, items=names)
 99:             except Exception:
100:                 pass
</file>
