<file path="desktop/src/backend/models/event_bus.py">
 1: """Lightweight publish-subscribe event bus for decoupled communication.
 2: 
 3: Usage
 4: -----
 5:     bus = EventBus()
 6:     bus.subscribe("model_changed", my_callback)
 7:     bus.publish("model_changed", sender=some_obj)
 8: 
 9: Subscribers receive ``(event_name, **kwargs)`` so they can inspect context
10: without being coupled to the publisher's type.
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: from collections import defaultdict
16: from typing import Any, Callable
17: 
18: 
19: class EventBus:
20:     """A simple, synchronous pub-sub hub.
21: 
22:     * **subscribe(event, callback)** — register a listener.
23:     * **unsubscribe(event, callback)** — remove a listener.
24:     * **publish(event, **kwargs)** — notify all listeners for *event*.
25:     """
26: 
27:     def __init__(self) -> None:
28:         self._subscribers: dict[str, list[Callable[..., Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
29: 
30:     # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
31: 
32:     def subscribe(self, event: str, callback: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
33:         """Register *callback* for *event*.  Duplicates are silently ignored."""
34:         if callback not in self._subscribers[event]:
35:             self._subscribers[event].append(callback)
36: 
37:     def unsubscribe(self, event: str, callback: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
38:         """Remove *callback* from *event*.  No-op if not registered."""
39:         try:
40:             self._subscribers[event].remove(callback)
41:         except ValueError:
42:             pass
43: 
44:     def publish(self, event: str, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
45:         """Call every subscriber of *event* with the supplied keyword args."""
46:         for cb in self._subscribers.get(event, []):
47:             cb(event, **kwargs)
48: 
49:     def clear(self) -> None:
50:         """Remove **all** subscriptions (useful for tests / teardown)."""
51:         self._subscribers.clear()
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/models/lineup_model.py">
  1: """Pure-Python data model for a lineup event.
  2: 
  3: This module defines plain dataclasses that capture the *complete* state of a
  4: lineup — titles, timestamps, slots, open-decks config, and DJ metadata — with
  5: **zero** dependency on Tkinter or any GUI framework.
  6: 
  7: The model is the single source of truth.  UI widgets read from / write to
  8: instance attributes, and the ``EventBus`` broadcasts changes so other
  9: subsystems (output generation, auto-save, etc.) can react without coupling.
 10: 
 11: Typical flow
 12: ------------
 13: 1. User types in a ``CTkEntry`` → UI handler calls ``model.set_title("…")``.
 14: 2. The setter publishes ``"model_changed"`` on the bus.
 15: 3. ``OutputGenerator`` (subscribed) rebuilds the preview text.
 16: 4. The output panel (subscribed) writes the new text to the textbox.
 17: """
 18: 
 19: from __future__ import annotations
 20: 
 21: import datetime
 22: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 23: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
 24: 
 25: from .types import DJInfo, EventSnapshot, SlotData
 26: 
 27: if TYPE_CHECKING:
 28:     from .event_bus import EventBus
 29: 
 30: 
 31: # ── Re-export for backward compatibility ──────────────────────────────────
 32: __all__ = ["SlotData", "DJInfo", "EventSnapshot", "LineupModel"]
 33: 
 34: 
 35: # ── Live mutable model ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 36: 
 37: class LineupModel:
 38:     """The runtime model that backs the editor UI.
 39: 
 40:     Every mutating setter publishes ``"model_changed"`` on the bus so
 41:     subscribers (output panel, auto-save timer, etc.) can react.
 42: 
 43:     The model is deliberately *not* a dataclass itself because it manages
 44:     change-notification logic and provides ``snapshot()`` for consumers
 45:     that need a frozen view.
 46:     """
 47: 
 48:     def __init__(self, bus: EventBus) -> None:
 49:         self._bus = bus
 50: 
 51:         self.title: str = ""
 52:         self.vol: str = ""
 53:         self.timestamp: str = ""
 54:         self.genres: list[str] = []
 55:         self.slots: list[SlotData] = []
 56:         self.names_only: bool = False
 57:         self.output_format: str = "discord"
 58:         self.saved_djs: list[DJInfo] = []
 59: 
 60:     # ── Snapshot ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 61: 
 62:     def snapshot(self) -> EventSnapshot:
 63:         """Return a frozen copy suitable for ``OutputGenerator``."""
 64:         return EventSnapshot(
 65:             title=self.title,
 66:             vol=self.vol,
 67:             timestamp=self.timestamp,
 68:             genres=list(self.genres),
 69:             slots=[SlotData(s.name, s.genre, s.duration) for s in self.slots],
 70:             names_only=self.names_only,
 71:             output_format=self.output_format,
 72:             saved_djs=[DJInfo(d.name, d.stream, d.exact_link) for d in self.saved_djs],
 73:         )
 74: 
 75:     # ── Bulk load (e.g. loading a saved event) ────────────────────────
 76: 
 77:     def load_from_dict(self, data: dict) -> None:
 78:         """Populate the model from a plain dict (event save format)."""
 79:         self.title = data.get("title", "")
 80:         self.vol = data.get("vol", "")
 81:         self.timestamp = data.get("timestamp", "")
 82:         self.genres = list(data.get("genres", []))
 83:         self.names_only = bool(data.get("names_only", False))
 84:         self.slots = [
 85:             SlotData(
 86:                 name=s.get("name", ""),
 87:                 genre=s.get("genre", ""),
 88:                 duration=int(s.get("duration", 60)),
 89:             )
 90:             for s in data.get("slots", [])
 91:         ]
 92:         self._notify()
 93: 
 94:     def to_dict(self) -> dict:
 95:         """Serialize to the same plain-dict format used for persistence."""
 96:         return {
 97:             "title": self.title,
 98:             "vol": self.vol,
 99:             "timestamp": self.timestamp,
100:             "genres": list(self.genres),
101:             "names_only": self.names_only,
102:             "slots": [
103:                 {"name": s.name, "genre": s.genre, "duration": str(s.duration)}
104:                 for s in self.slots
105:             ],
106:         }
107: 
108:     # ── Change notification ───────────────────────────────────────────
109: 
110:     def notify(self) -> None:
111:         """Publicly trigger a model-changed broadcast.
112: 
113:         Useful when the UI has mutated a ``SlotData`` in-place and needs
114:         to tell subscribers about it.
115:         """
116:         self._notify()
117: 
118:     def _notify(self) -> None:
119:         self._bus.publish("model_changed")
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/mixins/drag_drop.py">
 1: """
 2: Module: drag_drop.py
 3: Purpose: Slot reordering and DJ-to-lineup interaction helpers.
 4: Architecture: Mixin for App class. Drag-and-drop for slot reordering
 5:               and DJ roster → lineup insertion.
 6: """
 7: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 8: 
 9: from ..styling import theme as T
10: 
11: # ── Highlight theme (created once, reused) ────────────────────────────────
12: _flash_theme_tag = "_dd_flash_theme"
13: 
14: 
15: def _ensure_flash_theme():
16:     """Create a DPG theme that tints a group with the accent color."""
17:     if dpg.does_item_exist(_flash_theme_tag):
18:         return
19:     with dpg.theme(tag=_flash_theme_tag):
20:         with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvAll):
21:             dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Text, T.DPG_ACCENT)
22: 
23: 
24: class DragDropMixin:
25:     """Helpers for roster-to-lineup drag/drop and slot reordering."""
26: 
27:     def _add_dj_to_lineup(self, dj_name: str):
28:         """Add a DJ from the roster directly into a new lineup slot."""
29:         self.add_slot(dj_name, "")
30: 
31:     def _drop_dj_on_lineup(self, sender, app_data):
32:         """Handle DJ card dropped onto the slots panel — creates a new slot."""
33:         if app_data:
34:             self._add_dj_to_lineup(str(app_data))
35: 
36:     def _drop_on_slot(self, sender, app_data):
37:         """Unified drop handler for slot rows.
38: 
39:         Accepts two payload shapes:
40:         - ("slot_reorder", slot_id)  — reorder slots
41:         - str (DJ name)              — assign DJ to slot
42:         """
43:         if not app_data:
44:             return
45:         # Slot reorder
46:         if isinstance(app_data, (tuple, list)) and len(app_data) == 2 and app_data[0] == "slot_reorder":
47:             dragged_id = app_data[1]
48:             self._reorder_slot_by_drop(sender, dragged_id)
49:             return
50:         # DJ card drop — assign name to existing slot
51:         dj_name = str(app_data)
52:         for slot in self.slots:
53:             if slot.row_tag == sender:
54:                 slot.name_var.set(dj_name)
55:                 self._schedule_update()
56:                 from ..ui.slot_ui import _update_slot_info
57:                 _update_slot_info(slot, self)
58:                 self._flash_slot(slot)
59:                 return
60: 
61:     def _reorder_slot_by_drop(self, target_row_tag: str, dragged_slot_id: int):
62:         """Move the dragged slot to the position of the target slot."""
63:         drag_idx = next((i for i, s in enumerate(self.slots) if s._id == dragged_slot_id), None)
64:         drop_idx = next((i for i, s in enumerate(self.slots) if s.row_tag == target_row_tag), None)
65:         if drag_idx is None or drop_idx is None or drag_idx == drop_idx:
66:             return
67:         slot = self.slots.pop(drag_idx)
68:         self.slots.insert(drop_idx, slot)
69:         self.refresh_slots()
70:         self.update_output()
71:         # Flash the moved slot briefly to confirm placement
72:         self._flash_slot(slot)
73: 
74:     def _flash_slot(self, slot):
75:         """Briefly highlight a slot row with the accent theme, then revert."""
76:         _ensure_flash_theme()
77:         tag = slot.row_tag
78:         if not tag or not dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
79:             return
80:         dpg.bind_item_theme(tag, _flash_theme_tag)
81:         # Remove the highlight after 400 ms
82:         self._timer("_flash_job", 0.4, lambda: self._clear_flash(tag))
83: 
84:     @staticmethod
85:     def _clear_flash(tag: str):
86:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
87:             dpg.bind_item_theme(tag, 0)
88: 
89:     def _refresh_slot_combos(self):
90:         """Update suggestion list items on all existing slot rows after roster changes."""
91:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
92:         names = self.get_dj_names()
93:         for slot in self.slots:
94:             # The name field is now an input_text; just keep the current value as-is.
95:             # Suggestion list will always use fresh get_dj_names() when opened.
96:             tag = f"slot_name_{slot._id}"
97:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
98:                 current = dpg.get_value(tag)
99:                 # Keep current value — nothing to reconfigure for input_text
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/mixins/slot_manager.py">
 1: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 2: 
 3: from ..ui.slot_ui import SlotState, build_slot_row, build_add_slot_row
 4: 
 5: 
 6: class SlotMixin:
 7:     """Manages lineup slots: add, remove, reorder."""
 8: 
 9:     def add_initial_slots(self):
10:         build_add_slot_row(self, "slots_scroll")
11: 
12:     def _last_slot_duration(self) -> int:
13:         """Return the duration of the last slot, defaulting to 60."""
14:         if self.slots:
15:             try:
16:                 return int(self.slots[-1].duration_var.get())
17:             except (ValueError, AttributeError):
18:                 pass
19:         return 60
20: 
21:     def add_slot(self, name: str = "", genre: str = "", duration: int | None = None):
22:         if duration is None:
23:             duration = self._last_slot_duration()
24:         slot = SlotState(name, genre, duration)
25:         self.slots.append(slot)
26:         build_slot_row(slot, self, "slots_scroll")
27:         build_add_slot_row(self, "slots_scroll")
28:         self.update_output()
29: 
30:     def refresh_slots(self):
31:         """Delete and recreate all slot rows in the current order."""
32:         # Remove existing
33:         for slot in self.slots:
34:             if dpg.does_item_exist(slot.row_tag):
35:                 dpg.delete_item(slot.row_tag)
36:         # Recreate in current order
37:         for slot in self.slots:
38:             build_slot_row(slot, self, "slots_scroll")
39:         build_add_slot_row(self, "slots_scroll")
40: 
41:     def move_slot(self, slot_state: SlotState, direction: int):
42:         idx = self.slots.index(slot_state)
43:         new_idx = idx + direction
44:         if 0 <= new_idx < len(self.slots):
45:             self.slots[idx], self.slots[new_idx] = self.slots[new_idx], self.slots[idx]
46:             self.refresh_slots()
47:             self.update_output()
48: 
49:     def _duplicate_last_slot(self):
50:         if self.slots:
51:             s = self.slots[-1]
52:             try:
53:                 dur = int(s.duration_var.get())
54:             except ValueError:
55:                 dur = 60
56:             self.add_slot(s.name_var.get(), s.genre_var.get(), dur)
57: 
58:     def delete_slot(self, slot_state: SlotState):
59:         if slot_state in self.slots:
60:             slot_state.destroy()
61:             self.slots.remove(slot_state)
62:             self.update_output()
</file>

114: def hex_to_dpg(hex_color: str, alpha: int = 255) -> tuple:
115:     """Convert a CSS hex color string to a DearPyGui (R, G, B, A) tuple."""
116:     h = hex_color.lstrip("#")
117:     return (int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16), alpha)

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/types.py">
 1: """DearPyGui variable wrappers and slot state for the lineup editor UI.
 2: 
 3: These classes bridge Tkinter-style variable APIs (StringVar / BooleanVar)
 4: to DearPyGui widget values, so the rest of the codebase can use a familiar
 5: ``.get()`` / ``.set()`` interface.
 6: """
 7: 
 8: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 9: 
10: 
11: class DPGVar:
12:     """tkinter.StringVar replacement backed by a DPG input item."""
13: 
14:     def __init__(self, tag=None, default=""):
15:         self._tag = tag
16:         self._value = str(default)
17: 
18:     def get(self) -> str:
19:         if self._tag and dpg.does_item_exist(self._tag):
20:             itype = dpg.get_item_info(self._tag).get("type", "")
21:             if "Button" in itype:
22:                 label = dpg.get_item_configuration(self._tag).get("label")
23:                 return str(label)
24:             val = dpg.get_value(self._tag)
25:             return str(val) if val is not None else self._value
26:         return self._value
27: 
28:     def set(self, value):
29:         self._value = str(value)
30:         if self._tag and dpg.does_item_exist(self._tag):
31:             itype = dpg.get_item_info(self._tag).get("type", "")
32:             if "Button" in itype:
33:                 dpg.configure_item(self._tag, label=self._value)
34:             else:
35:                 dpg.set_value(self._tag, self._value)
36: 
37: 
38: class DPGBoolVar:
39:     """tkinter.BooleanVar replacement backed by a DPG checkbox item."""
40: 
41:     def __init__(self, tag=None, default=False):
42:         self._tag = tag
43:         self._value = bool(default)
44: 
45:     def get(self) -> bool:
46:         if self._tag and dpg.does_item_exist(self._tag):
47:             return bool(dpg.get_value(self._tag))
48:         return self._value
49: 
50:     def set(self, value):
51:         self._value = bool(value)
52:         if self._tag and dpg.does_item_exist(self._tag):
53:             dpg.set_value(self._tag, self._value)
54: 
55:     def trace_add(self, mode, callback):
56:         pass
57: 
58:     def trace_remove(self, mode, name):
59:         pass
60: 
61: 
62: class SlotState:
63:     """Pure-data holder for a single performer slot; UI built by build_slot_row()."""
64: 
65:     _counter = 0
66: 
67:     def __init__(self, name="", genre="", duration=60):
68:         SlotState._counter += 1
69:         self._id = SlotState._counter
70:         self.name_var     = DPGVar(default=name)
71:         self.genre_var    = DPGVar(default=genre)
72:         self.duration_var = DPGVar(default=str(duration))
73:         self.row_tag      = None  # set by build_slot_row
74: 
75:     def destroy(self):
76:         """Remove the DPG widgets for this slot."""
77:         if self.row_tag and dpg.does_item_exist(self.row_tag):
78:             dpg.delete_item(self.row_tag)
79:         self.row_tag = None
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/utils.py">
 1: import os
 2: import sys
 3: 
 4: 
 5: def _project_root() -> str:
 6:     """Return the project root (parent of desktop/)."""
 7:     return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))))
 8: 
 9: 
10: def get_data_dir() -> str:
11:     """Return the directory where runtime data files live."""
12:     if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
13:         return os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
14:     # desktop/src/frontend/utils.py → desktop/
15:     return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
16: 
17: 
18: def get_icon_path() -> str | None:
19:     """Return path to assets/icon.ico, converting from PNG on first run."""
20:     base = _project_root()
21:     ico_path = os.path.join(base, "assets", "icon.ico")
22:     if os.path.exists(ico_path):
23:         return ico_path
24:     png_path = os.path.join(base, "assets", "icon.png")
25:     if not os.path.exists(png_path):
26:         return None
27:     try:
28:         from PIL import Image
29:         img = Image.open(png_path).convert("RGBA")
30:         sizes = [(16, 16), (32, 32), (48, 48), (64, 64), (128, 128), (256, 256)]
31:         img.save(ico_path, format="ICO", sizes=sizes)
32:         return ico_path
33:     except Exception as e:
34:         print(f"[icon] Could not convert PNG to ICO: {e}")
35:         return None
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/models/types.py">
 1: """Pure-Python value objects for lineup events.
 2: 
 3: These dataclasses capture the complete state of a lineup — slots, DJ metadata,
 4: and a frozen snapshot for output generation — with **zero** dependency on any
 5: GUI framework.
 6: """
 7: 
 8: from __future__ import annotations
 9: 
10: import datetime
11: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
12: 
13: 
14: @dataclass
15: class SlotData:
16:     """One performer slot — pure data, no widgets."""
17:     name: str = ""
18:     genre: str = ""
19:     duration: int = 60
20: 
21: 
22: @dataclass
23: class DJInfo:
24:     """Saved DJ metadata from the library."""
25:     name: str = ""
26:     stream: str = ""
27:     exact_link: bool = False
28: 
29: 
30: @dataclass
31: class EventSnapshot:
32:     """An immutable snapshot of the full event state.
33: 
34:     ``OutputGenerator`` works exclusively with this — it never touches
35:     GUI widgets or the live model.
36:     """
37:     title: str = ""
38:     vol: str = ""
39:     group_name: str = ""
40:     collab: bool = False
41:     collab_with: str = ""
42:     timestamp: str = ""            # "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM"
43:     genres: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
44:     slots: list[SlotData] = field(default_factory=list)
45:     names_only: bool = False
46:     output_format: str = "discord"  # "discord" | "local"
47:     stream_link_format: str = ""    # "" | "quest" | "pc"
48:     saved_djs: list[DJInfo] = field(default_factory=list)
49:     social_links: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
50:     discord_embed_image: str = ""
51: 
52:     @property
53:     def start_datetime(self) -> datetime.datetime:
54:         """Parse ``self.timestamp`` into a ``datetime``, falling back to *now*."""
55:         try:
56:             return datetime.datetime.strptime(self.timestamp, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
57:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
58:             return datetime.datetime.now()
59: 
60:     @property
61:     def full_title(self) -> str:
62:         base = f"{self.title} VOL.{self.vol}" if self.vol.isdigit() else self.title
63:         if self.collab and self.collab_with:
64:             base += f" x {self.collab_with}"
65:         return base
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/output/output_builder.py">
  1: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
  2: 
  3: from ..models.types import DJInfo, EventSnapshot, SlotData
  4: from .output_generator import OutputGenerator
  5: 
  6: 
  7: class OutputMixin:
  8:     """Bridges the Tkinter UI with the pure-Python OutputGenerator.
  9: 
 10:     Reads widget state → builds an ``EventSnapshot`` → delegates text
 11:     generation to ``OutputGenerator`` → writes the result back to the
 12:     output textbox.  Copy helpers and format-button state remain here
 13:     because they are inherently UI concerns.
 14:     """
 15: 
 16:     # ── VRCDN link conversion (thin wrapper kept for backward compat) ─────
 17: 
 18:     @staticmethod
 19:     def _vrcdn_convert(link: str, fmt: str) -> str:
 20:         """Delegate to ``OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert``."""
 21:         return OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(link, fmt)
 22: 
 23:     # ── Snapshot builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 24: 
 25:     def _build_snapshot(self) -> EventSnapshot:
 26:         """Harvest the current UI state into an ``EventSnapshot``."""
 27:         slots = []
 28:         for s in self.slots:
 29:             try:
 30:                 dur = int(s.duration_var.get())
 31:             except (ValueError, AttributeError):
 32:                 dur = 0
 33:             slots.append(SlotData(
 34:                 name=s.name_var.get().strip(),
 35:                 genre=s.genre_var.get().strip(),
 36:                 duration=dur,
 37:             ))
 38: 
 39:         dj_list = [
 40:             DJInfo(
 41:                 name=d.get("name", ""),
 42:                 stream=d.get("stream", ""),
 43:                 exact_link=bool(d.get("exact_link")),
 44:             )
 45:             for d in self.saved_djs
 46:         ]
 47: 
 48:         # Read social links directly from widgets for real-time values
 49:         merged_social = {}
 50:         for label, _ in getattr(self, "_SOCIAL_FIELDS", []):
 51:             tag = f"social_input_{label.replace(' ', '_')}"
 52:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 53:                 val = (dpg.get_value(tag) or "").strip()
 54:                 if val:
 55:                     merged_social[label] = val
 56:             elif label in getattr(self, "social_links", {}):
 57:                 val = self.social_links[label].strip()
 58:                 if val:
 59:                     merged_social[label] = val
 60:         for key, val in getattr(self, "persistent_links", {}).items():
 61:             if isinstance(val, dict) and val.get("enabled") and val.get("link", "").strip():
 62:                 merged_social[key] = val["link"].strip()
 63:         # Also read club link widgets directly for real-time values
 64:         for label, _ in getattr(self, "_CLUB_LINK_FIELDS", []):
 65:             tag = f"group_link_{label.replace(' ', '_')}"
 66:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 67:                 val = (dpg.get_value(tag) or "").strip()
 68:                 if val:
 69:                     merged_social[label] = val
 70: 
 71:         collab_with = self.collab_with_var.get().strip()
 72: 
 73:         return EventSnapshot(
 74:             title=self.event_title_var.get().strip(),
 75:             vol=self.event_vol_var.get().strip(),
 76:             group_name=self.group_name_var.get().strip(),
 77:             collab=bool(collab_with),
 78:             collab_with=collab_with,
 79:             timestamp=self.event_timestamp.get(),
 80:             genres=list(self.active_genres),
 81:             slots=slots,
 82:             names_only=self.names_only.get(),
 83:             output_format=self.output_format.get(),
 84:             stream_link_format=self.stream_link_format.get(),
 85:             saved_djs=dj_list,
 86:             social_links=merged_social,
 87:         )
 88: 
 89:     # ── Main output builder ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 90: 
 91:     def update_output(self):
 92:         snap = self._build_snapshot()
 93: 
 94:         # Always refresh slot start-time labels regardless of output format
 95:         slot_times = OutputGenerator.compute_slot_times(snap)
 96:         for _slot, time_str in zip(self.slots, slot_times):
 97:             tag = f"slot_time_{_slot._id}"
 98:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 99:                 dpg.set_value(tag, time_str)
100: 
101:         # Sync button themes for formats, stream links, and times toggle
102:         if hasattr(self, "output_format") and hasattr(self, "names_only"):
103:             fmt = self.output_format.get()
104:             slf = self.stream_link_format.get()
105:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_discord"):
106:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_discord", "success_btn_theme" if fmt == "discord" else "secondary_btn_theme")
107:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_plain"):
108:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_plain", "success_btn_theme" if fmt == "local" else "secondary_btn_theme")
109:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_quest"):
110:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_quest", "success_btn_theme" if slf == "quest" else "secondary_btn_theme")
111:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_pc"):
112:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_pc", "success_btn_theme" if slf == "pc" else "secondary_btn_theme")
113:                 
114:             if dpg.does_item_exist("fmt_times"):
115:                 times_on = not self.names_only.get()
116:                 dpg.configure_item("fmt_times", label="Times on" if times_on else "Times off")
117:                 dpg.bind_item_theme("fmt_times", "success_btn_theme" if times_on else "secondary_btn_theme")
118: 
119:         # Delegate the heavy lifting to the pure-Python generator
120:         import pathlib as _pl
121:         _dbg_lines = []
122:         for _lbl, _ in getattr(self, "_SOCIAL_FIELDS", []):
123:             _tag = f"social_input_{_lbl.replace(' ', '_')}"
124:             _exists = dpg.does_item_exist(_tag)
125:             _val = dpg.get_value(_tag) if _exists else "N/A"
126:             _dbg_lines.append(f"  {_lbl}: exists={_exists}, val={_val!r}")
127:         import traceback as _tb
128:         _stack = "".join(_tb.format_stack()[-5:-1])
129:         _pl.Path("debug_social.txt").write_text(
130:             f"=== update_output call ===\n"
131:             f"caller stack:\n{_stack}\n"
132:             f"_SOCIAL_FIELDS widget state:\n" + "\n".join(_dbg_lines) + "\n"
133:             f"social_links dict: {getattr(self, 'social_links', 'MISSING')}\n"
134:             f"merged_social in snapshot: {snap.social_links!r}\n"
135:             f"output_format: {snap.output_format}\n"
136:         )
137:         body = OutputGenerator.generate(snap)
138: 
139:         if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text"):
140:             dpg.set_value("output_text", body)
141:             
142:         if hasattr(self, "_update_visual_preview"):
143:             self._update_visual_preview()
144: 
145:     # ── Copy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
146: 
147:     def copy_template(self):
148:         import threading
149:         text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
150:         dpg.set_clipboard_text(text)
151: 
152:     def copy_quest_links(self):
153:         self.stream_link_format.set("quest")
154:         self.update_output()
155:         self._copy_output_to_clipboard()
156: 
157:     def copy_pc_links(self):
158:         self.stream_link_format.set("pc")
159:         self.update_output()
160:         self._copy_output_to_clipboard()
161: 
162:     def _copy_output_to_clipboard(self):
163:         text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
164:         dpg.set_clipboard_text(text)
165: 
166:     # ── Format-button state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
167: 
168:     def toggle_format(self):
169:         self.output_format.set("discord")
170:         self.update_output()
171: 
172:     def set_plain_text(self):
173:         self.output_format.set("local")
174:         self.update_output()
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/output/output_generator.py">
  1: """Standalone text generator — **no Tkinter dependency**.
  2: 
  3: This module takes an ``EventSnapshot`` (a frozen, pure-Python data object)
  4: and returns fully-formatted output strings for every supported format:
  5: 
  6: * **Discord** — ``#`` headers, ``<t:UNIX:t>`` timestamps, ``**bold**`` names.
  7: * **Plain text (local)** — ``HH:MM`` times with local timezone abbreviation.
  8: * **Quest / PC** — VRCDN stream links only.
  9: 
 10: Because the generator is a pure function of data, it is trivially unit-testable
 11: without ever instantiating a Tkinter root window.
 12: 
 13: Example
 14: -------
 15:     from src.backend.types import EventSnapshot, SlotData
 16:     from src.backend.output_generator import OutputGenerator
 17: 
 18:     snap = EventSnapshot(
 19:         title="Friday Night Beats", vol="3",
 20:         timestamp="2025-06-01 20:00",
 21:         genres=["House", "Techno"],
 22:         slots=[SlotData("DJ Alpha", "House", 60)],
 23:         output_format="discord",
 24:     )
 25:     print(OutputGenerator.generate(snap))
 26: """
 27: 
 28: from __future__ import annotations
 29: 
 30: import datetime
 31: import re
 32: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
 33: 
 34: if TYPE_CHECKING:
 35:     from ..models.types import EventSnapshot
 36: 
 37: 
 38: class OutputGenerator:
 39:     """Pure-function text builder.  Every public method is ``@staticmethod``."""
 40: 
 41:     # ── Public entry point ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 42: 
 43:     @staticmethod
 44:     def generate(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
 45:         """Return the formatted output string for *snap*'s ``output_format``."""
 46:         fmt = snap.output_format
 47:         if fmt == "local":
 48:             return OutputGenerator._generate_plain(snap)
 49:         return OutputGenerator._generate_discord(snap)
 50: 
 51:     # ── Slot start-time labels (HH:MM) ────────────────────────────────────
 52: 
 53:     @staticmethod
 54:     def compute_slot_times(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> list[str]:
 55:         """Return a list of ``HH:MM`` strings — one per slot — for UI labels.
 56: 
 57:         This lets the UI update its time labels without parsing the output text.
 58:         """
 59:         start = snap.start_datetime
 60:         result: list[str] = []
 61:         ptr = start
 62:         for slot in snap.slots:
 63:             result.append(ptr.strftime("%H:%M"))
 64:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
 65:         return result
 66: 
 67:     # ── VRCDN link conversion ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 68: 
 69:     @staticmethod
 70:     def vrcdn_convert(link: str, fmt: str) -> str:
 71:         """Return a VRCDN link reformatted for *fmt* (``'quest'`` or ``'pc'``).
 72: 
 73:         Non-VRCDN links are returned unchanged.
 74:         """
 75:         if not link:
 76:             return link
 77:         m = re.match(
 78:             r"(?:https://stream\.vrcdn\.live/live/|rtspt://stream\.vrcdn\.live/live/)"
 79:             r"(.+?)(?:\.live\.ts)?$",
 80:             link,
 81:         )
 82:         if not m:
 83:             return link
 84:         key = m.group(1)
 85:         if fmt == "quest":
 86:             return f"https://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}.live.ts"
 87:         return f"rtspt://stream.vrcdn.live/live/{key}"
 88: 
 89:     # ── Private builders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 90: 
 91:     @staticmethod
 92:     def _generate_discord(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
 93:         lines: list[str] = []
 94: 
 95:         # Title
 96:         if snap.title:
 97:             lines.append(f"# {snap.full_title}")
 98: 
 99:         # Club name
100:         if snap.group_name:
101:             lines.append(f"## {snap.group_name}")
102: 
103:         # Timestamp
104:         start = snap.start_datetime
105:         unix = int(start.timestamp())
106:         lines.append(f"# <t:{unix}:F> (<t:{unix}:R>)")
107: 
108:         # Genres
109:         if snap.genres:
110:             lines.append(f"## {' // '.join(snap.genres)}")
111: 
112:         # Lineup header
113:         lines.append("### LINEUP")
114: 
115:         # Build DJ lookup for stream links
116:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs} if snap.stream_link_format else {}
117:         slf = snap.stream_link_format
118: 
119:         ptr = start
120:         for idx, slot in enumerate(snap.slots, start=1):
121:             name = slot.name or str(idx)
122:             if snap.names_only:
123:                 lines.append(name)
124:             else:
125:                 genre_str = f" ({slot.genre})" if slot.genre else ""
126:                 ts = int(ptr.timestamp())
127:                 lines.append(f"<t:{ts}:t> | **{name}**{genre_str}")
128: 
129:             # Interleave stream link after each slot
130:             if slf and slot.name.strip():
131:                 dj = dj_lookup.get(slot.name.strip())
132:                 if dj and dj.stream:
133:                     link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, slf)
134:                     lines.append(f"```\n{link}\n```")
135: 
136:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
137: 
138:         # Social links (bottom of lineup)
139:         _LINK_ORDER = ["TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"]
140:         if snap.social_links:
141:             parts = [
142:                 f"[{label}]({snap.social_links[label]})"
143:                 for label in _LINK_ORDER
144:                 if snap.social_links.get(label, "").strip()
145:             ]
146:             if parts:
147:                 lines.append("")
148:                 lines.append(" | ".join(parts))
149: 
150:         return "\n".join(lines)
151: 
152:     @staticmethod
153:     def _generate_plain(snap: "EventSnapshot") -> str:
154:         lines: list[str] = []
155: 
156:         # Title
157:         if snap.title:
158:             lines.append(snap.full_title)
159: 
160:         # Club name
161:         if snap.group_name:
162:             lines.append(snap.group_name)
163: 
164:         # Timestamp + timezone
165:         start = snap.start_datetime
166:         local_tz = datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().tzname()
167:         if sum(1 for c in local_tz if c.isupper()) >= 3:
168:             local_tz = "".join(c for c in local_tz if c.isupper())
169:         lines.append(f"{start.strftime('%Y-%m-%d @ %H:%M')} ({local_tz})")
170: 
171:         # Genres
172:         if snap.genres:
173:             lines.append(" // ".join(snap.genres))
174: 
175:         # Lineup header
176:         lines.append("LINEUP")
177: 
178:         # Build DJ lookup for stream links
179:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs} if snap.stream_link_format else {}
180:         slf = snap.stream_link_format
181: 
182:         ptr = start
183:         for idx, slot in enumerate(snap.slots, start=1):
184:             name = slot.name or str(idx)
185:             if snap.names_only:
186:                 lines.append(name)
187:             else:
188:                 genre_str = f" ({slot.genre})" if slot.genre else ""
189:                 lines.append(f"{ptr.strftime('%H:%M')} | {name}{genre_str}")
190: 
191:             # Interleave stream link after each slot
192:             if slf and slot.name.strip():
193:                 dj = dj_lookup.get(slot.name.strip())
194:                 if dj and dj.stream:
195:                     link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, slf)
196:                     lines.append(link)
197: 
198:             ptr += datetime.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
199: 
200:         # Social links (bottom of lineup)
201:         _LINK_ORDER = ["TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"]
202:         if snap.social_links:
203:             parts = [
204:                 f"{label}: {snap.social_links[label]}"
205:                 for label in _LINK_ORDER
206:                 if snap.social_links.get(label, "").strip()
207:             ]
208:             if parts:
209:                 lines.append("")
210:                 lines.append(" | ".join(parts))
211: 
212:         return "\n".join(lines)
213: 
214:     @staticmethod
215:     def _generate_stream_links(snap: "EventSnapshot", fmt: str) -> str:
216:         """Build Quest / PC stream-link-only output."""
217:         links: list[str] = []
218:         dj_lookup = {d.name: d for d in snap.saved_djs}
219: 
220:         for slot in snap.slots:
221:             name = slot.name.strip()
222:             if not name:
223:                 continue
224:             dj = dj_lookup.get(name)
225:             if dj and dj.stream:
226:                 link = dj.stream if dj.exact_link else OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(dj.stream, fmt)
227:                 links.append(link)
228: 
229:         if not links:
230:             return ""
231:         return "\n".join(f"```\n{link}\n```" for link in links)
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/services/discord_oauth.py">
  1: """
  2: Discord OAuth2 helper — no GUI imports.
  3: Opens the user's browser for Discord OAuth2 authorization,
  4: catches the redirect on a temporary local HTTP server,
  5: exchanges the code for an access token, and fetches basic user info.
  6: """
  7: 
  8: import http.server
  9: import json
 10: import secrets
 11: import threading
 12: import urllib.error
 13: import urllib.parse
 14: import urllib.request
 15: from typing import Callable
 16: 
 17: DISCORD_AUTH_URL = "https://discord.com/api/oauth2/authorize"
 18: DISCORD_TOKEN_URL = "https://discord.com/api/oauth2/token"
 19: DISCORD_USER_URL = "https://discord.com/api/users/@me"
 20: SCOPES = "identify guilds"
 21: OAUTH_PORT = 18457
 22: REDIRECT_URI = f"http://127.0.0.1:{OAUTH_PORT}/callback"
 23: USER_AGENT = "LineupBuilder/1.0 (Desktop App)"
 24: 
 25: 
 26: class DiscordOAuth:
 27:     """Manages Discord OAuth2 sign-in for a desktop app."""
 28: 
 29:     def __init__(self):
 30:         self.access_token: str = ""
 31:         self.user_info: dict | None = None  # {"id", "username", "discriminator", "avatar"}
 32: 
 33:     @property
 34:     def is_signed_in(self) -> bool:
 35:         return bool(self.access_token and self.user_info)
 36: 
 37:     @property
 38:     def display_name(self) -> str:
 39:         if not self.user_info:
 40:             return ""
 41:         name = self.user_info.get("global_name") or self.user_info.get("username", "")
 42:         return name
 43: 
 44:     def restore(self, data: dict):
 45:         """Restore saved OAuth state from settings."""
 46:         self.access_token = data.get("access_token", "")
 47:         self.user_info = data.get("user_info")
 48:         # Validate the token is still good
 49:         if self.access_token:
 50:             try:
 51:                 self.user_info = self._fetch_user(self.access_token)
 52:             except Exception:
 53:                 self.access_token = ""
 54:                 self.user_info = None
 55: 
 56:     def to_dict(self) -> dict:
 57:         """Serialize state for settings persistence."""
 58:         return {
 59:             "access_token": self.access_token,
 60:             "user_info": self.user_info,
 61:         }
 62: 
 63:     def sign_out(self):
 64:         self.access_token = ""
 65:         self.user_info = None
 66: 
 67:     def start_sign_in(
 68:         self,
 69:         client_id: str,
 70:         client_secret: str,
 71:         *,
 72:         on_success: Callable[[dict], None] | None = None,
 73:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
 74:     ):
 75:         """Open browser for OAuth2 and handle the redirect in a background thread."""
 76:         if not client_id.strip():
 77:             if on_error:
 78:                 on_error("No Client ID configured.")
 79:             return
 80: 
 81:         thread = threading.Thread(
 82:             target=self._run_flow,
 83:             args=(client_id.strip(), client_secret.strip(), on_success, on_error),
 84:             daemon=True,
 85:         )
 86:         thread.start()
 87: 
 88:     # ── Internal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 89: 
 90:     def _run_flow(self, client_id, client_secret, on_success, on_error):
 91:         state = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
 92: 
 93:         # Build authorize URL
 94:         params = {
 95:             "client_id": client_id,
 96:             "redirect_uri": REDIRECT_URI,
 97:             "response_type": "code",
 98:             "scope": SCOPES,
 99:             "state": state,
100:         }
101:         auth_url = f"{DISCORD_AUTH_URL}?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params)}"
102: 
103:         # Open browser
104:         import webbrowser
105:         webbrowser.open(auth_url)
106: 
107:         # Wait for redirect
108:         result = {"code": None, "error": None}
109: 
110:         class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
111:             def do_GET(self):
112:                 parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path)
113:                 qs = urllib.parse.parse_qs(parsed.query)
114:                 if parsed.path == "/callback":
115:                     got_state = qs.get("state", [None])[0]
116:                     if got_state != state:
117:                         result["error"] = "State mismatch — possible CSRF."
118:                     elif "error" in qs:
119:                         result["error"] = qs["error"][0]
120:                     else:
121:                         result["code"] = qs.get("code", [None])[0]
122: 
123:                     self.send_response(200)
124:                     self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html")
125:                     self.end_headers()
126:                     msg = "Sign-in successful! You can close this tab."
127:                     if result["error"]:
128:                         msg = f"Sign-in failed: {result['error']}"
129:                     self.wfile.write(
130:                         f"<html><body><h2>{msg}</h2></body></html>".encode()
131:                     )
132:                 else:
133:                     self.send_response(404)
134:                     self.end_headers()
135: 
136:             def log_message(self, *args):
137:                 pass  # silence request logs
138: 
139:         try:
140:             server = http.server.HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", OAUTH_PORT), Handler)
141:         except OSError as exc:
142:             if on_error:
143:                 on_error(f"Could not start local server on port {OAUTH_PORT}: {exc}")
144:             return
145:         server.timeout = 120  # 2 minute timeout
146:         server.handle_request()
147:         server.server_close()
148: 
149:         if result["error"]:
150:             if on_error:
151:                 on_error(result["error"])
152:             return
153: 
154:         code = result["code"]
155:         if not code:
156:             if on_error:
157:                 on_error("No authorization code received.")
158:             return
159: 
160:         # Exchange code for token
161:         try:
162:             token_data = self._exchange_code(client_id, client_secret, code, REDIRECT_URI)
163:             self.access_token = token_data["access_token"]
164:             self.user_info = self._fetch_user(self.access_token)
165:             if on_success:
166:                 on_success(self.user_info)
167:         except Exception as exc:
168:             if on_error:
169:                 on_error(str(exc))
170: 
171:     def _exchange_code(self, client_id, client_secret, code, redirect_uri) -> dict:
172:         data = urllib.parse.urlencode({
173:             "client_id": client_id,
174:             "client_secret": client_secret,
175:             "grant_type": "authorization_code",
176:             "code": code,
177:             "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
178:         }).encode()
179:         req = urllib.request.Request(
180:             DISCORD_TOKEN_URL, data=data,
181:             headers={
182:                 "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
183:                 "User-Agent": USER_AGENT,
184:             },
185:         )
186:         try:
187:             with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
188:                 return json.loads(resp.read())
189:         except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
190:             body = exc.read().decode(errors="replace")
191:             # Try to parse Discord's JSON error for a readable message
192:             detail = body
193:             try:
194:                 err = json.loads(body)
195:                 desc = err.get("error_description") or err.get("message") or ""
196:                 code_val = err.get("error") or err.get("code") or ""
197:                 detail = f"{code_val}: {desc}".strip(": ") if (code_val or desc) else body
198:             except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
199:                 pass
200:             hint = ""
201:             if exc.code == 403:
202:                 hint = (
203:                     f"\nCheck Discord Developer Portal → OAuth2 → Redirects "
204:                     f"includes exactly: {redirect_uri}"
205:                     f"\nAlso verify your Client Secret is current."
206:                 )
207:             raise RuntimeError(
208:                 f"Discord OAuth failed ({exc.code}): {detail}{hint}"
209:             ) from exc
210: 
211:     @staticmethod
212:     def _fetch_user(access_token: str) -> dict:
213:         req = urllib.request.Request(
214:             DISCORD_USER_URL,
215:             headers={
216:                 "Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}",
217:                 "User-Agent": USER_AGENT,
218:             },
219:         )
220:         with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
221:             return json.loads(resp.read())
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/backend/services/discord_service.py">
  1: """
  2: Pure-Python Discord bot service — no GUI imports.
  3: Runs a discord.py client on a background thread and exposes helpers
  4: to send messages to channels by ID.
  5: """
  6: 
  7: import asyncio
  8: import threading
  9: from typing import Callable
 10: 
 11: import discord
 12: 
 13: 
 14: class DiscordService:
 15:     """Manages a Discord bot connection on a daemon thread."""
 16: 
 17:     def __init__(self):
 18:         self._token: str = ""
 19:         self._client: discord.Client | None = None
 20:         self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
 21:         self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
 22:         self._ready = threading.Event()
 23:         self._on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None  # status callback
 24: 
 25:     # ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 26: 
 27:     @property
 28:     def is_running(self) -> bool:
 29:         return (
 30:             self._client is not None
 31:             and self._loop is not None
 32:             and self._ready.is_set()
 33:         )
 34: 
 35:     def start(self, token: str, *, on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None):
 36:         """Start the bot in a background thread. No-op if already running."""
 37:         if self.is_running:
 38:             return
 39:         if not token.strip():
 40:             if on_status:
 41:                 on_status("No bot token provided.")
 42:             return
 43: 
 44:         self._token = token.strip()
 45:         self._on_status = on_status
 46:         self._ready.clear()
 47: 
 48:         self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
 49:         self._thread.start()
 50: 
 51:     def stop(self):
 52:         """Gracefully shut down the bot."""
 53:         if self._client and self._loop and not self._loop.is_closed():
 54:             asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self._client.close(), self._loop)
 55:         self._ready.clear()
 56:         self._client = None
 57:         self._loop = None
 58:         self._thread = None
 59:         if self._on_status:
 60:             self._on_status("Disconnected")
 61: 
 62:     # ── Send helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 63: 
 64:     def get_text_channels(
 65:         self,
 66:         *,
 67:         on_result: Callable[[list[tuple[str, str, int]]], None] | None = None,
 68:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
 69:     ):
 70:         """Fetch all visible text channels from the bot's guilds.
 71: 
 72:         Calls *on_result* with a list of ``(guild_name, channel_name, channel_id)``
 73:         tuples, sorted by guild then channel name.
 74:         """
 75:         if not self.is_running:
 76:             if on_error:
 77:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
 78:             return
 79: 
 80:         async def _fetch():
 81:             try:
 82:                 results: list[tuple[str, str, int]] = []
 83:                 for guild in self._client.guilds:
 84:                     for ch in guild.text_channels:
 85:                         results.append((guild.name, ch.name, ch.id))
 86:                 results.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0].lower(), t[1].lower()))
 87:                 if on_result:
 88:                     on_result(results)
 89:             except Exception as exc:
 90:                 if on_error:
 91:                     on_error(str(exc))
 92: 
 93:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
 94: 
 95:     def get_guilds(
 96:         self,
 97:         *,
 98:         on_result: Callable[[list[tuple[str, int]]], None] | None = None,
 99:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
100:     ):
101:         """Fetch all visible guilds."""
102:         if not self.is_running:
103:             if on_error:
104:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
105:             return
106: 
107:         async def _fetch():
108:             try:
109:                 results = [(g.name, g.id) for g in self._client.guilds]
110:                 results.sort(key=lambda t: t[0].lower())
111:                 if on_result:
112:                     on_result(results)
113:             except Exception as exc:
114:                 if on_error:
115:                     on_error(str(exc))
116: 
117:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
118: 
119:     def get_roles(
120:         self,
121:         guild_id: int,
122:         *,
123:         on_result: Callable[[list[tuple[str, int]]], None] | None = None,
124:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
125:     ):
126:         """Fetch all roles for a guild."""
127:         if not self.is_running:
128:             if on_error:
129:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
130:             return
131: 
132:         async def _fetch():
133:             try:
134:                 guild = self._client.get_guild(guild_id)
135:                 if not guild:
136:                     raise ValueError(f"Guild {guild_id} not found.")
137:                 results = [(r.name, r.id) for r in guild.roles if not r.is_default()]
138:                 results.sort(key=lambda t: t[0].lower())
139:                 if on_result:
140:                     on_result(results)
141:             except Exception as exc:
142:                 if on_error:
143:                     on_error(str(exc))
144: 
145:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_fetch(), self._loop)
146: 
147:     def send_message(
148:         self,
149:         channel_id: int,
150:         content: str,
151:         *,
152:         on_success: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
153:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
154:     ):
155:         """Queue a message send.  Callbacks fire on the bot's event loop."""
156:         if not self.is_running:
157:             if on_error:
158:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
159:             return
160: 
161:         async def _send():
162:             try:
163:                 channel = self._client.get_channel(channel_id)
164:                 if channel is None:
165:                     channel = await self._client.fetch_channel(channel_id)
166:                 # Split into 2000-char chunks for Discord's limit
167:                 for i in range(0, len(content), 2000):
168:                     await channel.send(content[i : i + 2000])
169:                 if on_success:
170:                     on_success()
171:             except Exception as exc:
172:                 if on_error:
173:                     on_error(str(exc))
174: 
175:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_send(), self._loop)
176: 
177:     def send_embed(
178:         self,
179:         channel_id: int,
180:         embed: discord.Embed | None = None,
181:         *,
182:         content: str | None = None,
183:         file: discord.File | None = None,
184:         on_success: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
185:         on_error: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
186:     ):
187:         """Queue an embed message send, optionally with a file attachment."""
188:         if not self.is_running:
189:             if on_error:
190:                 on_error("Bot is not connected.")
191:             return
192: 
193:         async def _send():
194:             try:
195:                 channel = self._client.get_channel(channel_id)
196:                 if channel is None:
197:                     channel = await self._client.fetch_channel(channel_id)
198:                 kwargs: dict = {}
199:                 if embed:
200:                     kwargs["embed"] = embed
201:                 if content:
202:                     kwargs["content"] = content
203:                 if file is not None:
204:                     kwargs["file"] = file
205:                 await channel.send(**kwargs)
206:                 if on_success:
207:                     on_success()
208:             except Exception as exc:
209:                 if on_error:
210:                     on_error(str(exc))
211: 
212:         asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_send(), self._loop)
213: 
214:     # ── Internal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
215: 
216:     def _run(self):
217:         """Entry point for the background thread."""
218:         intents = discord.Intents.default()
219:         intents.message_content = True
220:         self._client = discord.Client(intents=intents)
221: 
222:         @self._client.event
223:         async def on_ready():
224:             self._ready.set()
225:             if self._on_status:
226:                 self._on_status(f"Connected as {self._client.user}")
227: 
228:         self._loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
229:         asyncio.set_event_loop(self._loop)
230:         try:
231:             self._loop.run_until_complete(self._client.start(self._token))
232:         except Exception as exc:
233:             if self._on_status:
234:                 self._on_status(f"Bot error: {exc}")
235:         finally:
236:             self._ready.clear()
</file>

 62:     def _toggle_genre(self, genre: str, is_active: bool):
 63:         if is_active:
 64:             self.remove_genre(genre)
 65:         else:
 66:             if genre.lower() not in [g.lower() for g in self.active_genres]:
 67:                 self.active_genres.append(genre)
 68:             self.refresh_genre_tags()
 69:             self.update_output()
 70: 
 71:     def refresh_genre_tags(self):
 72:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("genre_tags_frame"):
 73:             return
 74:         dpg.delete_item("genre_tags_frame", children_only=True)
 75:         if not self.saved_genres:
 76:             styled_text("Type a genre above and press Enter to add it.",
 77:                          MUTED, parent="genre_tags_frame")
 78:             return
 79:         query = self.genre_search_var.get().strip().lower() if hasattr(self, 'genre_search_var') else ""
 80:         filtered = [g for g in self.saved_genres if not query or query in g.lower()]
 81:         if not filtered and query:
 82:             styled_text("No genres match your search.",
 83:                          MUTED, parent="genre_tags_frame")
 84:             return
 85: 
 86:         active_lower = {g.lower() for g in self.active_genres}
 87:         # Intelligent greedy bin-packing: pack as many genre buttons per row to minimize empty space
 88:         container_w = 0
 89:         if dpg.does_item_exist("genre_tags_frame"):
 90:             container_w = dpg.get_item_rect_size("genre_tags_frame")[0]
 91:         if container_w <= 0:
 92:             container_w = dpg.get_item_width("genre_tags_frame")
 93:         if container_w is None or container_w <= 0:
 94:             container_w = self._LEFT_DEFAULT if hasattr(self, '_LEFT_DEFAULT') else 300
 95:         
 96:         # Compensate for padding/scrollbars inside the child window
 97:         usable_w = max(100, container_w - 20)
 98: 
 99:         frame_pad_x = 6   # mvStyleVar_FramePadding X (from theme)
100:         item_gap = 6       # mvStyleVar_ItemSpacing X (from theme)
101: 
102:         # Precompute widths for all buttons
103:         items = []
104:         for genre in filtered:
105:             text_size = dpg.get_text_size(genre)
106:             btn_w = (text_size[0] if text_size else len(genre) * 8) + 2 * frame_pad_x
107:             items.append((genre, btn_w))
108: 
109:         # Sort items descending by width for best-fit bin packing
110:         items.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
111: 
112:         rows = []  # list of tuples: (used_width, [list of (genre, btn_w)])
113:         
114:         for item in items:
115:             genre, btn_w = item
116:             placed = False
117:             # Find the first row that can fit this item
118:             for i, row in enumerate(rows):
119:                 used_w, items_in_row = row
120:                 # The extra cost is the item width + an item gap
121:                 cost = btn_w
122:                 if len(items_in_row) > 0:
123:                     cost += item_gap
124:                 
125:                 if used_w + cost <= usable_w:
126:                     rows[i] = (used_w + cost, items_in_row + [item])
127:                     placed = True
128:                     break
129:             
130:             # If it doesn't fit in any existing row, create a new row
131:             if not placed:
132:                 rows.append((btn_w, [item]))
133: 
134:         # Render the grouped rows
135:         for used_w, items_in_row in rows:
136:             row_group = dpg.add_group(horizontal=True, parent="genre_tags_frame")
137:             for genre, btn_w in items_in_row:
138:                 is_active = genre.lower() in active_lower
139:                 btn = dpg.add_button(
140:                     label=genre, parent=row_group, height=20,
141:                     user_data=(genre, is_active),
142:                     callback=lambda s, a, u: self._toggle_genre(u[0], u[1]),
143:                 )
144:                 if is_active:
145:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "success_btn_theme")
146: 
147:     # ── Genre editor popout ───────────────────────────────────────────────
148: 
149:     def open_genre_editor(self):
150:         """Open a floating window for managing the saved-genre library."""
151:         win_tag = "genre_editor_win"
152:         if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
153:             dpg.focus_item(win_tag)
154:             return
155: 
156:         def _rebuild(_wg=None):
157:             if _wg is None:
158:                 _wg = "genre_editor_list"
159:             if not dpg.does_item_exist(_wg):
160:                 return
161:             dpg.delete_item(_wg, children_only=True)
162:             if not self.saved_genres:
163:                 styled_text("No genres saved yet.", MUTED, parent=_wg)
164:                 return

166:                 with dpg.group(parent=_wg, horizontal=True):

169:                                    callback=lambda s, a, u: _move(u, -1))
170:                     dpg.add_button(label="v", small=True, user_data=i,
171:                                    callback=lambda s, a, u: _move(u, 1))
172:                     dpg.add_button(label="Del", small=True, user_data=genre,
173:                                    callback=lambda s, a, u: _delete(u))
174: 
175:         def _move(idx, delta):
176:             ni = idx + delta
177:             if 0 <= ni < len(self.saved_genres):
178:                 self.saved_genres[idx], self.saved_genres[ni] = (
179:                     self.saved_genres[ni], self.saved_genres[idx])
180:                 self._save_library()
181:                 self.refresh_genre_tags()
182:                 _rebuild()
183: 
184:         def _delete(genre):
185:             if genre in self.saved_genres:
186:                 self.saved_genres.remove(genre)
187:             if genre in self.active_genres:
188:                 self.active_genres.remove(genre)
189:             self._save_library()
190:             self.refresh_genre_tags()
191:             self.update_output()
192:             _rebuild()
193: 
194:         with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Edit Genres", width=340, height=480):
195:             styled_text("GENRE LIBRARY", HEADER)
196:             new_input = dpg.add_input_text(hint="New genre name...", width=-11)
197: 
198:             def _add_new(s, a, _ni=new_input):
199:                 val = dpg.get_value(_ni).strip()
200:                 if not val:
201:                     return
202:                 dpg.set_value(_ni, "")
203:                 self.add_genre(val)
204:                 _rebuild()
205: 
206:             add_btn = dpg.add_button(label="+ Add", callback=_add_new, width=-1)
207:             dpg.bind_item_theme(add_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
208:             dpg.add_separator()
209:             dpg.add_group(tag="genre_editor_list")
210:             _rebuild()
