<file path="desktop/src/frontend/mixins/import_parser.py">
  1: import datetime
  2: import re
  3: 
  4: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
  5: 
  6: from ..styling.fonts import HEADER, LABEL, MUTED, styled_text
  7: from ..ui.widgets import popup_pos
  8: 
  9: 
 10: class ImportMixin:
 11:     """Parses pasted Discord / plain-text event text and loads it into the editor."""
 12: 
 13:     # ── Import dialog ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 14: 
 15:     def open_import_dialog(self):
 16:         """Open a modal with a paste area and Import button."""
 17:         win_tag = "import_dialog_win"
 18:         if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
 19:             dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
 20:         with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Import Event", modal=True,
 21:                         width=480, no_resize=False, no_scrollbar=True,
 22:                         pos=popup_pos(width=480, height=300)):
 23:             styled_text("IMPORT EVENT", HEADER)
 24:             styled_text(
 25:                 "Paste a Discord or plain-text formatted event below.\n"
 26:                 "Supports: titles, timestamps, genres, lineup slots, and Open Decks.",
 27:                 MUTED, wrap=450,
 28:             )
 29:             paste_tag   = f"{win_tag}_paste"
 30:             dpg.add_input_text(tag=paste_tag, multiline=True, width=-11, height=120)
 31:             status_text = styled_text("", LABEL, show=False)
 32: 
 33:             def _preview(s, a, _pi=paste_tag, _st=status_text):
 34:                 raw = (dpg.get_value(_pi) or "").strip()
 35:                 if not raw:
 36:                     dpg.show_item(_st); dpg.set_value(_st, "Paste something first."); return
 37:                 parsed = self._parse_event_text(raw)
 38:                 if not parsed:
 39:                     dpg.show_item(_st); dpg.set_value(_st, "Could not recognise any event data."); return
 40:                 n_slots = len(parsed.get("slots", []))
 41:                 title = parsed.get("title") or "Untitled"
 42:                 genres = ", ".join(parsed.get("genres", [])) or "\u2014"
 43:                 dpg.show_item(_st)
 44:                 dpg.set_value(_st, f'"{title}" \u2014 {n_slots} slot(s) \u2014 Genres: {genres}')
 45: 
 46:             def _import(s, a, _pi=paste_tag, _st=status_text, _wt=win_tag):
 47:                 raw = (dpg.get_value(_pi) or "").strip()
 48:                 if not raw:
 49:                     dpg.show_item(_st); dpg.set_value(_st, "Paste something first."); return
 50:                 parsed = self._parse_event_text(raw)
 51:                 if not parsed:
 52:                     dpg.show_item(_st); dpg.set_value(_st, "Could not recognise any event data."); return
 53:                 self._apply_parsed_event(parsed)
 54:                 dpg.delete_item(_wt)
 55: 
 56:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
 57:                 dpg.add_button(label="Preview", width=140, callback=_preview)
 58:                 import_btn = dpg.add_button(label="Import", width=140, callback=_import)
 59:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(import_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
 60: 
 61: 
 62: 
 63:     # ── Parser ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 64: 
 65:     def _parse_event_text(self, text: str) -> dict | None:
 66:         """Parse text generated by OutputGenerator back into event data.
 67: 
 68:         Detects format automatically (Discord vs plain-text) and delegates to
 69:         the appropriate sub-parser.  Both parsers recover:
 70:           • title + volume number
 71:           • event timestamp
 72:           • genre list
 73:           • per-slot name, genre, and duration (computed from time deltas)
 74:         """
 75:         lines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.strip().splitlines() if ln.strip()]
 76:         if not lines:
 77:             return None
 78:         is_discord = any(re.search(r'<t:\d+:', ln) for ln in lines)
 79:         return self._parse_discord(lines) if is_discord else self._parse_plain(lines)
 80: 
 81:     # ── Discord format ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 82: 
 83:     def _parse_discord(self, lines: list[str]) -> dict | None:
 84:         """Parse the Discord output format:
 85: 
 86:             # Event Title VOL.3
 87:             # <t:UNIX:F> (<t:UNIX:R>)
 88:             ## Genre1 // Genre2
 89:             ### LINEUP
 90:             <t:UNIX:t> | **DJ Name** (Genre)
 91:         """
 92:         parsed = {"title": "", "vol": "", "timestamp": "", "genres": [], "slots": [],
 93:                   "names_only": False, "social_links": {}}
 94:         slot_times: list[int] = []
 95:         in_lineup = False
 96: 
 97:         for line in lines:
 98:             # ── Social links: "[LABEL](url) | [LABEL](url)" ──────────────
 99:             social = self._extract_social_links(line)
100:             if social:
101:                 parsed["social_links"].update(social)
102:                 continue
103: 
104:             # ── Event timestamp: "# <t:UNIX:F> (<t:UNIX:R>)" ────────────
105:             m = re.search(r'<t:(\d+):F>', line)
106:             if m and not parsed["timestamp"]:
107:                 dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(int(m.group(1)))
108:                 parsed["timestamp"] = dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
109:                 continue
110: 
111:             # ── Slot with timestamp: "<t:UNIX:t> | **Name** (Genre)" ─────
112:             m = re.match(r'^<t:(\d+):t>\s*\|\s*(.+)$', line)
113:             if m:
114:                 unix = int(m.group(1))
115:                 rest = re.sub(r'\*\*(.+?)\*\*', r'\1', m.group(2)).strip()
116:                 name, genre = self._extract_name_genre(rest)
117:                 slot_times.append(unix)
118:                 parsed["slots"].append({"name": name, "genre": genre, "duration": "60"})
119:                 continue
120: 
121:             # ── Section header: "### LINEUP" ─────────────────────────────
122:             if re.match(r'^#{0,3}\s*LINEUP\s*$', line, re.IGNORECASE):
123:                 in_lineup = True
124:                 continue
125: 
126:             # ── Genre line: "## Genre1 // Genre2" ────────────────────────
127:             if '//' in line and not in_lineup:
128:                 genre_text = re.sub(r'^#+\s*', '', line).strip()
129:                 parsed["genres"] = [g.strip() for g in genre_text.split("//") if g.strip()]
130:                 continue
131: 
132:             # ── Title: "# Night Beats VOL.3" (no timestamp on this line) ─
133:             m = re.match(r'^#+\s+(.+)$', line)
134:             if m and not parsed["title"] and '<t:' not in line:
135:                 self._split_title_vol(m.group(1).strip(), parsed)
136:                 continue
137: 
138:             # ── Names-only slot: plain text line inside LINEUP section ────
139:             if in_lineup and not re.match(r'^#+', line):
140:                 parsed["names_only"] = True
141:                 parsed["slots"].append({"name": line, "genre": "", "duration": "60"})
142: 
143:         self._compute_slot_durations(parsed["slots"], slot_times)
144:         return parsed if (parsed["title"] or parsed["slots"]) else None
145: 
146:     # ── Plain-text / local format ─────────────────────────────────────────
147: 
148:     def _parse_plain(self, lines: list[str]) -> dict | None:
149:         """Parse the local/plain-text output format:
150: 
151:             Event Title VOL.3
152:             2025-06-01 @ 20:00 (PST)
153:             Genre1 // Genre2
154:             LINEUP
155:             20:00 | DJ Name (Genre)
156:         """
157:         parsed = {"title": "", "vol": "", "timestamp": "", "genres": [], "slots": [],
158:                   "names_only": False, "social_links": {}}
159:         slot_times: list[int] = []
160:         in_lineup = False
161: 
162:         for line in lines:
163:             # ── Timestamp: "YYYY-MM-DD @ HH:MM (TZ)" ─────────────────────
164:             m = re.match(r'^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\s*@\s*(\d{1,2}:\d{2})', line)
165:             if m and not parsed["timestamp"]:
166:                 parsed["timestamp"] = f"{m.group(1)} {m.group(2).zfill(5)}"
167:                 continue
168: 
169:             # ── Section header: "LINEUP" ──────────────────────────────────
170:             if re.match(r'^LINEUP\s*$', line, re.IGNORECASE):
171:                 in_lineup = True
172:                 continue
173: 
174:             if in_lineup:
175:                 # ── Timed slot: "HH:MM | DJ Name (Genre)" ────────────────
176:                 m = re.match(r'^(\d{1,2}:\d{2})\s*\|\s*(.+)$', line)
177:                 if m:
178:                     time_str = m.group(1)
179:                     name, genre = self._extract_name_genre(m.group(2).strip())
180:                     unix = self._time_str_to_unix(time_str, parsed["timestamp"])
181:                     if unix is not None:
182:                         slot_times.append(unix)
183:                     parsed["slots"].append({"name": name, "genre": genre, "duration": "60"})
184:                     continue
185:                 # ── Names-only slot: any non-empty line inside LINEUP ─────
186:                 parsed["names_only"] = True
187:                 parsed["slots"].append({"name": line, "genre": "", "duration": "60"})
188:                 continue
189: 
190:             # ── Genre line: "Genre1 // Genre2" ───────────────────────────
191:             if '//' in line:
192:                 parsed["genres"] = [g.strip() for g in line.split("//") if g.strip()]
193:                 continue
194: 
195:             # ── Title: first unrecognised line before LINEUP ──────────────
196:             if not parsed["title"]:
197:                 self._split_title_vol(line, parsed)
198: 
199:         self._compute_slot_durations(parsed["slots"], slot_times)
200:         return parsed if (parsed["title"] or parsed["slots"]) else None
201: 
202:     # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
203: 
204:     _SOCIAL_LABELS = {"TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"}
205: 
206:     @staticmethod
207:     def _extract_social_links(line: str) -> dict[str, str]:
208:         """Extract ``[LABEL](url)`` markdown links whose label matches a known social field.
209: 
210:         Returns a dict of ``{label: url}`` for every match, or empty dict if none found.
211:         """
212:         pairs = re.findall(r'\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?://[^\)]+)\)', line)
213:         if not pairs:
214:             return {}
215:         result = {}
216:         for label, url in pairs:
217:             if label.upper() in ImportMixin._SOCIAL_LABELS:
218:                 result[label.upper()] = url
219:         return result
220: 
221:     @staticmethod
222:     def _split_title_vol(candidate: str, parsed: dict):
223:         """Split 'Event Title VOL.3' into separate title and vol fields.
224: 
225:         Matches the exact format produced by ``EventSnapshot.full_title``:
226:         ``"{title} VOL.{vol}"`` when vol is a digit string.
227:         Writes directly into *parsed* in-place.
228:         """
229:         m = re.search(r'\s+VOL\.(\d+)\s*$', candidate, re.IGNORECASE)
230:         if m:
231:             parsed["vol"] = m.group(1)
232:             parsed["title"] = candidate[: m.start()].strip()
233:         else:
234:             parsed["title"] = candidate
235: 
236:     @staticmethod
237:     def _extract_name_genre(rest: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
238:         """Split the right-hand side of a slot line into (name, genre).
239: 
240:         Handles:
241:           ``Name (Genre)``  – parens
242:           ``Name [Genre]``  – square brackets
243:           ``Name - Genre``  – trailing dash / en-dash separator
244:           ``Name``          – no genre
245:         """
246:         name = rest.strip()
247:         genre = ""
248: 
249:         # (Genre) or [Genre] at end
250:         m = re.search(r'\s*[\(\[]([^\)\]]+)[\)\]]\s*$', name)
251:         if m:
252:             genre = m.group(1).strip()
253:             name = name[: m.start()].strip()
254:             return name, genre
255: 
256:         # " - Genre" or " – Genre" trailing suffix
257:         m = re.search(r'\s+[-–]\s+(.+)$', name)
258:         if m:
259:             possible_name = name[: m.start()].strip()
260:             if possible_name:          # only split if name part is non-empty
261:                 genre = m.group(1).strip()
262:                 name = possible_name
263: 
264:         return name, genre
265: 
266:     @staticmethod
267:     def _time_str_to_unix(time_str: str, timestamp_ctx: str) -> int | None:
268:         """Convert 'HH:MM' to unix seconds using the event date for context."""
269:         try:
270:             h, m = time_str.split(":")
271:             if timestamp_ctx:
272:                 date_part = timestamp_ctx.split(" ")[0]
273:                 dt = datetime.datetime.strptime(f"{date_part} {time_str}", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
274:             else:
275:                 today = datetime.date.today()
276:                 dt = datetime.datetime(today.year, today.month, today.day, int(h), int(m))
277:             return int(dt.timestamp())
278:         except (ValueError, IndexError):
279:             return None
280: 
281:     @staticmethod
282:     def _compute_slot_durations(slots: list, slot_times: list[int]):
283:         """Fill in duration for each slot from consecutive start-time deltas."""
284:         if not slot_times or len(slot_times) != len(slots):
285:             return
286: 
287:         # Handle midnight wrap-around for plain-text times
288:         for i in range(1, len(slot_times)):
289:             if slot_times[i] <= slot_times[i - 1]:
290:                 slot_times[i] += 86400  # +24h
291: 
292:         for i in range(len(slot_times)):
293:             if i + 1 < len(slot_times):
294:                 diff = (slot_times[i + 1] - slot_times[i]) // 60
295:             else:
296:                 diff = 0
297:             if 0 < diff <= 480:
298:                 slots[i]["duration"] = str(diff)
299: 
300:     # ── Apply parsed data to editor ───────────────────────────────────────
301: 
302:     def _apply_parsed_event(self, parsed: dict):
303:         """Load parsed event data into the current editor state."""
304:         if parsed.get("title"):
305:             self.event_title_var.set(parsed["title"])
306:         if parsed.get("vol"):
307:             self.event_vol_var.set(parsed["vol"])
308:         if parsed.get("timestamp"):
309:             self.event_timestamp.set(parsed["timestamp"])
310: 
311:         if parsed.get("genres"):
312:             self.active_genres = parsed["genres"][:]
313:             for g in parsed["genres"]:
314:                 if g.lower() not in [sg.lower() for sg in self.saved_genres]:
315:                     self.saved_genres.append(g)
316:             self._save_library()
317:             self.refresh_genre_tags()
318: 
319:         self.names_only.set(parsed.get("names_only", False))
320: 
321:         if parsed.get("social_links"):
322:             self.social_links = parsed["social_links"]
323: 
324:         # Clear existing slots and rebuild
325:         for slot in self.slots:
326:             slot.destroy()
327:         self.slots.clear()
328: 
329:         for slot_data in parsed.get("slots", []):
330:             self.add_slot(
331:                 slot_data.get("name", ""),
332:                 slot_data.get("genre", ""),
333:                 int(slot_data.get("duration", 60)),
334:             )
335: 
336:         # Save new DJs to roster (preserve existing entries that already have links)
337:         existing_names = {d.get("name", "").strip().lower() for d in self.saved_djs}
338:         added = False
339:         for slot_data in parsed.get("slots", []):
340:             name = slot_data.get("name", "").strip()
341:             # Skip open-decks placeholders like "Slot 1: [Available]"
342:             if not name or re.match(r'Slot\s+\d+', name, re.IGNORECASE):
343:                 continue
344:             if name.lower() not in existing_names:
345:                 self.saved_djs.append({"name": name, "stream": "", "exact_link": False})
346:                 existing_names.add(name.lower())
347:                 added = True
348:         if added:
349:             self._save_library()
350:             self._schedule_roster_refresh()
351:             self._refresh_slot_combos()
352: 
353:         if dpg.does_item_exist("left_tabs"):
354:             dpg.set_value("left_tabs", "Event")
355:         self.update_output()
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/mixins/sections.py">
 1: """
 2: Module: sections.py
 3: Purpose: Collapsible section management for left-panel tabs.
 4: Architecture: Mixin for App class.
 5: """
 6: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 7: 
 8: # Default section order per tab (used as fallback when no saved order exists)
 9: SECTION_DEFAULTS = {
10:     "Event": ["evt_config", "evt_genres", "evt_links"],
11:     "Club": ["club_links", "club_vrchat", "club_sent"],
12:     "Roster": ["roster_local", "roster_booked"],
13:     "DJ": ["dj_links", "dj_logo", "dj_genres", "dj_avail", "dj_bookings"],
14: }
15: 
16: 
17: class SectionsMixin:
18:     """Manages collapsible UI sections in left-panel tabs."""
19: 
20:     def _toggle_section(self, section_id: str):
21:         """Toggle a section's collapsed/expanded state."""
22:         content_tag = f"sect_c_{section_id}"
23:         btn_tag = f"sect_btn_{section_id}"
24:         if not dpg.does_item_exist(content_tag):
25:             return
26:         currently_shown = dpg.get_item_configuration(content_tag).get("show", True)
27:         new_show = not currently_shown
28:         dpg.configure_item(content_tag, show=new_show)
29:         label = self._section_labels.get(section_id, "")
30:         dpg.configure_item(btn_tag, label=f"  {label}")
31:         self._section_collapsed[section_id] = not new_show
32:         self.save_settings()
33: 
34:     def _apply_section_order(self):
35:         """Restore saved collapsed state after UI construction."""
36:         for section_id, collapsed in self._section_collapsed.items():
37:             content_tag = f"sect_c_{section_id}"
38:             btn_tag = f"sect_btn_{section_id}"
39:             if dpg.does_item_exist(content_tag):
40:                 dpg.configure_item(content_tag, show=not collapsed)
41:             if dpg.does_item_exist(btn_tag):
42:                 label = self._section_labels.get(section_id, "")
43:                 dpg.configure_item(btn_tag, label=f"  {label}")

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/styling/fonts.py">
  1: """
  2: Module: fonts.py
  3: Purpose: Central text style configuration and font management.
  4:          Single source of truth for all text styling (colors, fonts).
  5:          Provides Icon constants, text style presets, and a styled_text() helper.
  6: """
  7: import os
  8: import sys
  9: 
 10: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 11: 
 12: from . import theme as T
 13: 
 14: 
 15: # ── Icon constants (plain text labels) ────────────────────────────────────────
 16: class Icon:
 17:     DRAG     = "\ue945"   # drag_indicator
 18:     UP       = "\ue5ce"   # expand_less
 19:     DOWN     = "\ue5cf"   # expand_more
 20:     DROPDOWN = "\ue5c5"   # arrow_drop_down
 21:     CLOSE    = "\ue5cd"   # close
 22:     CHECK    = "\ue5ca"   # check
 23:     ADD      = "\ue145"   # add
 24:     EDIT     = "\ue3c9"   # edit
 25:     DELETE   = "\ue872"   # delete
 26:     SAVE     = "\ue161"   # save
 27:     REFRESH  = "\ue5d5"   # refresh
 28:     RESTORE  = "\ue8ba"   # restore
 29:     SEARCH   = "\ue8b6"   # search
 30:     DOWNLOAD = "\uf090"   # download
 31:     UPLOAD   = "\ue2c6"   # upload
 32:     COPY     = "\ue14d"   # content_copy
 33:     FOLDER   = "\ue2c7"   # folder
 34:     PREVIEW  = "\ue8f4"   # visibility
 35:     LINK     = "\ue157"   # link
 36:     NOTES    = "\ue873"   # description
 37:     COMPUTER = "\ue30a"   # computer
 38:     CHAT     = "\ue0b7"   # chat
 39:     CODE     = "\ue86f"   # code
 40:     HEADSET  = "\ue310"   # headset
 41:     SETTINGS = "\ue8b8"   # settings
 42:     VR       = "\ue3c7"   # vrpano
 43:     SCHEDULE = "\ue8b5"   # schedule
 44:     TUNE     = "\ue429"   # tune
 45:     PASTE    = "\ue14f"   # content_paste
 46:     IMAGE    = "\ue3f4"   # image
 47:     PERSON   = "\ue7fd"   # person
 48: 
 49: 
 50: # ── Font configuration ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 51: FONT_SIZE_DEFAULT = 16    # base UI font size
 52: 
 53: 
 54: # ── Text styles ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 55: # Each style is a dict of kwargs forwarded to dpg.add_text().
 56: # To change how any text category looks app-wide, edit its dict here.
 57: 
 58: HEADER  = {"color": T.DPG_ACCENT}           # section headers: "EVENT CONFIGURATION", "DJ ROSTER", etc.
 59: LABEL   = {"color": T.DPG_TEXT_SECONDARY}    # field labels: "EVENT TITLE", "GENRES", etc.
 60: BODY    = {"color": T.DPG_TEXT_PRIMARY}      # primary body text: event titles, DJ names
 61: MUTED   = {"color": T.DPG_TEXT_MUTED}        # hints, disabled text, secondary info
 62: ERROR   = {"color": T.DPG_ERROR}             # validation / error messages
 63: HINT    = {"color": T.DPG_DRAG_HINT}         # subtle drag-hint text
 64: SUCCESS = {"color": T.DPG_IMPORT_SUCCESS}    # success feedback messages
 65: 
 66: 
 67: def styled_text(label: str, style: dict = None, **kwargs) -> int:
 68:     """Create a dpg.add_text() with centralized styling.
 69: 
 70:     Usage::
 71: 
 72:         styled_text("EVENT CONFIG", HEADER)
 73:         styled_text("field label", LABEL, tag="my_tag")
 74:         styled_text("plain text")  # no style — uses DPG theme default
 75:     """
 76:     if style:
 77:         merged = {**style, **kwargs}
 78:     else:
 79:         merged = kwargs
 80:     return dpg.add_text(label, **merged)
 81: 
 82: 
 83: # ── Font loading ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 84: 
 85: def _find_system_font() -> str | None:
 86:     """Return path to a suitable system sans-serif TTF."""
 87:     if sys.platform == "win32":
 88:         candidates = ["C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeui.ttf", "C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf"]
 89:     elif sys.platform == "darwin":
 90:         candidates = ["/System/Library/Fonts/SFNS.ttf", "/Library/Fonts/Arial.ttf"]
 91:     else:
 92:         candidates = [
 93:             "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf",
 94:             "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Regular.ttf",
 95:         ]
 96:     for c in candidates:
 97:         if os.path.exists(c):
 98:             return c
 99:     return None
100: 
101: 
102: def _find_icon_font() -> str | None:
103:     """Return path to the Material Symbols Rounded TTF bundled in assets/."""
104:     if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
105:         base = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
106:     else:
107:         # desktop/src/frontend/styling/fonts.py → 5 levels up → project root
108:         base = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))))
109:     path = os.path.join(base, "assets", "Material_Symbols_Rounded", "static",
110:                         "MaterialSymbolsRounded-Regular.ttf")
111:     return path if os.path.exists(path) else None
112: 
113: 
114: # Module-level reference to the icon font (set by setup_fonts)
115: icon_font = None
116: 
117: 
118: def setup_fonts(size: int = FONT_SIZE_DEFAULT) -> int | None:
119:     """Load a system sans-serif font and a Material Symbols icon font.
120: 
121:     Call once after dpg.create_context() and before any widget creation.
122:     """
123:     global icon_font
124:     font_path = _find_system_font()
125:     if not font_path:
126:         return None
127: 
128:     icon_path = _find_icon_font()
129: 
130:     main_font = None
131:     with dpg.font_registry():
132:         with dpg.font(font_path, size) as main_font:
133:             dpg.add_font_range_hint(dpg.mvFontRangeHint_Default)
134:             dpg.add_font_range(0x00A0, 0x02FF)   # Latin Extended
135:         if icon_path:
136:             with dpg.font(icon_path, size) as icon_font:
137:                 dpg.add_font_range(0xE000, 0xF8FF)  # PUA icon range
138: 
139:     if main_font is not None:
140:         dpg.bind_font(main_font)
141: 
142:     return main_font
143: 
144: 
145: def bind_icon_font(item_id: int):
146:     """Bind the icon font to a specific widget. No-op if icon font not loaded."""
147:     if icon_font is not None:
148:         dpg.bind_item_font(item_id, icon_font)
</file>

<file path="desktop/src/frontend/ui/date_time_picker.py">
  1: """
  2: Module: date_time_picker.py
  3: Purpose: DPG date/time input helper with a custom Sun-Sat Calendar popout.
  4:          Supports full date+time, date-only, and time-only picker modes.
  5: """
  6: import calendar
  7: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  8: 
  9: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
 10: 
 11: from .widgets import popup_pos
 12: 
 13: 
 14: # ── Full date+time picker (existing) ─────────────────────────────────────
 15: 
 16: 
 17: def add_datetime_row(tag: str, var, parent: str = "", callback=None):
 18:     """Add a DPG input_text for date/time that opens the picker on click."""
 19:     grp_kwargs = {"horizontal": True}
 20:     if parent:
 21:         grp_kwargs["parent"] = parent
 22: 
 23:     with dpg.group(**grp_kwargs):
 24:         dpg.add_input_text(
 25:             tag=tag,
 26:             default_value=var.get() if var is not None else "",
 27:             width=-1,
 28:             hint="YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM",
 29:             callback=callback,
 30:             readonly=True,
 31:         )
 32:         if var is not None:
 33:             var._tag = tag
 34: 
 35:         # Click handler on the text box opens the picker
 36:         _hr_tag = f"{tag}_click_hr"
 37:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag=_hr_tag):
 38:             dpg.add_item_clicked_handler(
 39:                 callback=lambda s, a, u=None: open_datetime_picker(var, callback)
 40:             )
 41:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(tag, _hr_tag)
 42: 
 43: 
 44: def open_datetime_picker(var, callback=None):
 45:     """Open a modal DPG window with a Sun-Sat calendar and time picker."""
 46:     current_str = var.get() if var is not None else ""
 47:     try:
 48:         current_dt = datetime.strptime(current_str, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
 49:     except ValueError:
 50:         try:
 51:             current_dt = datetime.strptime(current_str, "%Y-%m-%d")
 52:         except ValueError:
 53:             current_dt = datetime.now()
 54: 
 55:     win_tag = "dt_picker_modal"
 56:     if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
 57:         dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
 58:     if dpg.does_item_exist("dt_picker_key_hr"):
 59:         dpg.delete_item("dt_picker_key_hr")
 60:     if dpg.does_item_exist("dt_picker_wheel_hr"):
 61:         dpg.delete_item("dt_picker_wheel_hr")
 62: 
 63:     state = {
 64:         "view_year": current_dt.year,
 65:         "view_month": current_dt.month,
 66:         "sel_year": current_dt.year,
 67:         "sel_month": current_dt.month,
 68:         "sel_day": current_dt.day,
 69:         "hour": current_dt.hour,
 70:         "minute": current_dt.minute
 71:     }
 72: 
 73:     months = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
 74:               "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"]
 75: 
 76:     state["cal_btns"] = []
 77:     
 78:     def _rebuild_calendar():
 79:         header_text = f"{months[state['view_month']-1]} {state['view_year']}"
 80:         if dpg.does_item_exist("cal_month_year_text"):
 81:             dpg.set_value("cal_month_year_text", header_text.center(22))
 82: 
 83:         cal = calendar.Calendar(firstweekday=calendar.SUNDAY)
 84:         month_days = cal.monthdatescalendar(state["view_year"], state["view_month"])
 85:         
 86:         days = []
 87:         for week in month_days:
 88:             days.extend(week)
 89: 
 90:         def _select_date(s, a, u):
 91:             state["sel_year"] = u.year
 92:             state["sel_month"] = u.month
 93:             state["sel_day"] = u.day
 94:             state["view_year"] = u.year
 95:             state["view_month"] = u.month
 96:             _rebuild_calendar()
 97: 
 98:         for i, btn in enumerate(state["cal_btns"]):
 99:             if i < len(days):
100:                 dt = days[i]
101:                 dpg.configure_item(btn, label=str(dt.day), show=True)
102:                 dpg.set_item_user_data(btn, dt)
103:                 dpg.set_item_callback(btn, _select_date)
104:                 
105:                 is_current_month = (dt.month == state["view_month"])
106:                 is_selected = (
107:                     dt.year == state["sel_year"]
108:                     and dt.month == state["sel_month"]
109:                     and dt.day == state["sel_day"]
110:                 )
111:                 
112:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, 0)
113:                 if is_selected:
114:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "primary_btn_theme")
115:                 elif not is_current_month:
116:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "cal_muted_theme")
117:             else:
118:                 dpg.configure_item(btn, show=False)
119: 
120:     if not dpg.does_item_exist("cal_muted_theme"):
121:         with dpg.theme(tag="cal_muted_theme"):
122:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvButton):
123:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button, (0, 0, 0, 0))
124:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered, (255, 255, 255, 20))
125:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Text, (100, 100, 100, 255))
126: 
127:     with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Select Date & Time", modal=True,
128:                     no_resize=True, autosize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
129:                     pos=popup_pos(width=280, height=340)):
130: 
131:         # ── Calendar Navigation ──
132:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
133:             def _prev():
134:                 state["view_month"] -= 1
135:                 if state["view_month"] < 1:
136:                     state["view_month"] = 12
137:                     state["view_year"] -= 1
138:                 _rebuild_calendar()
139: 
140:             def _next():
141:                 state["view_month"] += 1
142:                 if state["view_month"] > 12:
143:                     state["view_month"] = 1
144:                     state["view_year"] += 1
145:                 _rebuild_calendar()
146: 
147:             dpg.add_button(label="<", width=25, callback=_prev)
148:             dpg.add_text("", tag="cal_month_year_text")
149:             dpg.add_button(label=">", width=25, callback=_next)
150: 
151:         dpg.add_separator()
152:         dpg.add_separator()
153:         with dpg.group(tag="cal_grid_group"):
154:             with dpg.table(header_row=True, borders_innerH=False, borders_innerV=False):
155:                 for day_name in ["Su", "Mo", "Tu", "We", "Th", "Fr", "Sa"]:
156:                     dpg.add_table_column(label=day_name, width_fixed=True, init_width_or_weight=32)
157:                 for r in range(6):
158:                     with dpg.table_row():
159:                         for c in range(7):
160:                             btn = dpg.add_button(label="", width=32, height=20, show=False)
161:                             state["cal_btns"].append(btn)
162:         dpg.add_separator()
163: 
164:         # ── Time Picker ──
165:         dpg.add_text("Time (HH : MM)")
166:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
167:             hour_tag = dpg.add_input_int(
168:                 default_value=state["hour"], min_value=0, max_value=23,
169:                 step=1, width=70)
170:             dpg.add_text(":")
171:             minute_tag = dpg.add_input_int(
172:                 default_value=state["minute"], min_value=0, max_value=59,
173:                 step=15, width=70)
174: 
175:         # Scroll-wheel on hour/minute fields
176:         _hour_hovered = {"v": False}
177:         _min_hovered = {"v": False}
178: 
179:         def _hour_hover(sender, app_data):
180:             _hour_hovered["v"] = (app_data == 1)
181: 
182:         def _min_hover(sender, app_data):
183:             _min_hovered["v"] = (app_data == 1)
184: 
185:         _hh_hr = "dt_hour_hover_hr"
186:         _mm_hr = "dt_min_hover_hr"
187:         for t in (_hh_hr, _mm_hr):
188:             if dpg.does_item_exist(t):
189:                 dpg.delete_item(t)
190: 
191:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag=_hh_hr):
192:             dpg.add_item_hover_handler(callback=_hour_hover)
193:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(hour_tag, _hh_hr)
194: 
195:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag=_mm_hr):
196:             dpg.add_item_hover_handler(callback=_min_hover)
197:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(minute_tag, _mm_hr)
198: 
199:         def _on_wheel(sender, app_data):
200:             delta = int(app_data)  # positive = scroll up, negative = scroll down
201:             if _hour_hovered["v"]:
202:                 h = (dpg.get_value(hour_tag) + delta) % 24
203:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, h)
204:                 state["hour"] = h
205:             elif _min_hovered["v"]:
206:                 m = dpg.get_value(minute_tag) + delta * 15
207:                 h = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
208:                 if m > 59:
209:                     m = 0
210:                     h = (h + 1) % 24
211:                 elif m < 0:
212:                     m = 45
213:                     h = (h - 1) % 24
214:                 dpg.set_value(minute_tag, m)
215:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, h)
216:                 state["minute"] = m
217:                 state["hour"] = h
218: 
219:         _wheel_hr = "dt_picker_wheel_hr"
220:         if dpg.does_item_exist(_wheel_hr):
221:             dpg.delete_item(_wheel_hr)
222:         with dpg.handler_registry(tag=_wheel_hr):
223:             dpg.add_mouse_wheel_handler(callback=_on_wheel)
224: 
225:         dpg.add_separator()
226: 
227:         # ── Actions ──
228:         def _confirm():
229:             state["hour"] = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
230:             state["minute"] = dpg.get_value(minute_tag)
231:             dt_str = (
232:                 f"{state['sel_year']}-{state['sel_month']:02d}-"
233:                 f"{state['sel_day']:02d} "
234:                 f"{state['hour']:02d}:{state['minute']:02d}"
235:             )
236: 
237:             if var is not None:
238:                 var.set(dt_str)
239:                 if hasattr(var, "_tag") and dpg.does_item_exist(var._tag):
240:                     dpg.set_value(var._tag, dt_str)
241: 
242:             if callback:
243:                 callback(None, None, None)
244: 
245:             if dpg.does_item_exist("dt_picker_key_hr"):
246:                 dpg.delete_item("dt_picker_key_hr")
247:             if dpg.does_item_exist("dt_picker_wheel_hr"):
248:                 dpg.delete_item("dt_picker_wheel_hr")
249:             dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
250: 
251:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
252:             ok_btn = dpg.add_button(label="OK", width=128, callback=_confirm)
253:             dpg.bind_item_theme(ok_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
254: 
255:         # ── Arrow-key handlers ──
256:         def _on_key(sender, app_data):
257:             key = app_data
258:             if key == dpg.mvKey_Up:
259:                 # Increment minute by 15
260:                 m = dpg.get_value(minute_tag) + 15
261:                 h = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
262:                 if m > 59:
263:                     m = 0
264:                     h = (h + 1) % 24
265:                 dpg.set_value(minute_tag, m)
266:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, h)
267:                 state["minute"] = m
268:                 state["hour"] = h
269:             elif key == dpg.mvKey_Down:
270:                 # Decrement minute by 15
271:                 m = dpg.get_value(minute_tag) - 15
272:                 h = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
273:                 if m < 0:
274:                     m = 45
275:                     h = (h - 1) % 24
276:                 dpg.set_value(minute_tag, m)
277:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, h)
278:                 state["minute"] = m
279:                 state["hour"] = h
280:             elif key in (dpg.mvKey_Left, dpg.mvKey_Right):
281:                 # Navigate date by +/- 1 day
282:                 delta = 1 if key == dpg.mvKey_Right else -1
283:                 cur = datetime(state["sel_year"], state["sel_month"], state["sel_day"])
284:                 nxt = cur + timedelta(days=delta)
285:                 state["sel_year"] = nxt.year
286:                 state["sel_month"] = nxt.month
287:                 state["sel_day"] = nxt.day
288:                 state["view_year"] = nxt.year
289:                 state["view_month"] = nxt.month
290:                 _rebuild_calendar()
291: 
292:         _key_hr = "dt_picker_key_hr"
293:         if dpg.does_item_exist(_key_hr):
294:             dpg.delete_item(_key_hr)
295:         with dpg.handler_registry(tag=_key_hr):
296:             dpg.add_key_press_handler(callback=_on_key)
297: 
298:     _rebuild_calendar()
299: 
300: 
301: # ── Date-only picker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
302: 
303: 
304: def add_date_row(tag: str, default: str = "", parent: str = "", callback=None,
305:                  width: int = -1):
306:     """Add a readonly input_text that opens a date-only picker on click."""
307:     grp_kwargs = {"horizontal": True}
308:     if parent:
309:         grp_kwargs["parent"] = parent
310: 
311:     with dpg.group(**grp_kwargs):
312:         dpg.add_input_text(
313:             tag=tag,
314:             default_value=default,
315:             width=width,
316:             hint="YYYY-MM-DD",
317:             readonly=True,
318:         )
319:         _hr_tag = f"{tag}_click_hr"
320:         if dpg.does_item_exist(_hr_tag):
321:             dpg.delete_item(_hr_tag)
322:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag=_hr_tag):
323:             dpg.add_item_clicked_handler(
324:                 callback=lambda s, a, u=None: open_date_picker(tag, callback)
325:             )
326:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(tag, _hr_tag)
327: 
328: 
329: def open_date_picker(input_tag: str, callback=None):
330:     """Open a modal date-only calendar picker. Writes YYYY-MM-DD to *input_tag*."""
331:     current_str = dpg.get_value(input_tag) if dpg.does_item_exist(input_tag) else ""
332:     try:
333:         current_dt = datetime.strptime(current_str, "%Y-%m-%d")
334:     except ValueError:
335:         current_dt = datetime.now()
336: 
337:     win_tag = "date_picker_modal"
338:     if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
339:         dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
340: 
341:     state = {
342:         "view_year": current_dt.year,
343:         "view_month": current_dt.month,
344:         "sel_year": current_dt.year,
345:         "sel_month": current_dt.month,
346:         "sel_day": current_dt.day,
347:     }
348:     months = [
349:         "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
350:         "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December",
351:     ]
352: 
353:     state["cal_btns"] = []
354: 
355:     def _rebuild():
356:         header = f"{months[state['view_month']-1]} {state['view_year']}"
357:         if dpg.does_item_exist("dpk_month_year"):
358:             dpg.set_value("dpk_month_year", header.center(22))
359:             
360:         cal = calendar.Calendar(firstweekday=calendar.SUNDAY)
361:         month_days = cal.monthdatescalendar(state["view_year"], state["view_month"])
362:         
363:         days = []
364:         for week in month_days:
365:             days.extend(week)
366: 
367:         def _select_date(s, a, u):
368:             state["sel_year"] = u.year
369:             state["sel_month"] = u.month
370:             state["sel_day"] = u.day
371:             state["view_year"] = u.year
372:             state["view_month"] = u.month
373:             _rebuild()
374: 
375:         for i, btn in enumerate(state["cal_btns"]):
376:             if i < len(days):
377:                 dt = days[i]
378:                 dpg.configure_item(btn, label=str(dt.day), show=True)
379:                 dpg.set_item_user_data(btn, dt)
380:                 dpg.set_item_callback(btn, _select_date)
381:                 
382:                 is_current_month = (dt.month == state["view_month"])
383:                 is_selected = (
384:                     dt.year == state["sel_year"]
385:                     and dt.month == state["sel_month"]
386:                     and dt.day == state["sel_day"]
387:                 )
388:                 
389:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, 0)
390:                 if is_selected:
391:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "primary_btn_theme")
392:                 elif not is_current_month:
393:                     if not dpg.does_item_exist("cal_muted_theme"):
394:                         with dpg.theme(tag="cal_muted_theme"):
395:                             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvButton):
396:                                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button, (0, 0, 0, 0))
397:                                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered, (255, 255, 255, 20))
398:                                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Text, (100, 100, 100, 255))
399:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "cal_muted_theme")
400:             else:
401:                 dpg.configure_item(btn, show=False)
402: 
403:     with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Select Date", modal=True,
404:                     no_resize=True, autosize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
405:                     pos=popup_pos(width=280, height=280)):
406:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
407:             def _prev():
408:                 state["view_month"] -= 1
409:                 if state["view_month"] < 1:
410:                     state["view_month"] = 12
411:                     state["view_year"] -= 1
412:                 _rebuild()
413: 
414:             def _next():
415:                 state["view_month"] += 1
416:                 if state["view_month"] > 12:
417:                     state["view_month"] = 1
418:                     state["view_year"] += 1
419:                 _rebuild()
420: 
421:             dpg.add_button(label="<", width=25, callback=_prev)
422:             dpg.add_text("", tag="dpk_month_year")
423:             dpg.add_button(label=">", width=25, callback=_next)
424: 
425:         dpg.add_separator()
426:         dpg.add_separator()
427:         with dpg.group(tag="dpk_cal_grid"):
428:             with dpg.table(header_row=True, borders_innerH=False, borders_innerV=False):
429:                 for day_name in ["Su", "Mo", "Tu", "We", "Th", "Fr", "Sa"]:
430:                     dpg.add_table_column(label=day_name, width_fixed=True, init_width_or_weight=32)
431:                 for r in range(6):
432:                     with dpg.table_row():
433:                         for c in range(7):
434:                             btn = dpg.add_button(label="", width=32, height=20, show=False)
435:                             state["cal_btns"].append(btn)
436:         dpg.add_separator()
437: 
438:         def _confirm():
439:             dt_str = (f"{state['sel_year']}-{state['sel_month']:02d}-"
440:                       f"{state['sel_day']:02d}")
441:             if dpg.does_item_exist(input_tag):
442:                 dpg.set_value(input_tag, dt_str)
443:             if callback:
444:                 callback(dt_str)
445:             dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
446: 
447:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
448:             ok = dpg.add_button(label="OK", width=128, callback=_confirm)
449:             dpg.bind_item_theme(ok, "primary_btn_theme")
450: 
451:     _rebuild()
452:

454: # ── Time-only picker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

457: def add_time_row(tag: str, default: str = "", parent: str = "", callback=None,
458:                  width: int = -1):
459:     """Add a readonly input_text that opens a time-only picker on click."""
460:     grp_kwargs = {"horizontal": True}
461:     if parent:
462:         grp_kwargs["parent"] = parent
463: 
464:     with dpg.group(**grp_kwargs):
465:         dpg.add_input_text(
466:             tag=tag,
467:             default_value=default,
468:             width=width,
469:             hint="H:MM AM/PM",
470:             readonly=True,
471:         )
472:         _hr_tag = f"{tag}_click_hr"
473:         if dpg.does_item_exist(_hr_tag):
474:             dpg.delete_item(_hr_tag)
475:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag=_hr_tag):
476:             dpg.add_item_clicked_handler(
477:                 callback=lambda s, a, u=None: open_time_picker(tag, callback)
478:             )
479:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(tag, _hr_tag)
480: 
481: 
482: def _parse_12h(val: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
483:     """Parse '8:00 PM' or '12:30 AM' into (hour_24, minute)."""
484:     val = val.strip().upper()
485:     try:
486:         dt = datetime.strptime(val, "%I:%M %p")
487:         return dt.hour, dt.minute
488:     except ValueError:
489:         return 20, 0  # fallback 8 PM
490: 
491: 
492: def _format_12h(h: int, m: int) -> str:
493:     """Format 24h (hour, minute) as '8:00 PM'."""
494:     period = "AM" if h < 12 else "PM"
495:     display_h = h % 12 or 12
496:     return f"{display_h}:{m:02d} {period}"
497: 
498: 
499: def open_time_picker(input_tag: str, callback=None):
500:     """Open a modal time-only picker. Writes 'H:MM AM/PM' to *input_tag*."""
501:     current_str = dpg.get_value(input_tag) if dpg.does_item_exist(input_tag) else ""
502:     h, m = _parse_12h(current_str) if current_str else (20, 0)
503: 
504:     win_tag = "time_picker_modal"
505:     for old in (win_tag, "tp_wheel_hr"):
506:         if dpg.does_item_exist(old):
507:             dpg.delete_item(old)
508: 
509:     state = {"hour": h, "minute": m}
510: 
511:     with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Select Time", modal=True,
512:                     no_resize=True, autosize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
513:                     pos=popup_pos(width=200, height=150)):
514: 
515:         dpg.add_text("Time")
516:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
517:             hour_tag = dpg.add_input_int(
518:                 default_value=h, min_value=0, max_value=23,
519:                 step=1, width=70)
520:             dpg.add_text(":")
521:             minute_tag = dpg.add_input_int(
522:                 default_value=m, min_value=0, max_value=59,
523:                 step=15, width=70)
524: 
525:         # Live preview label
526:         dpg.add_text(_format_12h(h, m), tag="tp_preview")
527: 
528:         def _update_preview():
529:             hv = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
530:             mv = dpg.get_value(minute_tag)
531:             dpg.set_value("tp_preview", _format_12h(hv, mv))
532: 
533:         # Scroll-wheel support
534:         _hh = {"v": False}
535:         _mm = {"v": False}
536: 
537:         def _hh_hover(s, a):
538:             _hh["v"] = (a == 1)
539: 
540:         def _mm_hover(s, a):
541:             _mm["v"] = (a == 1)
542: 
543:         for t in ("tp_hh_hr", "tp_mm_hr"):
544:             if dpg.does_item_exist(t):
545:                 dpg.delete_item(t)
546:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag="tp_hh_hr"):
547:             dpg.add_item_hover_handler(callback=_hh_hover)
548:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(hour_tag, "tp_hh_hr")
549:         with dpg.item_handler_registry(tag="tp_mm_hr"):
550:             dpg.add_item_hover_handler(callback=_mm_hover)
551:         dpg.bind_item_handler_registry(minute_tag, "tp_mm_hr")
552: 
553:         def _on_wheel(sender, app_data):
554:             delta = int(app_data)
555:             if _hh["v"]:
556:                 hv = (dpg.get_value(hour_tag) + delta) % 24
557:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, hv)
558:             elif _mm["v"]:
559:                 mv = dpg.get_value(minute_tag) + delta * 15
560:                 hv = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
561:                 if mv > 59:
562:                     mv = 0
563:                     hv = (hv + 1) % 24
564:                 elif mv < 0:
565:                     mv = 45
566:                     hv = (hv - 1) % 24
567:                 dpg.set_value(minute_tag, mv)
568:                 dpg.set_value(hour_tag, hv)
569:             _update_preview()
570: 
571:         with dpg.handler_registry(tag="tp_wheel_hr"):
572:             dpg.add_mouse_wheel_handler(callback=_on_wheel)
573: 
574:         dpg.add_separator()
575: 
576:         def _confirm():
577:             hv = dpg.get_value(hour_tag)
578:             mv = dpg.get_value(minute_tag)
579:             result = _format_12h(hv, mv)
580:             if dpg.does_item_exist(input_tag):
581:                 dpg.set_value(input_tag, result)
582:             if callback:
583:                 callback(result)
584:             for old in ("tp_wheel_hr", "tp_hh_hr", "tp_mm_hr"):
585:                 if dpg.does_item_exist(old):
586:                     dpg.delete_item(old)
587:             dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
588: 
589:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
590:             ok = dpg.add_button(label="OK", width=128, callback=_confirm)
591:             dpg.bind_item_theme(ok, "primary_btn_theme")

  1: from contextlib import contextmanager
  2: 
  3: import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
  4: 
  5: from ..styling import theme as T
  6: from ..styling.fonts import bind_icon_font
  7: 
  8: 
  9: def popup_pos(trigger_tag: str | int | None = None, width: int = 300, height: int = 200):
 10:     """Return (x, y) near *trigger_tag* (below-right of its rect), clamped to viewport.
 11: 
 12:     Falls back to the current mouse position when *trigger_tag* is None or
 13:     the item doesn't exist yet.
 14:     """
 15:     vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
 16:     vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
 17: 
 18:     if trigger_tag and dpg.does_item_exist(trigger_tag):
 19:         try:
 20:             mn = dpg.get_item_rect_min(trigger_tag)
 21:             mx = dpg.get_item_rect_max(trigger_tag)
 22:             x = int(mn[0])
 23:             y = int(mx[1]) + 4  # just below the trigger
 24:         except Exception:
 25:             x, y = dpg.get_mouse_pos(local=False)
 26:     else:
 27:         x, y = dpg.get_mouse_pos(local=False)
 28: 
 29:     # Clamp so the popup stays inside the viewport
 30:     x = max(0, min(x, vp_w - width))
 31:     y = max(0, min(y, vp_h - height))
 32:     return [x, y]
 33: 
 34: 
 35: def add_icon_button(icon: str, is_danger: bool = False, is_primary: bool = False, **kwargs) -> int:
 36:     """Create a standardized icon button, automatically binding the icon font and applying the proper theme."""
 37:     if "width" not in kwargs and "width=-1" not in str(kwargs):
 38:         kwargs["width"] = T.ICON_BTN_W
 39: 
 40:     btn = dpg.add_button(label=icon, **kwargs)
 41:     bind_icon_font(btn)
 42:     
 43:     if is_danger:
 44:         dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "danger_btn_theme")
 45:     elif is_primary:
 46:         dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "primary_btn_theme")
 47:         
 48:     return btn
 49: 
 50: def add_primary_button(label: str, **kwargs) -> int:
 51:     """Create a standard primary button with the primary theme applied."""
 52:     btn = dpg.add_button(label=label, **kwargs)
 53:     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "primary_btn_theme")
 54:     return btn
 55: 
 56: def add_danger_button(label: str, **kwargs) -> int:
 57:     """Create a standard danger button with the danger theme applied."""
 58:     btn = dpg.add_button(label=label, **kwargs)
 59:     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "danger_btn_theme")
 60:     return btn
 61: 
 62: def add_context_menu(item_tag: int | str):
 63:     """Adds a standard Copy/Paste right click menu to a text input."""
 64:     with dpg.popup(item_tag, mousebutton=dpg.mvMouseButton_Right):
 65:         def _copy(s, a, u):
 66:             val = dpg.get_value(item_tag)
 67:             if val:
 68:                 dpg.set_clipboard_text(str(val))
 69:         def _paste(s, a, u):
 70:             clip = dpg.get_clipboard_text()
 71:             if clip:
 72:                 dpg.set_value(item_tag, clip)
 73:         dpg.add_menu_item(label="Copy", callback=_copy)
 74:         dpg.add_menu_item(label="Paste", callback=_paste)
 75: 
 76: def add_styled_input(**kwargs) -> int:
 77:     """Create a stylized text input widget, applying appropriate defaults and context menu."""
 78:     item = dpg.add_input_text(**kwargs)
 79:     add_context_menu(item)
 80:     return item
 81: 
 82: def add_styled_combo(**kwargs) -> int:
 83:     """Create a dropdown combo box."""
 84:     return dpg.add_combo(**kwargs)
 85: 
 86: 
 87: @contextmanager
 88: def section(app, section_id: str, label: str, default_open: bool = True):
 89:     """Collapsible section. Use as a context manager.
 90: 
 91:     Creates a wrapper group + toggle button header.
 92:     All widgets created inside the ``with`` block go into the content group.
 93:     """
 94:     wrapper_tag = f"sect_{section_id}"
 95:     content_tag = f"sect_c_{section_id}"
 96: 
 97:     dpg.add_group(tag=wrapper_tag)
 98:     dpg.push_container_stack(dpg.last_item())
 99: 
100:     # Header toggle button
101:     collapsed = app._section_collapsed.get(section_id, not default_open)
102:     btn = dpg.add_button(
103:         label=f"  {label}",
104:         tag=f"sect_btn_{section_id}",
105:         callback=lambda: app._toggle_section(section_id),
106:         width=-1,
107:     )
108:     dpg.bind_item_theme(btn, "section_btn_theme")
109: 
110:     # Register the label for later reference
111:     app._section_labels[section_id] = label
112: 
113:     # Content container
114:     dpg.add_group(tag=content_tag, show=not collapsed)
115:     dpg.push_container_stack(dpg.last_item())
116: 
117:     try:
118:         yield content_tag
119:     finally:
120:         dpg.pop_container_stack()  # content
121:         dpg.pop_container_stack()  # wrapper

  1: import json
  2: import logging
  3: import os
  4: import threading
  5: 
  6: import yaml
  7: 
  8: log = logging.getLogger("data")
  9: 
 10: 
 11: class DataMixin:
 12:     """Handles all persistence — server-first with local YAML/JSON fallback."""
 13: 
 14:     # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 15: 
 16:     def _discord_id(self) -> str:
 17:         """Return the signed-in Discord user's ID, or empty string."""
 18:         oauth = getattr(self, "_oauth", None)
 19:         if oauth and oauth.user_info:
 20:             return str(oauth.user_info.get("id", ""))
 21:         return ""
 22: 
 23:     # ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 24: 
 25:     def load_data(self):
 26:         """Load library + events from server (fallback to local files)."""
 27:         discord_id = self._discord_id()
 28:         server_data = {}
 29: 
 30:         # Try fetching everything from the server in one call
 31:         if discord_id and getattr(self, "api", None):
 32:             try:
 33:                 server_data = self.api.get_all_user_data(discord_id)
 34:             except Exception as exc:
 35:                 log.warning("Could not fetch cloud data: %s", exc)
 36: 
 37:         # ── Library ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 38:         lib = server_data.get("library", {})
 39:         if not lib:
 40:             # Fallback to local YAML
 41:             for src in [self.LIBRARY_FILE, "lineup_data.yaml"]:
 42:                 if os.path.exists(src):
 43:                     try:
 44:                         with open(src, "r") as f:
 45:                             lib = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
 46:                         break
 47:                     except Exception as e:
 48:                         print(f"Error loading {src}: {e}")
 49: 
 50:         self.saved_titles = lib.get("titles", [])
 51: 
 52:         raw_djs = lib.get("djs", []) or []
 53:         self.saved_djs = []
 54:         for _d in raw_djs:
 55:             if not isinstance(_d, dict):
 56:                 name = _d or ""
 57:                 if not name:
 58:                     continue
 59:                 self.saved_djs.append({"name": name, "stream": ""})
 60:             elif "stream" not in _d:
 61:                 name = _d.get("name") or ""
 62:                 if not name:
 63:                     continue
 64:                 self.saved_djs.append({
 65:                     "name": name,
 66:                     "stream": _d.get("goggles", "") or _d.get("link", "") or "",
 67:                 })
 68:             else:
 69:                 name = _d.get("name") or ""
 70:                 if not name:
 71:                     continue
 72:                 self.saved_djs.append(_d)
 73: 
 74:         self.saved_genres = lib.get("genres", [])
 75: 
 76:         # ── Events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 77:         evts = server_data.get("events", {})
 78:         if not evts:
 79:             for src in [self.EVENTS_FILE, "lineup_data.yaml"]:
 80:                 if os.path.exists(src):
 81:                     try:
 82:                         with open(src, "r") as f:
 83:                             evts = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
 84:                         break
 85:                     except Exception as e:
 86:                         print(f"Error loading {src}: {e}")
 87: 
 88:         raw_events = evts.get("events", []) if isinstance(evts, dict) else []
 89:         self.saved_events = sorted(
 90:             raw_events, key=lambda e: e.get("created_at", ""), reverse=True
 91:         )
 92: 
 93:     def get_dj_names(self):
 94:         return [d["name"] for d in self.saved_djs if d.get("name")]
 95: 
 96:     # ── Persistence ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 97: 
 98:     def save_data(self):
 99:         self._save_library()
100:         self._save_events()
101: 
102:     def _save_library(self):
103:         data = {
104:             "titles": self.saved_titles,
105:             "djs": self.saved_djs,
106:             "genres": self.saved_genres,
107:         }
108:         # Local file
109:         try:
110:             with open(self.LIBRARY_FILE, "w") as f:
111:                 yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True)
112:         except Exception as e:
113:             print(f"Error saving {self.LIBRARY_FILE}: {e}")
114:         # Push to server
115:         self._push_to_server("library", data)
116: 
117:     def _save_events(self):
118:         data = {"events": self.saved_events}
119:         # Local file
120:         try:
121:             with open(self.EVENTS_FILE, "w") as f:
122:                 yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True)
123:         except Exception as e:
124:             print(f"Error saving {self.EVENTS_FILE}: {e}")
125:         # Push to server
126:         self._push_to_server("events", data)
127: 
128:     def _push_to_server(self, key: str, value):
129:         """Push a data blob to the server in a background thread."""
130:         discord_id = self._discord_id()
131:         api = getattr(self, "api", None)
132:         if not discord_id or not api:
133:             return
134: 
135:         def _push():
136:             try:
137:                 api.put_user_data(discord_id, key, value)
138:             except Exception as exc:
139:                 log.warning("Cloud save (%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
140: 
141:         threading.Thread(target=_push, daemon=True).start()
142: 
143:     # ── Auto-save (crash recovery) ────────────────────────────────────────
144: 
145:     def _save_auto_state(self):
146:         """Persist the current working session to auto_save.json for crash recovery."""
147:         state = {
148:             "title": self.event_title_var.get(),
149:             "vol": self.event_vol_var.get(),
150:             "group_name": self.group_name_var.get(),
151:             "collab": bool(self.collab_with_var.get().strip()),
152:             "collab_with": self.collab_with_var.get(),
153:             "timestamp": self.event_timestamp.get(),
154:             "genres": list(self.active_genres),
155:             "names_only": self.names_only.get(),
156:             "social_links": dict(getattr(self, "social_links", {})),
157:             "slots": [
158:                 {
159:                     "name": s.name_var.get().strip(),
160:                     "genre": s.genre_var.get().strip(),
161:                     "duration": s.duration_var.get(),
162:                 }
163:                 for s in self.slots
164:             ],
165:         }
166:         try:
167:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "w") as f:
168:                 json.dump(state, f)
169:         except Exception as e:
170:             print(f"Auto-save error: {e}")
171: 
172:     def _check_auto_save(self):
173:         """On startup, detect an unclean exit and offer to restore the session."""
174:         if not os.path.exists(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE):
175:             return
176:         try:
177:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "r") as f:
178:                 state = json.load(f)
179:         except Exception:
180:             return
181:         # Clean-exit flag written by _on_close — no restore needed
182:         if state.get("clean_close"):
183:             return
184:         # Skip if essentially empty (no title and all slots are blank)
185:         title = state.get("title", "").strip()
186:         slots = state.get("slots", [])
187:         has_content = title or any(s.get("name") or s.get("genre") for s in slots)
188:         if not has_content:
189:             return
190:         # Show a DPG confirm dialog to let user decide whether to restore
191:         self._work_queue.put(lambda s=state: self._ask_restore_modal(s))
192: 
193:     def _ask_restore_modal(self, state):
194:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
195:         win_tag = "restore_session_win"
196:         if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
197:             return
198:         title = state.get("title", "").strip()
199:         label = f"An unsaved lineup was found{(' — ' + title) if title else ''}.\nRestore it?"
200:         vp_w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
201:         vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
202:         with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Restore Unsaved Session", modal=True,
203:                         autosize=True, no_resize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
204:                         pos=[max(0, vp_w // 2 - 180), max(0, vp_h // 2 - 60)]):
205:             dpg.add_text(label, wrap=340)
206:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
207:                 restore_btn = dpg.add_button(
208:                     label="\u21BB Restore", width=140,
209:                     user_data=state,
210:                     callback=lambda s, a, u: (
211:                         self.load_event_lineup(u),
212:                         dpg.delete_item(win_tag) if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag) else None,
213:                     ))
214:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(restore_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
215:                 dpg.add_button(
216:                     label="\u00D7 Discard", width=140,
217:                     user_data=win_tag,
218:                     callback=lambda s, a, u: dpg.delete_item(u) if dpg.does_item_exist(u) else None)
219: 
220:     # ── Window state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
221: 
222:     def _restore_window_state(self):
223:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
224:         try:
225:             if os.path.exists(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE):
226:                 with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE, "r") as f:
227:                     state = json.load(f)
228:                 geo = state.get("geometry")
229:                 if geo:
230:                     # Parse "WxH+X+Y"
231:                     try:
232:                         size, pos = geo.split("+", 1)
233:                         w, h = map(int, size.split("x"))
234:                         x, y = map(int, pos.split("+"))
235:                         dpg.set_viewport_width(w)
236:                         dpg.set_viewport_height(h)
237:                         dpg.set_viewport_pos([x, y])
238:                     except Exception:
239:                         pass
240:         except Exception:
241:             pass
242: 
243:     def _on_close(self):
244:         import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg
245:         try:
246:             w = dpg.get_viewport_width()
247:             h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
248:             pos = dpg.get_viewport_pos()
249:             x, y = pos[0], pos[1]
250:             geo = f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}"
251:             with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
252:                 json.dump({"geometry": geo, "maximized": False}, f)
253:         except Exception:
254:             pass
255:         # Mark clean exit so auto-save won't prompt on next launch
256:         try:
257:             with open(self.AUTO_SAVE_FILE, "w") as f:
258:                 json.dump({"clean_close": True}, f)
259:         except Exception:
260:             pass

133:     def _load_last_event(self):
134:         """Load the most recently saved event, or the current event if set."""
135:         if self._current_event_key:
136:             title, vol = self._current_event_key

191:     def _auto_event_save(self):
192:         """Silently save the current event; bump vol if title collides with a different event."""
193:         title = self.event_title_var.get().strip()
194:         if not title:
195:             self._save_auto_state()
196:             return
197:         vol = self.event_vol_var.get().strip()
198: 
199:         def _full(t, v):
200:             return f"{t} VOL.{v}" if str(v).isdigit() else t
201: 
202:         full_title = _full(title, vol)
203:         my_key_full = _full(*self._current_event_key) if self._current_event_key else None
204: 
205:         # Detect collision with a DIFFERENT saved event
206:         clash = any(
207:             _full(ev["title"], ev.get("vol", "")) == full_title
208:             for ev in self.saved_events
209:             if _full(ev["title"], ev.get("vol", "")) != my_key_full
210:         )
211:         if clash:
212:             all_vols = [
213:                 int(ev["vol"]) for ev in self.saved_events
214:                 if ev["title"] == title and str(ev.get("vol", "")).isdigit()
215:             ]
216:             next_vol = str(max(all_vols) + 1) if all_vols else "2"
217:             self.event_vol_var.set(next_vol)
218:             vol = next_vol
219:             full_title = _full(title, vol)
220: 
221:         event_data = {
222:             "title": title,
223:             "vol": vol,
224:             "group_name": self.group_name_var.get().strip(),
225:             "collab": bool(self.collab_with_var.get().strip()),
226:             "collab_with": self.collab_with_var.get().strip(),
227:             "created_at": datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
228:             "timestamp": self.event_timestamp.get(),
229:             "genres": self.active_genres.copy(),
230:             "names_only": self.names_only.get(),
231:             "social_links": dict(getattr(self, "social_links", {})),
232:             "discord_embed_image": getattr(self, "discord_embed_image", ""),
233:             "slots": [
234:                 {"name": s.name_var.get().strip(), "genre": s.genre_var.get().strip(), "duration": s.duration_var.get()}
235:                 for s in self.slots
236:             ]
237:         }
238: 
239:         existing_idx = next(
240:             (i for i, ev in enumerate(self.saved_events)
241:              if _full(ev["title"], ev.get("vol", "")) == full_title),
242:             None
243:         )
244:         if existing_idx is not None:
245:             self.saved_events[existing_idx] = event_data
246:         else:
247:             self.saved_events.append(event_data)
248: 
249:         self._current_event_key = (title, vol)
250:         self.saved_events.sort(key=lambda e: e.get("created_at", ""), reverse=True)
251:         self._save_events()
252:         self.refresh_saved_events_ui()

254:     def delete_event_lineup(self, event_data):
255:         full_title = (
256:             f"{event_data['title']} VOL.{event_data.get('vol', '')}"
257:             if event_data.get('vol', '').isdigit()
258:             else event_data['title']
259:         )
260:         wt = "delete_event_confirm"
261:         if dpg.does_item_exist(wt):

263:         def _do_delete(_wt=wt, _ev=event_data):
264:             if _ev in self.saved_events:

266:             self._save_events()
267:             self.refresh_saved_events_ui()

271:                         autosize=True, no_resize=True, no_scrollbar=True,

274:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
275:                 _yes = dpg.add_button(label="Yes", width=140, callback=lambda s, a, u=None: _do_delete())
276:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(_yes, self._danger_btn_theme)
277:                 dpg.add_button(label="No", width=140, user_data=wt,
278:                                callback=lambda s, a, u: dpg.delete_item(u))
279: 
280:     def duplicate_event_lineup(self, event_data):
281:         dupe = copy.deepcopy(event_data)
282:         try:
283:             dupe["vol"] = str(int(dupe.get("vol", "0")) + 1)
284:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
285:             dupe["vol"] = ""
286:         dupe["created_at"] = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
287:         self.saved_events.append(dupe)
288:         self.saved_events.sort(key=lambda e: e.get("created_at", ""), reverse=True)
289:         self._save_events()
290:         self.refresh_saved_events_ui()
291: 
292:     def _toggle_saved_events_drawer(self):
293:         """Toggle the saved events drawer open/closed inside the Event tab."""
294:         self._saved_events_drawer_open = not self._saved_events_drawer_open
295:         show = self._saved_events_drawer_open
296:         dpg.configure_item("saved_events_drawer", show=show)
297:         if show:
298:             self.refresh_saved_events_ui()
299: 
300:     def refresh_saved_events_ui(self):
301:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("saved_events_scroll"):
302:             return
303:         dpg.delete_item("saved_events_scroll", children_only=True)
304:         if not self.saved_events:
305:             styled_text("No saved events yet.",
306:                          LABEL, parent="saved_events_scroll")
307:             return
308:         for ev in self.saved_events:
309:             full_title = (
310:                 f"{ev['title']} VOL.{ev.get('vol', '')}"
311:                 if ev.get('vol', '').isdigit()
312:                 else ev['title']
313:             )
314:             slots_count = len(ev.get("slots", []))
315:             timestamp = ev.get("timestamp", "")
316:             saved_at = ev.get("created_at", "")[:16]
317:             with dpg.group(parent="saved_events_scroll"):
318:                 with dpg.table(header_row=False, borders_innerH=False, borders_innerV=False, borders_outerH=False, borders_outerV=False, pad_outerX=False):
319:                     dpg.add_table_column(width_stretch=True)
320:                     dpg.add_table_column(width_fixed=True)
321:                     with dpg.table_row():
322:                         with dpg.group():
323:                             styled_text(full_title, BODY)
324:                             styled_text(f"{timestamp}  |  {slots_count} slots", MUTED)
325:                         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
326:                             add_icon_button(Icon.DOWNLOAD, width=28, height=20, user_data=ev, callback=lambda s, a, u: (self.load_event_lineup(u), self._toggle_saved_events_drawer() if self._saved_events_drawer_open else None))
327:                             add_icon_button(Icon.COPY, width=28, height=20, user_data=ev, callback=lambda s, a, u: self.duplicate_event_lineup(u))
328:                             add_icon_button(Icon.DELETE, width=28, height=20, is_danger=True, user_data=ev, callback=lambda s, a, u: self.delete_event_lineup(u))
329:                             dpg.add_spacer(width=10)
330:                 dpg.add_separator()
</file>

 17: def _load_dotenv() -> dict[str, str]:
 18:     """Read key=value pairs from .env in the data directory (no third-party dep)."""
 19:     env: dict[str, str] = {}
 20:     env_path = os.path.join(get_data_dir(), ".env")
 21:     if not os.path.isfile(env_path):
 22:         return env
 23:     try:
 24:         with open(env_path) as f:
 25:             for line in f:
 26:                 line = line.strip()
 27:                 if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
 28:                     continue
 29:                 key, _, value = line.partition("=")
 30:                 env[key.strip()] = value.strip()
 31:     except Exception:
 32:         pass
 33:     return env
