196: 
197: class SettingsMixin:
198:     """Manages application settings: persistence, theme application, and Settings UI tab."""
199: 
200:     # ── Init & persistence ────────────────────────────────────────────────
201: 
202:     def load_settings(self):
203:         """Load settings from JSON, falling back to defaults. Must be called before setup_ui."""
204:         self.settings = dict(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)
205:         self.user_presets: list = []
206:         self.sync_data_dir: str = ""
207:         self.persistent_links: dict = {
208:             "DISCORD":   {"link": "", "enabled": False},
209:             "VRC GROUP": {"link": "", "enabled": False},
210:         }
211:         self.dj_profile: dict = {
212:             "name": "",
213:             "links": {},
214:             "logo": "",
215:             "availability": [],
216:         }
217:         self.discord_channels: dict = {
218:             "events": "",
219:             "popup": "",
220:             "signups": "",
221:         }
222:         self.discord_bot_token: str = ""
223:         self.discord_client_id: str = ""
224:         self.discord_embed_image: str = ""
225:         self.discord_ping_server: str = ""
226:         self.discord_ping_roles: str = ""
227:         self.discord_channel_id: str = ""
228: 
229:         # Section layout state (collapsible drawers)
230:         self._section_collapsed: dict[str, bool] = {}
231:         self._section_labels: dict[str, str] = {}
232: 
233:         # Load .env defaults
234:         env = _load_dotenv()
235:         if env.get("DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN"):
236:             self.discord_bot_token = env["DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN"]
237:         if env.get("DISCORD_CLIENT_ID"):
238:             self.discord_client_id = env["DISCORD_CLIENT_ID"]
239: 
240:         if os.path.exists(SETTINGS_FILE):
241:             try:
242:                 with open(SETTINGS_FILE) as f:
243:                     data = json.load(f)
244:                 self.settings.update(
245:                     {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in DEFAULT_SETTINGS}
246:                 )
247:                 self.user_presets = data.get("user_presets", [])
248:                 self.sync_data_dir = data.get("sync_data_dir", "")
249:                 for key in self.persistent_links:
250:                     saved = data.get("persistent_links", {}).get(key)
251:                     if isinstance(saved, dict):
252:                         self.persistent_links[key] = saved
253:                 saved_profile = data.get("dj_profile", {})
254:                 if isinstance(saved_profile, dict):
255:                     self.dj_profile.update(saved_profile)
256:                 saved_channels = data.get("discord_channels", {})
257:                 if isinstance(saved_channels, dict):
258:                     self.discord_channels.update(saved_channels)
259:                 self.discord_bot_token = data.get("discord_bot_token", "")
260:                 self.discord_client_id = data.get("discord_client_id", "")
261:                 self.discord_ping_server = data.get("discord_ping_server", "")
262:                 self.discord_ping_roles = data.get("discord_ping_roles", "")
263:                 self.discord_channel_id = data.get("discord_channel_id", "")
264:                 # Section layout persistence
265:                 saved_collapsed = data.get("section_collapsed", {})
266:                 if isinstance(saved_collapsed, dict):
267:                     self._section_collapsed.update(saved_collapsed)
268:             except Exception:
269:                 pass
270: 
271:         # Tracks the last-applied color for each key
272:         self._applied_settings: dict = dict(self.settings)
273:         self._global_theme = None
274:         self._danger_btn_theme = None
275: 
276: 
277:     def save_settings(self):
278:         # ── Local file (everything, including secrets) ────────────────────
279:         try:
280:             with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "w") as f:
281:                 json.dump(
282:                     {**self.settings, "user_presets": self.user_presets,
283:                      "sync_data_dir": getattr(self, "sync_data_dir", ""),
284:                      "persistent_links": getattr(self, "persistent_links", {}),
285:                      "dj_profile": getattr(self, "dj_profile", {}),
286:                      "discord_channels": getattr(self, "discord_channels", {}),
287:                      "discord_bot_token": getattr(self, "discord_bot_token", ""),
288:                      "discord_client_id": getattr(self, "discord_client_id", ""),
289:                      "discord_ping_server": getattr(self, "discord_ping_server", ""),
290:                      "discord_ping_roles": getattr(self, "discord_ping_roles", ""),
291:                      "discord_channel_id": getattr(self, "discord_channel_id", ""),
292:                      "section_collapsed": getattr(self, "_section_collapsed", {})},
293:                     f, indent=2,
294:                 )
295:         except Exception as e:
296:             print(f"Error saving settings: {e}")
297: 
298:     # ── Theme application ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
299: 
300:     def apply_theme(self):
301:         """Build and bind a global DPG theme from current settings."""
302:         def _c(hex_val, alpha=255):
303:             h = hex_val.lstrip("#")
304:             r, g, b = int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16)
305:             return (r, g, b, alpha)
306: 
307:         s = self.settings
308:         with dpg.theme() as global_theme:
309:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvAll):
310:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_WindowBg,           _c(s.get("panel_bg",             "#1E293B")))
311:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ChildBg,             _c(s.get("panel_bg",            "#1E293B")))
312:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_FrameBg,             _c(s.get("card_bg",             "#0F172A")))
313:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_FrameBgHovered,      _c(s.get("hover_color",         "#334155")))
314:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_FrameBgActive,       _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
315:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button,              _c(s.get("secondary_color",     "#334155")))
316:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered,       _c(s.get("secondary_hover",     "#475569")))
317:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonActive,        _c(s.get("secondary_color",     "#334155")))
318:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Text,                _c(s.get("text_primary",        "#CBD5E1")))
319:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_TextDisabled,        _c(s.get("text_secondary",      "#94A3B8")))
320:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Border,              _c(s.get("border_color",        "#334155")))
321:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ScrollbarBg,         (0, 0, 0, 0))
322:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ScrollbarGrab,       _c(s.get("scrollbar_color",     "#334155")))
323:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ScrollbarGrabHovered,_c(s.get("hover_color",         "#475569")))
324:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ScrollbarGrabActive, _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
325:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Header,              _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
326:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_HeaderHovered,       _c(s.get("primary_hover_color", "#4338CA")))
327:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Tab,                 _c(s.get("panel_bg",            "#1E293B")))
328:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_TabHovered,          _c(s.get("primary_hover_color", "#4338CA")))
329:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_TabActive,           _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
330:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_TitleBg,             _c(s.get("panel_bg",            "#1E293B")))
331:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_TitleBgActive,       _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
332:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_PopupBg,             _c(s.get("panel_bg",            "#1E293B")))
333:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Separator,           _c(s.get("border_color",        "#334155")))
334:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_CheckMark,           _c(s.get("accent_color",        "#818CF8")))
335:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_SliderGrab,          _c(s.get("primary_color",       "#4F46E5")))
336:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_SliderGrabActive,    _c(s.get("primary_hover_color", "#4338CA")))
337:                 # Rounding  (from Style)
338:                 S = T.Style
339:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_FrameRounding,    S.FRAME_ROUNDING)
340:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ChildRounding,     S.CHILD_ROUNDING)
341:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowRounding,    S.WINDOW_ROUNDING)
342:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_PopupRounding,     S.POPUP_ROUNDING)
343:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ScrollbarRounding, S.SCROLLBAR_ROUNDING)
344:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_GrabRounding,      S.GRAB_ROUNDING)
345:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_TabRounding,       S.TAB_ROUNDING)
346:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ButtonTextAlign,   S.BTN_ALIGN_X, S.BTN_ALIGN_Y)
347:                 # Sizing  (fixed — font_scale handles perceived size)
348:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_FramePadding,     S.FRAME_PAD_X, S.FRAME_PAD_Y)
349:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ItemSpacing,      S.ITEM_SPACING_X, S.ITEM_SPACING_Y)
350:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ItemInnerSpacing, S.INNER_SPACING_X, S.INNER_SPACING_Y)
351:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ScrollbarSize,    S.SCROLLBAR_SIZE)
352:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_GrabMinSize,      S.GRAB_MIN_SIZE)
353:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowPadding,    S.WINDOW_PAD_X, S.WINDOW_PAD_Y)
354:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, S.WINDOW_BORDER)
355:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_FrameBorderSize,  S.FRAME_BORDER)
356:         # ── Explicit Button Themes ──────────────────────────────────────
357:         _btn_tags = ["primary_btn_theme", "secondary_btn_theme",
358:                      "success_btn_theme", "danger_btn_theme",
359:                      "resize_handle_theme", "local_toggle_active_theme",
360:                      "section_btn_theme"]
361:         for t in _btn_tags:
362:             try:
363:                 if dpg.does_item_exist(t):
364:                     dpg.delete_item(t)
365:                 if dpg.does_alias_exist(t):
366:                     dpg.remove_alias(t)
367:             except Exception:
368:                 pass
369: 
370:         with dpg.theme(tag="primary_btn_theme"):
371:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvButton):
372:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button,        _c(s.get("primary_color", "#4F46E5")))
373:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered, _c(s.get("primary_hover", "#4338CA")))
374:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonActive,  _c(s.get("primary_color", "#4F46E5")))
375: 
376:         with dpg.theme(tag="secondary_btn_theme"):
377:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvButton):
378:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button,        _c(s.get("secondary_color", "#334155")))
379:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered, _c(s.get("secondary_hover", "#475569")))
380:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonActive,  _c(s.get("secondary_color", "#334155")))
381: 
382:         with dpg.theme(tag="success_btn_theme"):
383:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvButton):
384:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Button,        _c(s.get("success_color", "#059669")))
385:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonHovered, _c(s.get("success_hover", "#047857")))
386:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ButtonActive,  _c(s.get("success_color", "#059669")))
387:                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_Text,          (10, 10, 10, 255))

446:         )
447: 
448:     def _set_titlebar_color(self, bg: str, text: str, border: str):
449:         """Set the Windows title bar background, text, and border color via DWM API (Windows 11+)."""
450:         import sys
451:         if sys.platform != "win32":
452:             return
453:         import ctypes
454: 
455:         def _colorref(hex_val):
456:             h = hex_val.lstrip("#")
457:             r, g, b = int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16)
458:             return ctypes.c_ulong(b | (g << 8) | (r << 16))  # DWM expects BGR
459: 
460:         DWMWA_BORDER_COLOR  = 34
461:         DWMWA_CAPTION_COLOR = 35
462:         DWMWA_TEXT_COLOR    = 36
463:         try:
464:             hwnd = ctypes.windll.user32.FindWindowW(None, "Lineup Builder")
465:             if hwnd:
466:                 for attr, val in [
467:                     (DWMWA_BORDER_COLOR,  _colorref(border)),
468:                     (DWMWA_CAPTION_COLOR, _colorref(bg)),
469:                     (DWMWA_TEXT_COLOR,    _colorref(text)),
470:                 ]:
471:                     ctypes.windll.dwmapi.DwmSetWindowAttribute(
472:                         hwnd, attr, ctypes.byref(val), ctypes.sizeof(val)
473:                     )
474:         except Exception:
475:             pass
476: 
477:     # ── Preset helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
478: 
479:     def apply_preset(self, preset_settings: dict):
480:         """Load a preset's color/font values and rebuild the settings tab."""
481:         self._applied_settings = dict(self.settings)
482:         self.settings.update(
483:             {k: v for k, v in preset_settings.items() if k in DEFAULT_SETTINGS}
484:         )
485:         self.save_settings()
486:         self.apply_theme()
487:         self._build_settings_tab()
488: 
489:     def save_current_as_preset(self, name: str):
490:         name = name.strip()
491:         if not name:
492:             return
493:         # Overwrite if same name exists
494:         self.user_presets = [p for p in self.user_presets if p["name"] != name]
495:         self.user_presets.append({"name": name, "settings": dict(self.settings)})
496:         self.save_settings()
497: 
498:     def delete_preset(self, name: str):
499:         self.user_presets = [p for p in self.user_presets if p["name"] != name]
500:         self.save_settings()
501: 
502:     # ── Settings tab builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────
503: 
504:     def _build_settings_tab(self):
505:         container = "settings_scroll"
506:         if not dpg.does_item_exist(container):
507:             return
508:         dpg.delete_item(container, children_only=True)
509: 
510: 
511:         # ── Theme Selection ───────────────────────────────────────────────
512:         styled_text("   THEME SELECTION", HEADER, parent=container)
513:         preset_names = [p["name"] for p in BUILTIN_PRESETS]
514:         current_selection = preset_names[0]
515:         for p in BUILTIN_PRESETS:
516:             if p["settings"].get("panel_bg") == self.settings.get("panel_bg"):
517:                 current_selection = p["name"]
518:                 break
519: 
520:         def _on_theme_change(s, a):
521:             choice = dpg.get_value(s)
522:             preset = next((p for p in BUILTIN_PRESETS if p["name"] == choice), None)
523:             if not preset:
524:                 return
525:             self.settings.update(preset["settings"])
526:             self.save_settings()
527:             self.apply_theme()
528:             self._build_settings_tab()
529: 
530:         _theme_combo = dpg.add_combo(items=preset_names, default_value=current_selection,
531:                       parent=container, width=-1, callback=_on_theme_change)
532:         with dpg.theme() as _center_theme:
533:             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvCombo):
534:                 dpg.add_theme_style(dpg.mvStyleVar_ButtonTextAlign, 0.5, 0.5)
535:         dpg.bind_item_theme(_theme_combo, _center_theme)
536:         dpg.add_separator(parent=container)
537:         dpg.add_button(
538:             label="Reset to Defaults", parent=container, width=-1,
539:             callback=lambda: self._reset_to_defaults(),
540:         )
541: 
542: 
543:     def _reset_to_defaults(self):
544:         self._applied_settings = dict(self.settings)
545:         self.settings = dict(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)
546:         self.save_settings()
547:         self.apply_theme()
548:         self._build_settings_tab()

 40:             return
 41:         for idx, dj in enumerate(app.saved_djs):

 74: def _show_suggestions(slot: SlotState, items: list):
 75:     sid = slot._id
 76:     suggest_tag = f"slot_suggest_{sid}"
 77:     list_tag = f"slot_suggest_list_{sid}"
 78:     name_tag = f"slot_name_{sid}"
 79:     num = min(5, len(items))
 80:     # Align suggestion list with the name input
 81:     if dpg.does_item_exist(name_tag) and dpg.does_item_exist(suggest_tag):
 82:         name_pos = dpg.get_item_pos(name_tag)
 83:         row_pos = dpg.get_item_pos(slot.row_tag) if slot.row_tag and dpg.does_item_exist(slot.row_tag) else [0, 0]
 84:         indent = max(0, name_pos[0] - row_pos[0])
 85:         dpg.configure_item(suggest_tag, indent=int(indent))
 86:     if dpg.does_item_exist(list_tag):
 87:         w = dpg.get_item_width(name_tag) if dpg.does_item_exist(name_tag) else 175
 88:         dpg.configure_item(list_tag, items=items, num_items=num, width=w)
 89:     if dpg.does_item_exist(suggest_tag):
 90:         dpg.show_item(suggest_tag)

<file path="dev.py">
 1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
 2: """
 3: Development script that monitors code changes and auto-restarts the app.
 4: """
 5: 
 6: import subprocess
 7: import sys
 8: import time
 9: from pathlib import Path
10: 
11: from watchdog.events import FileSystemEventHandler
12: from watchdog.observers import Observer
13: 
14: 
15: class RestartHandler(FileSystemEventHandler):
16:     def __init__(self, script_path):
17:         self.script_path = script_path
18:         self.process = None
19:         self.start_process()
20: 
21:     def start_process(self):
22:         if self.process:
23:             self.process.terminate()
24:             self.process.wait()
25:         print("Starting app...")
26:         self.process = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, self.script_path])
27: 
28:     def on_modified(self, event):
29:         if str(event.src_path).endswith('.py'):
30:             print(f"Detected change in {event.src_path}, restarting...")
31:             self.start_process()
32: 
33: 
34: def main():
35:     desktop_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "desktop"
36:     script_path = desktop_dir / "main.py"
37:     if not script_path.exists():
38:         print(f"Error: {script_path} not found")
39:         sys.exit(1)
40: 
41:     # Monitor the desktop/src directory
42:     watch_path = desktop_dir / "src"
43:     if not watch_path.exists():
44:         print(f"Error: {watch_path} not found")
45:         sys.exit(1)
46: 
47:     event_handler = RestartHandler(str(script_path))
48:     observer = Observer()
49:     observer.schedule(event_handler, str(watch_path), recursive=True)
50:     observer.start()
51: 
52:     print(f"Watching {watch_path} for changes...")
53: 
54:     try:
55:         while True:
56:             time.sleep(1)
57:     except KeyboardInterrupt:
58:         print("Stopping...")
59:         observer.stop()
60:         if event_handler.process:
61:             event_handler.process.terminate()
62:             event_handler.process.wait()
63:     observer.join()
64: 
65: 
66: if __name__ == "__main__":
67:     main()
</file>

 37: 
 38: class App(

 50:     ImportMixin,
 51: ):
 52:     @staticmethod
 53:     def _data_path(filename: str) -> str:

208:     def _save_window_state(self):
209:         try:
210:             state = {
211:                 "pos":          list(dpg.get_viewport_pos()),
212:                 "width":        dpg.get_viewport_width(),
213:                 "height":       dpg.get_viewport_height(),
214:                 "slots_height": dpg.get_item_height("slots_scroll") if dpg.does_item_exist("slots_scroll") else 320,
215:                 "tabs_height":  dpg.get_item_height("right_tabs_content") if dpg.does_item_exist("right_tabs_content") else 360,
216:             }
217:             with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
218:                 import json
219:                 json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
220:         except Exception as e:
221:             print(f"[app] _save_window_state error: {e}")
222: 
223:     def _restore_window_state(self):
224:         try:
225:             import json
226:             if not os.path.exists(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE):
227:                 return
228:             with open(self.WINDOW_STATE_FILE) as f:
229:                 state = json.load(f)
230:             if "pos" in state:
231:                 dpg.set_viewport_pos(state["pos"])
232:             if "width" in state and "height" in state:
233:                 dpg.set_viewport_width(state["width"])
234:                 dpg.set_viewport_height(state["height"])
235:             if "slots_height" in state and dpg.does_item_exist("slots_scroll"):
236:                 dpg.configure_item("slots_scroll", height=int(state["slots_height"]))
237:             if "tabs_height" in state and dpg.does_item_exist("right_tabs_content"):
238:                 h = int(state["tabs_height"])
239:                 dpg.configure_item("right_tabs_content", height=h)
240:                 self._base_tabs_height = h
241:                 self._base_vp_height = dpg.get_viewport_height()
242:         except Exception as e:
243:             print(f"[app] _restore_window_state error: {e}")
244: 
245:     def _on_close(self):
246:         self._save_window_state()
247:         # Cancel any pending debounce timers then flush a final library save
248:         for attr in ("_update_job", "_roster_job", "_save_lib_job",
249:                      "_auto_save_job", "_auto_event_save_job"):
250:             self._cancel(attr)
251:         self._save_library()

132:             def _parse(s, a, _pi=paste_input, _st=status_text, _rg=results_group,

140:                 if not entries:

142:                 dpg.set_value(_st, f"{len(entries)} link(s) detected.")
143:                 for det_name, url in entries:

145:                     with dpg.group(parent=_rg, horizontal=True):
146:                         styled_text(short_url[:45], MUTED)

150:             def _import(s, a, _rd=result_data, _st=status_text, _wt=win_tag):
151:                 if not _rd:
152:                     dpg.set_value(_st, "Parse first."); return
153:                 for row in _rd:
154:                     name = dpg.get_value(row["name_input"]).strip()
155:                     url = row["url"]
156:                     if not name:
157:                         continue
158:                     existing = next((d for d in self.saved_djs
159:                                      if d.get("name", "").lower() == name.lower()), None)
160:                     if existing:
161:                         existing["stream"] = url
162:                     else:
163:                         self.saved_djs.append({"name": name, "stream": url, "exact_link": False})
164:                 self._save_library()

166:                 self._work_queue.put(self._refresh_slot_combos)

172:                 dpg.add_button(label="Parse", width=140, callback=_parse)
173:                 import_btn = dpg.add_button(label="Import", width=140, callback=_import)
174:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(import_btn, "primary_btn_theme")
175: 
176:     @staticmethod
177:     def _parse_dj_links(text: str) -> list:
178:         import re as _re
179:         url_re = _re.compile(r'https?://[^\s<>"]+', _re.IGNORECASE)
180:         results = []
181:         for line in text.splitlines():
182:             s = line.strip()
183:             if not s:
184:                 continue
185:             s_clean = _re.sub(r'<(https?://[^>]+)>', r'\1', s)
186:             urls_in_line = url_re.findall(s_clean)
187:             if not urls_in_line:
188:                 continue
189:             url = urls_in_line[0]
190:             name_fragment = url_re.sub("", s_clean).strip().rstrip(":—-– ").strip()
191:             name_fragment = _re.sub(r'\*\*(.+?)\*\*', r'\1', name_fragment)
192:             name_fragment = _re.sub(r'\*([^*]+?)\*', r'\1', name_fragment)
193:             name_fragment = _re.sub(r'^[:\-–—]+\s*', '', name_fragment).strip()
194:             name_fragment = _re.sub(r'\s*[:\-–—]+$', '', name_fragment).strip()
195:             if name_fragment:
196:                 det_name = name_fragment
197:             else:
198:                 path_parts = [p for p in url.rstrip('/').split('/') if p]
199:                 raw_slug = path_parts[-1] if path_parts else ""
200:                 raw_slug = raw_slug.split('?')[0]
201:                 det_name = raw_slug.replace('_', ' ').replace('-', ' ').title()
202:             results.append((det_name, url))
203:         return results
204: 
205:     def _delete_dj_from_roster(self, idx):
206:         if idx < len(self.saved_djs):
207:             win_tag = f"confirm_del_dj_{idx}"
208:             if dpg.does_item_exist(win_tag):
209:                 dpg.delete_item(win_tag)
210:             name = self.saved_djs[idx].get("name", "this DJ")
211:             with dpg.window(tag=win_tag, label="Confirm Delete", modal=True,
212:                             autosize=True, no_resize=True, no_scrollbar=True,
213:                             pos=popup_pos()):
214:                 dpg.add_text(f"Remove '{name}' from the roster?")
215:                 with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
216:                     def _confirm(s, a, u):
217:                         i, wt = u
218:                         self.saved_djs.pop(i)
219:                         self._save_library()
220:                         self.refresh_dj_roster_ui()
221:                         self._work_queue.put(self._refresh_slot_combos)
222:                         self._refresh_all_slot_info()
223:                         self._schedule_update()
224:                         dpg.delete_item(wt)
225:                     dpg.add_button(label="Yes", width=140, user_data=(idx, win_tag), callback=_confirm)
226:                     dpg.bind_item_theme(dpg.last_item(), self._danger_btn_theme)
227:                     dpg.add_button(label="No", width=140, user_data=win_tag,
228:                                    callback=lambda s, a, u: dpg.delete_item(u))
229:

   1: """
   2: Module: ui_builder.py
   3: Purpose: Builds the entire application UI layout (Dear PyGui).
   4: Architecture: Mixin for App class.
   5: """
   6: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
   7: import io
   8: import logging
   9: import os as _os
  10: import secrets

  26: class UISetupMixin:
  27:     """Builds the entire application UI and provides helpers."""
  28: 
  29:     _LEFT_MIN = 300
  30:     _LEFT_MAX = 350
  31:     _LEFT_DEFAULT = 325
  32: 
  33:     def setup_ui(self):
  34:         with dpg.window(tag="primary_window", no_title_bar=True, no_resize=True,
  35:                         no_move=True, no_scrollbar=True,
  36:                         no_scroll_with_mouse=True):
  37:             with dpg.table(header_row=False, resizable=False,
  38:                            scrollX=False, scrollY=False,
  39:                            policy=dpg.mvTable_SizingFixedFit):
  40:                 dpg.add_table_column(tag="left_panel_column",
  41:                                      init_width_or_weight=self._LEFT_DEFAULT,
  42:                                      width_fixed=True)
  43:                 dpg.add_table_column(init_width_or_weight=1, width_fixed=True)
  44:                 dpg.add_table_column(width_stretch=True)
  45:                 with dpg.table_row():
  46:                     with dpg.child_window(tag="left_panel", border=False,
  47:                                           height=-1,
  48:                                           no_scrollbar=True,
  49:                                           no_scroll_with_mouse=True):
  50:                         self._build_left_panel()
  51:                     with dpg.child_window(tag="panel_divider", border=False,
  52:                                           height=-1,
  53:                                           no_scrollbar=True,
  54:                                           no_scroll_with_mouse=True, width=1):
  55:                         with dpg.theme() as _div_theme:
  56:                             with dpg.theme_component(dpg.mvChildWindow):
  57:                                 dpg.add_theme_color(dpg.mvThemeCol_ChildBg, T.DPG_BORDER)
  58:                         dpg.bind_item_theme("panel_divider", _div_theme)
  59:                     with dpg.child_window(tag="right_panel", border=False,
  60:                                           height=-1,
  61:                                           no_scrollbar=True,
  62:                                           no_scroll_with_mouse=True):
  63:                         self._build_right_panel()
  64: 
  65:         dpg.set_primary_window("primary_window", True)
  66:         self._build_settings_tab()
  67:         self.apply_theme()
  68:         self._setup_wheel_handler()
  69:         self._apply_section_order()
  70: 
  71:         # Track base dimensions for proportional resize of right_tabs_content
  72:         self._base_vp_height = dpg.get_viewport_height()
  73:         self._base_tabs_height = 360
  74:         dpg.set_viewport_resize_callback(self._on_viewport_resize)
  75: 
  76:     # ── Left panel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  77: 
  78:     _DRAWER_HEIGHT = 130
  79:     _AUTH_BTN_HEIGHT = 50
  80:     _AUTH_BTN_INNER  = 44

  82:     def _build_left_panel(self):
  83:         self._account_drawer_open = False
  84: 
  85:         with dpg.child_window(tag="left_tabs_wrapper", border=False,
  86:                               autosize_x=True, height=-1,
  87:                               no_scrollbar=True, no_scroll_with_mouse=True):
  88:             with dpg.tab_bar(tag="left_tabs"):
  89:                 with dpg.tab(label="Event", tag="Event"):
  90:                     self._build_event_tab()
  91:                 with dpg.tab(label="Roster", tag="Roster"):
  92:                     self._build_dj_roster_tab()
  93:                 with dpg.tab(label="Settings", tag="Settings"):
  94:                     with dpg.child_window(tag="settings_scroll", height=-1,
  95:                                           border=False, autosize_x=True):
  96:                         pass  # populated by _build_settings_tab()
  97: 
  98:         # ── Avatar texture ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  99:         with dpg.texture_registry():
 100:             dpg.add_static_texture(1, 1, [0, 0, 0, 0], tag="auth_avatar_tex")
 101: 
 102:         # ── Account drawer (hidden by default) ───────────────────────────
 103:         with dpg.child_window(tag="account_drawer", height=self._DRAWER_HEIGHT,
 104:                               border=True, autosize_x=True, no_scrollbar=True,
 105:                               show=False):
 106:             self._build_account_drawer()
 107: 
 108:         # ── Auth card toggle button ───────────────────────────────────
 109:         _btn_h = self._AUTH_BTN_INNER
 110:         _av_sz = 24
 111:         dpg.add_button(tag="auth_card_btn", label="      Local", width=-1,
 112:                        height=_btn_h,
 113:                        callback=lambda: self._toggle_account_drawer())
 114:         dpg.add_image("auth_avatar_tex", tag="auth_card_avatar",
 115:                       width=_av_sz, height=_av_sz, show=False)
 116: 
 117:     def _apply_local_mode_visibility(self):
 118:         """Hide server-dependent tabs/sections when running in local mode."""
 119:         is_local = getattr(self, "_local_mode", False)
 120:         for tag in ("DJ", "DiscordTab", "sect_club_vrchat", "sect_roster_booked"):
 121:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 122:                 dpg.configure_item(tag, show=not is_local)
 123:         self._update_auth_card()
 124: 
 125:     def _update_auth_card(self):
 126:         """Refresh the auth card button label to reflect sign-in state."""
 127:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("auth_card_btn"):
 128:             return
 129:         _pad = "      "  # space for avatar overlay
 130:         if self._oauth.is_signed_in:
 131:             user = self._oauth.user_info or {}
 132:             name = user.get("username", "Unknown")
 133:             dpg.configure_item("auth_card_btn", label=f"{_pad}{name}")
 134:         else:
 135:             dpg.configure_item("auth_card_btn", label=f"{_pad}Local")
 136:         self._position_auth_avatar()
 137: 
 138:     def _position_auth_avatar(self):
 139:         """Place the avatar image on top of the auth card button."""
 140:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("auth_card_btn") or not dpg.does_item_exist("auth_card_avatar"):
 141:             return
 142:         btn_pos = dpg.get_item_pos("auth_card_btn")
 143:         btn_h = dpg.get_item_height("auth_card_btn")
 144:         av_sz = 24
 145:         x = btn_pos[0] + 8
 146:         y = btn_pos[1] + max(0, (btn_h - av_sz) // 2)
 147:         dpg.configure_item("auth_card_avatar", pos=[x, y], show=True)
 148: 
 149:     def _load_discord_avatar(self, user: dict):
 150:         """Download and display the user's Discord avatar in the auth card."""
 151:         user_id = user.get("id", "")
 152:         avatar_hash = user.get("avatar", "")
 153:         if not user_id or not avatar_hash:
 154:             return
 155: 
 156:         url = f"https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/{user_id}/{avatar_hash}.png?size=32"
 157: 
 158:         def _fetch():
 159:             try:
 160:                 import urllib.request
 161:                 import io
 162:                 from PIL import Image
 163: 
 164:                 req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "LineupBuilder/1.2"})
 165:                 with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
 166:                     data = resp.read()
 167:                 img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)).convert("RGBA").resize((32, 32))
 168:                 # Normalise to 0–1 floats for DPG
 169:                 pixels = [v / 255.0 for v in img.tobytes()]
 170: 
 171:                 def _apply():
 172:                     if dpg.does_item_exist("auth_avatar_tex"):
 173:                         dpg.delete_item("auth_avatar_tex")
 174:                     if dpg.does_alias_exist("auth_avatar_tex"):
 175:                         dpg.remove_alias("auth_avatar_tex")
 176:                     with dpg.texture_registry():
 177:                         dpg.add_static_texture(32, 32, pixels, tag="auth_avatar_tex")
 178:                     if dpg.does_item_exist("auth_avatar_img"):
 179:                         dpg.configure_item("auth_avatar_img", texture_tag="auth_avatar_tex", show=True)
 180:                     if dpg.does_item_exist("auth_card_avatar"):
 181:                         dpg.configure_item("auth_card_avatar", texture_tag="auth_avatar_tex")
 182:                 self._work_queue.put(_apply)
 183:             except Exception as exc:
 184:                 log.debug("Failed to load Discord avatar: %s", exc)
 185: 
 186:         import threading
 187:         threading.Thread(target=_fetch, daemon=True).start()
 188: 
 189:     def _build_account_drawer(self):
 190:         """Build the account drawer contents (created once, shown/hidden)."""
 191:         dpg.add_spacer(height=4)
 192: 
 193:         # Avatar + status row
 194:         with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
 195:             dpg.add_image(
 196:                 "auth_avatar_tex", tag="account_avatar_img",
 197:                 width=32, height=32, show=False,
 198:             )
 199:             styled_text("  Not signed in", MUTED, tag="account_status_text")
 200:         dpg.add_spacer(height=6)
 201: 
 202:         # Sign-in button
 203:         add_primary_button(
 204:             "Sign in with Discord",
 205:             tag="account_signin_btn",
 206:             width=-1,
 207:             callback=lambda: self._sign_in_from_app(),
 208:         )
 209: 
 210:         # Sign-out button (hidden when not signed in)
 211:         from .widgets import add_danger_button
 212:         add_danger_button(
 213:             "Sign Out",
 214:             tag="account_signout_btn",
 215:             width=-1,
 216:             callback=lambda: self._sign_out_from_drawer(),
 217:         )
 218: 
 219:         # Local-mode toggle
 220:         dpg.add_checkbox(
 221:             tag="account_local_mode_cb",
 222:             label="Local Mode",
 223:             default_value=getattr(self, "_local_mode", False),
 224:             callback=lambda s, a: self._toggle_local_mode_from_drawer(a),
 225:         )
 226:         dpg.add_spacer(height=2)
 227:         styled_text("", ERROR, tag="account_error_label")
 228: 
 229:     def _toggle_account_drawer(self):
 230:         """Toggle the account drawer open/closed."""
 231:         self._account_drawer_open = not self._account_drawer_open
 232:         show = self._account_drawer_open
 233:         dpg.configure_item("account_drawer", show=show)
 234:         # Shrink tabs wrapper to make room for the drawer
 235:         offset = self._AUTH_BTN_HEIGHT + (self._DRAWER_HEIGHT if show else 0)
 236:         dpg.configure_item("left_tabs_wrapper", height=-offset)
 237:         if show:
 238:             self._refresh_account_drawer()
 239:         # Reposition avatar overlay after layout shift
 240:         self._work_queue.put(self._position_auth_avatar)
 241: 
 242:     def _sign_out_from_drawer(self):
 243:         """Sign out and update the drawer + auth card."""
 244:         self._local_mode = True
 245:         self._oauth.sign_out()
 246:         self.discord_oauth = {}
 247:         self.save_settings()
 248:         self._apply_local_mode_visibility()
 249:         self._refresh_account_drawer()
 250: 
 251:     def _toggle_local_mode_from_drawer(self, value):
 252:         """Toggle local mode from the drawer checkbox."""
 253:         self._local_mode = bool(value)
 254:         self._apply_local_mode_visibility()
 255:         self._refresh_account_drawer()
 256: 
 257:     def _refresh_account_drawer(self):
 258:         """Update the account drawer to reflect current sign-in status."""
 259:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("account_status_text"):
 260:             return
 261:         signed_in = self._oauth.is_signed_in
 262:         if signed_in:
 263:             user = self._oauth.user_info or {}
 264:             name = user.get("username", "Unknown")
 265:             dpg.set_value("account_status_text", f"  Signed in as {name}")
 266:             dpg.configure_item("account_signin_btn", show=False)
 267:             dpg.configure_item("account_signout_btn", show=True)
 268:             dpg.configure_item("account_local_mode_cb", show=False)
 269:             dpg.set_value("account_error_label", "")
 270:             if dpg.does_item_exist("account_avatar_img"):
 271:                 dpg.configure_item("account_avatar_img", show=True)
 272:             self._load_discord_avatar(user)
 273:         else:
 274:             dpg.set_value("account_status_text", "  Not signed in")
 275:             dpg.configure_item("account_signin_btn", show=True)
 276:             dpg.configure_item("account_signout_btn", show=False)
 277:             dpg.configure_item("account_local_mode_cb", show=True)
 278:             if dpg.does_item_exist("account_avatar_img"):
 279:                 dpg.configure_item("account_avatar_img", show=False)
 280:         self._update_auth_card()

 500:                             _img_label = _img_label[:25] + "..."
 501:                         add_primary_button(
 502:                             _img_label, tag="embed_image_browse_btn", width=-1,
 503:                             callback=lambda: self._browse_embed_image(),
 504:                         )

 506:                             tag="embed_image_clear_btn", label="X", width=35,
 507:                             callback=lambda: self._clear_embed_image(),
 508:                         )
 509: 
 510:                 dpg.add_spacer(height=4)

 514:                     "Post to Discord", tag="discord_post_btn", width=-1,

 639:     def _build_visual_preview(self):
 640:         """Build a DPG mockup of a Discord embed."""
 641:         # Discord dark theme background: #2f3136 (roughly)

 649:             dpg.bind_item_theme(dpg.last_item(), "discord_preview_theme")
 650:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True):
 651:                 # Accent color line on the left
 652:                 dpg.add_button(label="", width=4, height=100, enabled=False)
 653:                 dpg.bind_item_theme(dpg.last_item(), "primary_btn_theme")
 654:

 656:                     styled_text("Event Title Placeholder", HEADER, tag="preview_title")
 657:                     styled_text("Event details and lineup description will appear here as you type in the Details section.", BODY, tag="preview_desc", wrap=-10)
 658:

 660:                     with dpg.group(tag="preview_image_group", show=False):
 661:                         dpg.add_spacer(height=8)
 662:                         with dpg.child_window(width=250, height=150, border=True):
 663:                             styled_text("      IMAGE PREVIEW", MUTED)
 664:                             styled_text("   (Requires upload)", HINT)
 665:

 667:         styled_text("  * Preview reflects current Discord format text & image.", MUTED)

 669:     def _on_output_tab_changed(self, s, a):
 670:         if a == "output_tab_visual":
 671:             self._update_visual_preview()

 673:     def _update_visual_preview(self):
 674:         """Refresh the items in the visual preview mockup."""
 675:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("preview_title"):
 676:             return
 677:             
 678:         title = self.event_title_var.get().strip() or "UNTITLED EVENT"
 679:         vol = self.event_vol_var.get().strip()
 680:         if vol:
 681:             title = f"{title} [{vol}]"
 682:         
 683:         dpg.set_value("preview_title", title)
 684:         
 685:         # Get the current discord-formatted text
 686:         desc = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
 687:         dpg.set_value("preview_desc", desc)
 688:         
 689:         # Image
 690:         img_path = getattr(self, "discord_embed_image", "").strip()
 691:         if img_path and _os.path.exists(img_path):
 692:             dpg.configure_item("preview_image_group", show=True)
 693:             # We would need to load the image into DPG texture registry here
 694:             # For brevity, let's just show/hide the group for now
 695:         else:
 696:             dpg.configure_item("preview_image_group", show=False)

 700:     def _save_discord_credentials(self):
 701:         """Persist client ID from the input field."""
 702:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_client_id"):
 703:             self.discord_client_id = dpg.get_value("discord_client_id").strip()
 704:         self.save_settings()
 705: 
 706:     def _save_discord_ping_roles(self):
 707:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_ping_roles"):
 708:             display = dpg.get_value("discord_ping_roles")
 709:             role_map = getattr(self, "_discord_role_map", {})
 710:             self.discord_ping_roles = str(role_map.get(display, ""))
 711:         self.save_settings()
 712: 
 713:     def _invite_discord_bot(self):
 714:         """Open the bot invite URL in the browser."""
 715:         client_id = getattr(self, "discord_client_id", "")
 716:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_client_id"):
 717:             client_id = dpg.get_value("discord_client_id").strip()
 718:         if not client_id:
 719:             self._set_discord_status("No Client ID provided.")
 720:             return
 721:         invite_url = (
 722:             f"https://discord.com/oauth2/authorize"
 723:             f"?client_id={client_id}&permissions=67584&scope=bot"
 724:         )
 725:         import webbrowser
 726:         webbrowser.open(invite_url)
 727: 
 728:     def _clear_embed_image(self):
 729:         """Clear the embed image."""
 730:         self.discord_embed_image = ""
 731:         if dpg.does_item_exist("embed_image_browse_btn"):
 732:             dpg.set_item_label("embed_image_browse_btn", "Select Image...")
 733:             
 734:         if hasattr(self, "_auto_event_save"):
 735:             self._auto_event_save()
 736: 
 737:     def _browse_embed_image(self):
 738:         """Open the native Windows file explorer to pick a local image."""
 739:         import tkinter as _tk
 740:         from tkinter import filedialog as _fd
 741:         root = _tk.Tk()
 742:         root.withdraw()
 743:         root.attributes("-topmost", True)
 744:         path = _fd.askopenfilename(
 745:             parent=root,
 746:             title="Select Embed Image",
 747:             filetypes=[
 748:                 ("Image files", "*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.gif *.webp"),
 749:                 ("All files", "*.*"),
 750:             ],
 751:         )
 752:         root.destroy()
 753:         if path:
 754:             self.discord_embed_image = path
 755:             if dpg.does_item_exist("embed_image_browse_btn"):
 756:                 label = _os.path.basename(path)
 757:                 if len(label) > 32:
 758:                     label = label[:29] + "..."
 759:                 dpg.set_item_label("embed_image_browse_btn", label)
 760:                 
 761:             if hasattr(self, "_auto_event_save"):
 762:                 self._auto_event_save()
 763: 
 764:     def _build_discord_settings_drawer(self):

 770:             hint="Application Client ID...",
 771:             width=-1,
 772:             callback=lambda s, a, u=None: self._save_discord_credentials(),
 773:         )
 774:         dpg.add_spacer(height=2)
 775:         styled_text("  Bot Token", LABEL)
 776:         dpg.add_input_text(
 777:             tag="discord_bot_token",
 778:             default_value=getattr(self, "discord_bot_token", ""),
 779:             hint="Paste bot token here...",
 780:             password=True,
 781:             width=-1,
 782:             callback=lambda s, a, u=None: self._save_bot_token(),
 783:         )
 784:         dpg.add_spacer(height=4)
 785:         add_primary_button(
 786:             "Invite Bot to Server", width=-1,
 787:             callback=lambda: self._invite_discord_bot(),
 788:         )
 789: 
 790:     def _toggle_discord_settings_drawer(self):
 791:         """Toggle the inline Discord settings drawer open/closed."""
 792:         tag = "discord_settings_drawer"
 793:         if not dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 794:             return
 795:         currently_shown = dpg.is_item_shown(tag)
 796:         dpg.configure_item(tag, show=not currently_shown)
 797: 
 798:     def _on_server_selected(self):
 799:         """When server combo changes, save and fetch channels + roles."""
 800:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_ping_server"):
 801:             display = dpg.get_value("discord_ping_server")
 802:             guild_map = getattr(self, "_discord_guild_map", {})
 803:             guild_id = str(guild_map.get(display, ""))
 804:             self.discord_ping_server = guild_id
 805:             self.save_settings()
 806:             if guild_id:
 807:                 self._fetch_discord_roles(guild_id)
 808:                 self._fetch_discord_channels_for_guild(guild_id)
 809:             else:
 810:                 dpg.configure_item("discord_ping_roles", items=["None"])
 811:                 dpg.set_value("discord_ping_roles", "None")
 812:                 self.discord_ping_roles = ""
 813:                 dpg.configure_item("discord_channel", items=[])
 814:                 dpg.set_value("discord_channel", "")
 815:                 self.save_settings()
 816: 
 817:     def _save_discord_channel(self):
 818:         """Read Discord channel combo selection and persist channel ID."""
 819:         channel_map = getattr(self, "_discord_channel_map", {})
 820:         tag = "discord_channel"
 821:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 822:             display = dpg.get_value(tag).strip()
 823:             self.discord_channel_id = str(channel_map.get(display, ""))
 824:         self.save_settings()
 825: 
 826:     def _channel_display(self, channel_id_str: str) -> str:
 827:         """Return the display string for a saved channel ID, or empty."""
 828:         channel_map = getattr(self, "_discord_channel_map", {})
 829:         for display, cid in channel_map.items():
 830:             if str(cid) == channel_id_str:
 831:                 return display
 832:         return ""
 833: 
 834:     def _fetch_discord_channels_for_guild(self, guild_id_str: str):
 835:         """Ask the bot to list text channels for a specific guild."""
 836:         if not self._discord_service.is_running:
 837:             self._set_discord_status("Connect bot first.")
 838:             return
 839:         self._discord_service.get_text_channels(
 840:             on_result=lambda channels: self._queue_on_main(
 841:                 lambda: self._on_channels_fetched(channels, guild_id_str)),
 842:             on_error=lambda e: self._set_discord_status(f"Error: {e}"),
 843:         )
 844: 
 845:     def _on_channels_fetched(self, channels: list[tuple[str, str, int]], guild_id_str: str = ""):
 846:         """Populate the single channel combo with fetched data."""
 847:         items = []
 848:         channel_map: dict[str, int] = {}
 849:         for guild_name, ch_name, ch_id in channels:
 850:             # If we know the guild, filter to only that guild's channels
 851:             if guild_id_str:
 852:                 guild_map = getattr(self, "_discord_guild_map", {})
 853:                 matched = False
 854:                 for g_display, g_id in guild_map.items():
 855:                     if str(g_id) == guild_id_str and g_display == guild_name:
 856:                         matched = True
 857:                         break
 858:                 if not matched:
 859:                     continue
 860:             display = f"#{ch_name}"
 861:             items.append(display)
 862:             channel_map[display] = ch_id
 863: 
 864:         self._discord_channel_map = channel_map
 865: 
 866:         tag = "discord_channel"
 867:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
 868:             dpg.configure_item(tag, items=items)
 869:             saved_id = getattr(self, "discord_channel_id", "")
 870:             current = ""
 871:             for display, cid in channel_map.items():
 872:                 if str(cid) == saved_id:
 873:                     current = display
 874:                     break
 875:             dpg.set_value(tag, current)
 876: 
 877:     def _fetch_discord_guilds(self):
 878:         """Ask the bot to list all visible guilds."""
 879:         if not self._discord_service.is_running:
 880:             return
 881:         self._discord_service.get_guilds(
 882:             on_result=lambda guilds: self._queue_on_main(
 883:                 lambda: self._on_guilds_fetched(guilds)),
 884:             on_error=lambda e: self._set_discord_status(f"Error: {e}"),
 885:         )
 886: 
 887:     def _on_guilds_fetched(self, guilds: list[tuple[str, int]]):
 888:         items = []
 889:         guild_map = {}
 890:         for g_name, g_id in guilds:
 891:             items.append(g_name)
 892:             guild_map[g_name] = g_id
 893:         
 894:         self._discord_guild_map = guild_map
 895:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_ping_server"):
 896:             dpg.configure_item("discord_ping_server", items=items)
 897:             
 898:             saved_id = getattr(self, "discord_ping_server", "")
 899:             current = ""
 900:             for display, gid in guild_map.items():
 901:                 if str(gid) == saved_id:
 902:                     current = display
 903:                     break
 904:             dpg.set_value("discord_ping_server", current)
 905:             if current:
 906:                 self._fetch_discord_roles(str(guild_map[current]))
 907:                 self._fetch_discord_channels_for_guild(str(guild_map[current]))
 908: 
 909:     def _fetch_discord_roles(self, guild_id_str: str):
 910:         if not guild_id_str or not guild_id_str.isdigit():
 911:             return
 912:         if not self._discord_service.is_running:
 913:             return
 914:         guild_id = int(guild_id_str)
 915:         self._discord_service.get_roles(
 916:             guild_id=guild_id,
 917:             on_result=lambda roles: self._queue_on_main(
 918:                 lambda: self._on_roles_fetched(roles)),
 919:             on_error=lambda e: self._set_discord_status(f"Error fetching roles: {e}")
 920:         )
 921: 
 922:     def _on_roles_fetched(self, roles: list[tuple[str, int]]):
 923:         items = ["None"]
 924:         role_map = {"None": ""}
 925:         for r_name, r_id in roles:
 926:             display = f"@{r_name}"
 927:             items.append(display)
 928:             role_map[display] = r_id
 929:             
 930:         self._discord_role_map = role_map
 931:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_ping_roles"):
 932:             dpg.configure_item("discord_ping_roles", items=items)
 933:             
 934:             saved_id = getattr(self, "discord_ping_roles", "")
 935:             current = "None"
 936:             for display, rid in role_map.items():
 937:                 if str(rid) == saved_id:
 938:                     current = display
 939:                     break
 940:             dpg.set_value("discord_ping_roles", current)
 941: 
 942:     def _save_bot_token(self):
 943:         """Persist the bot token from the input field."""
 944:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_bot_token"):
 945:             self.discord_bot_token = dpg.get_value("discord_bot_token").strip()
 946:             self.save_settings()
 947: 
 948:     def _set_discord_status(self, text: str):
 949:         """Update the Discord status label (thread-safe via work queue)."""
 950:         def _update():
 951:             if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_status_text"):
 952:                 dpg.set_value("discord_status_text", f"  {text}")
 953:             # Auto-fetch guilds when connected
 954:             if "connected" in text.lower() or "ready" in text.lower():
 955:                 self._fetch_discord_guilds()
 956:                 show_toast(text, severity="success", duration=3.0)
 957:             elif "error" in text.lower() or "failed" in text.lower():
 958:                 show_toast(text, severity="error", duration=5.0)
 959:             elif "posted" in text.lower():
 960:                 show_toast(text, severity="success", duration=4.0)
 961:             elif "disconnect" in text.lower():
 962:                 show_toast(text, severity="warning", duration=3.0)
 963:         self._queue_on_main(_update)
 964: 
 965:     def _connect_discord_bot(self):
 966:         """Start the Discord bot with the saved token."""
 967:         token = getattr(self, "discord_bot_token", "")
 968:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_bot_token"):
 969:             token = dpg.get_value("discord_bot_token").strip()
 970:             self.discord_bot_token = token
 971:             self.save_settings()
 972:         if not token:
 973:             self._set_discord_status("No bot token provided.")
 974:             return
 975:         self._set_discord_status("Connecting...")
 976:         self._discord_service.start(token, on_status=self._set_discord_status)
 977: 
 978:     def _disconnect_discord_bot(self):
 979:         """Stop the Discord bot."""
 980:         self._discord_service.stop()
 981:         self._set_discord_status("Disconnected")
 982: 
 983:     def _confirm_post_to_discord(self):
 984:         """Show confirmation before posting."""
 985:         ch_display = ""
 986:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_channel"):
 987:             ch_display = dpg.get_value("discord_channel") or "selected channel"
 988:         confirm(
 989:             f"Post lineup to {ch_display}?",
 990:             on_confirm=self._post_to_discord,
 991:             title="Post to Discord",
 992:             confirm_label="Post",
 993:         )
 994: 
 995:     def _post_to_discord(self):
 996:         """Post the current lineup as a Discord embed to the selected channel."""
 997:         import datetime as _dt
 998: 
 999:         import discord
1000: 
1001:         if not self._discord_service.is_running:
1002:             self._set_discord_status("Bot is not connected.")
1003:             return
1004: 
1005:         channel_id_str = getattr(self, "discord_channel_id", "").strip()
1006:         if not channel_id_str:
1007:             self._set_discord_status("No channel selected.")
1008:             return
1009:         try:
1010:             channel_id = int(channel_id_str)
1011:         except ValueError:
1012:             self._set_discord_status("Invalid channel ID.")
1013:             return
1014: 
1015:         snap = self._build_snapshot()
1016:         if not snap.slots:
1017:             self._set_discord_status("Lineup is empty — nothing to post.")
1018:             return
1019: 
1020:         body_text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
1021:         if not body_text.strip():
1022:             self._set_discord_status("Output is empty.")
1023:             return
1024: 
1025:         image_path = getattr(self, "discord_embed_image", "").strip()
1026: 
1027:         attach_file = None
1028: 
1029:         ping_roles = getattr(self, "discord_ping_roles", "").strip()
1030:         ping_content = " ".join([f"<@&{r.strip()}>" for r in ping_roles.split(",") if r.strip()]) if ping_roles else ""
1031: 
1032:         embed_desc = body_text
1033:         if ping_content:
1034:             embed_desc = f"{body_text}\n\n{ping_content}".strip()
1035: 
1036:         embed = discord.Embed(
1037:             description=embed_desc,
1038:             color=0x4F46E5,
1039:         )
1040: 
1041:         if image_path:
1042:             if image_path.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
1043:                 embed.set_image(url=image_path)
1044:             elif _os.path.isfile(image_path):
1045:                 ext = _os.path.splitext(image_path)[1]
1046:                 safe_name = f"image{ext}"
1047:                 attach_file = discord.File(image_path, filename=safe_name)
1048:                 embed.set_image(url=f"attachment://{safe_name}")
1049: 
1050:         ch_display = ""
1051:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_channel"):
1052:             ch_display = dpg.get_value("discord_channel") or "channel"
1053: 
1054:         self._set_discord_status(f"Posting to {ch_display}...")
1055:         self._discord_service.send_embed(
1056:             channel_id,
1057:             embed=embed,
1058:             content=None,
1059:             file=attach_file,
1060:             on_success=lambda: self._set_discord_status(
1061:                 f"Posted to {ch_display}."),
1062:             on_error=lambda e: self._set_discord_status(f"Error: {e}"),
1063:         )
1064: 
1065:     # ── Scheduled posting ─────────────────────────────────────────────
