1067:     def _open_schedule_picker(self):
1068:         """Open the calendar picker for the schedule datetime field."""
1069:         from .date_time_picker import open_datetime_picker
1070:         from ..types import DPGVar
1071:         var = DPGVar(tag="discord_schedule_datetime")
1072:         open_datetime_picker(var, callback=None)
1073: 
1074:     def _schedule_discord_post(self):
1075:         """Add a scheduled post entry from the UI inputs."""
1076:         raw_dt = ""
1077:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_schedule_datetime"):
1078:             raw_dt = dpg.get_value("discord_schedule_datetime").strip()
1079:         if not raw_dt:
1080:             self._set_discord_status("Enter a date/time to schedule.")
1081:             return
1082:         try:
1083:             post_dt = datetime.strptime(raw_dt, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
1084:         except ValueError:
1085:             self._set_discord_status("Invalid format. Use YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM")
1086:             return
1087:         if post_dt <= datetime.now():
1088:             self._set_discord_status("Schedule time must be in the future.")
1089:             return
1090: 
1091:         channel_key = "events"
1092:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_schedule_channel"):
1093:             channel_key = dpg.get_value("discord_schedule_channel")
1094: 
1095:         # Snapshot the current lineup state
1096:         snap = self._build_snapshot()
1097:         if not snap.slots:
1098:             self._set_discord_status("Lineup is empty — nothing to schedule.")
1099:             return
1100: 
1101:         # Store as a pending entry
1102:         if not hasattr(self, "_discord_scheduled_posts"):
1103:             self._discord_scheduled_posts = []
1104: 
1105:         body_text = dpg.get_value("output_text") if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text") else ""
1106:         ping_roles = getattr(self, "discord_ping_roles", "").strip()
1107:         ping_str = " ".join(f"<@&{r.strip()}>" for r in ping_roles.split(",") if r.strip())
1108:         content_text = body_text
1109: 
1110:         entry = {
1111:             "datetime": raw_dt,
1112:             "channel": channel_key,
1113:             "content": content_text,
1114:             "ping": ping_str,
1115:             "snapshot": snap,
1116:             "image": getattr(self, "discord_embed_image", ""),
1117:         }
1118:         self._discord_scheduled_posts.append(entry)
1119:         self._save_scheduled_posts()
1120:         self._refresh_schedule_list_ui()
1121:         self._set_discord_status(f"Scheduled for {raw_dt} → {channel_key}")
1122: 
1123:         # Clear the datetime input
1124:         if dpg.does_item_exist("discord_schedule_datetime"):
1125:             dpg.set_value("discord_schedule_datetime", "")
1126: 
1127:     def _cancel_scheduled_post(self, idx: int):
1128:         """Remove a scheduled post by index."""
1129:         posts = getattr(self, "_discord_scheduled_posts", [])
1130:         if 0 <= idx < len(posts):
1131:             posts.pop(idx)
1132:             self._save_scheduled_posts()
1133:             self._refresh_schedule_list_ui()
1134: 
1135:     def _open_pending_popup(self):
1136:         """Open a popup window showing pending scheduled posts."""
1137:         tag = "pending_posts_win"
1138:         if dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
1139:             dpg.delete_item(tag)
1140:         with dpg.window(
1141:             tag=tag, label="Pending Scheduled Posts",
1142:             modal=True, autosize=True, no_resize=True,
1143:             no_scrollbar=True, min_size=(320, 100),
1144:             pos=popup_pos("discord_pending_btn", width=320, height=200),
1145:         ):
1146:             dpg.add_group(tag="discord_scheduled_list")
1147:             self._refresh_schedule_list_ui()
1148:             dpg.add_spacer(height=4)
1149:             dpg.add_button(label="Close", width=-1,
1150:                            callback=lambda: dpg.delete_item(tag))
1151: 
1152:     def _refresh_schedule_list_ui(self):
1153:         """Rebuild the pending-posts list in the popup."""
1154:         tag = "discord_scheduled_list"
1155:         if not dpg.does_item_exist(tag):
1156:             return
1157:         dpg.delete_item(tag, children_only=True)
1158: 
1159:         posts = getattr(self, "_discord_scheduled_posts", [])
1160:         if not posts:
1161:             styled_text("  No scheduled posts", MUTED, parent=tag)
1162:             return
1163: 
1164:         for i, entry in enumerate(posts):
1165:             dt_str = entry.get("datetime", "?")
1166:             ch = entry.get("channel", "?")
1167:             with dpg.group(horizontal=True, parent=tag):
1168:                 styled_text(f"  {dt_str} → {ch}", LABEL)
1169:                 idx = i  # capture for closure
1170:                 dpg.add_button(
1171:                     label=Icon.CLOSE, width=22, height=18,
1172:                     callback=lambda s, a, u=idx: self._cancel_scheduled_post(u),
1173:                 )
1174:                 bind_icon_font(dpg.last_item())
1175: 
1176:     def check_scheduled_posts(self):
1177:         """Called every frame via process_queue to fire due posts.
1178: 
1179:         Only checks once per second to avoid excess overhead.
1180:         """
1181:         now = datetime.now()
1182:         last = getattr(self, "_last_schedule_check", None)
1183:         if last and (now - last).total_seconds() < 1.0:
1184:             return
1185:         self._last_schedule_check = now
1186: 
1187:         posts = getattr(self, "_discord_scheduled_posts", [])
1188:         if not posts:
1189:             return
1190: 
1191:         fired = []
1192:         for i, entry in enumerate(posts):
1193:             try:
1194:                 post_dt = datetime.strptime(entry["datetime"], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
1195:             except (ValueError, KeyError):
1196:                 fired.append(i)
1197:                 continue
1198:             if now >= post_dt:
1199:                 self._fire_scheduled_post(entry)
1200:                 fired.append(i)
1201: 
1202:         if fired:
1203:             for idx in reversed(fired):
1204:                 posts.pop(idx)
1205:             self._save_scheduled_posts()
1206:             self._refresh_schedule_list_ui()
1207: 
1208:     def _fire_scheduled_post(self, entry: dict):
1209:         """Execute a scheduled post using its stored snapshot."""
1210:         import datetime as _dt
1211:         import os
1212: 
1213:         import discord
1214: 
1215:         channel_key = entry.get("channel", "events")
1216:         if not self._discord_service.is_running:
1217:             self._set_discord_status(f"Missed schedule ({channel_key}): bot not connected.")
1218:             return
1219: 
1220:         channel_id_str = self.discord_channels.get(channel_key, "").strip()
1221:         if not channel_id_str:
1222:             self._set_discord_status(f"Missed schedule: no channel for '{channel_key}'.")
1223:             return
1224:         try:
1225:             channel_id = int(channel_id_str)
1226:         except ValueError:
1227:             self._set_discord_status(f"Missed schedule: invalid channel for '{channel_key}'.")
1228:             return
1229: 
1230:         snap = entry.get("snapshot")
1231:         if snap is None or not snap.slots:
1232:             return
1233: 
1234:         start = snap.start_datetime
1235:         unix = int(start.timestamp())
1236: 
1237:         embed = discord.Embed(
1238:             title=snap.full_title or "Lineup",
1239:             description=f"<t:{unix}:F> (<t:{unix}:R>)",
1240:             color=0x5865F2,
1241:             timestamp=_dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(unix, tz=_dt.timezone.utc),
1242:         )
1243: 
1244:         if snap.genres:
1245:             embed.add_field(name="Genres", value=" // ".join(snap.genres), inline=False)
1246: 
1247:         ptr = start
1248:         lineup_lines: list[str] = []
1249:         for slot in snap.slots:
1250:             name = slot.name or "TBA"
1251:             if snap.names_only:
1252:                 lineup_lines.append(f"**{name}**")
1253:             else:
1254:                 ts = int(ptr.timestamp())
1255:                 genre_str = f"  •  {slot.genre}" if slot.genre else ""
1256:                 lineup_lines.append(f"<t:{ts}:t>  **{name}**{genre_str}")
1257:             ptr += _dt.timedelta(minutes=slot.duration)
1258: 
1259:         lineup_text = "\n".join(lineup_lines)
1260:         chunks = [lineup_text[i : i + 1024] for i in range(0, len(lineup_text), 1024)]
1261:         for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
1262:             embed.add_field(
1263:                 name="Lineup" if i == 0 else "\u200b",
1264:                 value=chunk, inline=False,
1265:             )
1266: 
1267:         link_order = ["TIMELINE", "VRCPOP", "X", "IG", "DISCORD", "VRC GROUP"]
1268:         if snap.social_links:
1269:             parts = [
1270:                 f"[{label}]({snap.social_links[label]})"
1271:                 for label in link_order
1272:                 if snap.social_links.get(label, "").strip()
1273:             ]
1274:             if parts:
1275:                 embed.add_field(name="Links", value=" | ".join(parts), inline=False)
1276: 
1277:         image_path = entry.get("image", "").strip()
1278:         attach_file = None
1279:         if image_path:
1280:             if image_path.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
1281:                 embed.set_image(url=image_path)
1282:             elif _os.path.isfile(image_path):
1283:                 ext = _os.path.splitext(image_path)[1]
1284:                 safe_name = f"image{ext}"
1285:                 attach_file = discord.File(image_path, filename=safe_name)
1286:                 embed.set_image(url=f"attachment://{safe_name}")
1287: 
1288:         embed.set_footer(text="GitHub | Baebu/lineup_builder")
1289: 
1290:         ping_content = entry.get("ping", "")
1291:         if ping_content:
1292:              # Just inject the ping at the end of the description block as well if we don't have another way
1293:              embed.description = f"{embed.description}\n\n{ping_content}"
1294: 
1295:         self._set_discord_status(f"Sending scheduled post to {channel_key}...")
1296:         self._discord_service.send_embed(
1297:             channel_id, embed,
1298:             content=entry.get("content", ""),
1299:             file=attach_file,
1300:             on_success=lambda: self._set_discord_status(
1301:                 f"Scheduled post sent to {channel_key}."),
1302:             on_error=lambda e: self._set_discord_status(f"Schedule error: {e}"),
1303:         )
1304: 
1305:     def _save_scheduled_posts(self):
1306:         """Persist scheduled posts to settings.json."""
1307:         from ..types import DPGVar  # noqa: F401
1308:         posts = getattr(self, "_discord_scheduled_posts", [])
1309:         serializable = []
1310:         for entry in posts:
1311:             serializable.append({
1312:                 "datetime": entry.get("datetime", ""),
1313:                 "channel": entry.get("channel", ""),
1314:                 "content": entry.get("content", ""),
1315:                 "ping": entry.get("ping", ""),
1316:                 "image": entry.get("image", ""),
1317:                 "snapshot": self._snapshot_to_dict(entry.get("snapshot"))
1318:                             if entry.get("snapshot") else None,
1319:             })
1320:         self.discord_scheduled_posts = serializable
1321:         self.save_settings()
1322: 
1323:     @staticmethod
1324:     def _snapshot_to_dict(snap) -> dict:
1325:         """Serialize an EventSnapshot to a plain dict."""
1326:         if snap is None:
1327:             return {}
1328:         return {
1329:             "title": snap.title,
1330:             "vol": snap.vol,
1331:             "timestamp": snap.timestamp,
1332:             "genres": list(snap.genres),
1333:             "slots": [{"name": s.name, "genre": s.genre, "duration": s.duration}
1334:                       for s in snap.slots],
1335:             "names_only": snap.names_only,
1336:             "output_format": snap.output_format,
1337:             "saved_djs": [{"name": d.name, "stream": d.stream, "exact_link": d.exact_link}
1338:                           for d in snap.saved_djs],
1339:             "social_links": dict(snap.social_links),
1340:         }
1341: 
1342:     @staticmethod
1343:     def _dict_to_snapshot(d: dict):
1344:         """Deserialize a plain dict back into an EventSnapshot."""
1345:         from ...backend.models.types import DJInfo, EventSnapshot, SlotData
1346:         if not d:
1347:             return None
1348:         return EventSnapshot(
1349:             title=d.get("title", ""),
1350:             vol=d.get("vol", ""),
1351:             timestamp=d.get("timestamp", ""),
1352:             genres=d.get("genres", []),
1353:             slots=[SlotData(s.get("name", ""), s.get("genre", ""), s.get("duration", 60))
1354:                    for s in d.get("slots", [])],
1355:             names_only=d.get("names_only", False),
1356:             output_format=d.get("output_format", "discord"),
1357:             saved_djs=[DJInfo(dj.get("name", ""), dj.get("stream", ""), dj.get("exact_link", False))
1358:                        for dj in d.get("saved_djs", [])],
1359:             social_links=d.get("social_links", {}),
1360:         )
1361: 
1362:     def _load_scheduled_posts(self):
1363:         """Restore scheduled posts from settings (called at startup)."""
1364:         self._discord_scheduled_posts = []
1365:         raw = getattr(self, "discord_scheduled_posts", [])
1366:         now = datetime.now()
1367:         for entry in raw:
1368:             try:
1369:                 post_dt = datetime.strptime(entry["datetime"], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
1370:             except (ValueError, KeyError):
1371:                 continue
1372:             if post_dt > now:
1373:                 snap = self._dict_to_snapshot(entry.get("snapshot"))
1374:                 self._discord_scheduled_posts.append({
1375:                     "datetime": entry["datetime"],
1376:                     "channel": entry.get("channel", "events"),
1377:                     "content": entry.get("content", ""),
1378:                     "snapshot": snap,
1379:                 })
1380: 
1381:     def _toggle_stream_links(self, fmt: str):
1382:         """Toggle stream link format — only one active at a time, or none."""
1383:         current = self.stream_link_format.get()
1384:         self.stream_link_format.set("" if current == fmt else fmt)
1385:         self.update_output()
1386: 
1387:     def _toggle_times(self):
1388:         self.names_only.set(not self.names_only.get())
1389:         self.update_output()
1390: 
1391:     def _copy_output(self):
1392:         """Copy the output text to the system clipboard."""
1393:         if dpg.does_item_exist("output_text"):
1394:             text = dpg.get_value("output_text")
1395:             if text:
1396:                 dpg.set_clipboard_text(text)
1397: 
1398:     # ── Scroll-wheel helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────
1399:     # One global handler checks is_item_hovered for every registered item.
1400: 
1401:     def _shift_timestamp(self, delta_mins: int):
1402:         """Shift the event timestamp by *delta_mins* minutes."""
1403:         raw = self.event_timestamp.get()
1404:         try:
1405:             dt = datetime.strptime(raw, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
1406:         except ValueError:
1407:             return
1408:         dt += timedelta(minutes=delta_mins)
1409:         new_str = dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
1410:         self.event_timestamp.set(new_str)
1411:         if dpg.does_item_exist("event_timestamp_input"):
1412:             dpg.set_value("event_timestamp_input", new_str)
1413:         self._schedule_update()
1414: 
1415:     def _setup_wheel_handler(self):
1416:         if not hasattr(self, '_scroll_combos'):
1417:             self._scroll_combos = {}
1418:         if not hasattr(self, '_scroll_ints'):
1419:             self._scroll_ints = {}
1420:         if not dpg.does_item_exist("global_wheel_hr"):
1421:             with dpg.handler_registry(tag="global_wheel_hr"):
1422:                 dpg.add_mouse_wheel_handler(callback=self._on_mouse_wheel)
1423:                 dpg.add_key_press_handler(dpg.mvKey_Up, callback=self._on_arrow_key)
1424:                 dpg.add_key_press_handler(dpg.mvKey_Down, callback=self._on_arrow_key)
1425: 
1426:     def _register_scroll_combo(self, tag: str, items: list, on_change):
1427:         if not hasattr(self, '_scroll_combos'):
1428:             self._scroll_combos = {}
1429:         self._scroll_combos[tag] = (list(items), on_change)
1430: 
1431:     def _register_scroll_int(self, tag: str, min_val: int = 0,
1432:                              max_val: int = 9999, on_change=None):
1433:         if not hasattr(self, '_scroll_ints'):
1434:             self._scroll_ints = {}
1435:         self._scroll_ints[tag] = (min_val, max_val, on_change)
1436: 
1437:     def _on_arrow_key(self, sender, app_data):
1438:         """Arrow Up/Down on the timestamp input shifts time."""
1439:         if not (dpg.does_item_exist("event_timestamp_input")
1440:                 and dpg.is_item_hovered("event_timestamp_input")):
1441:             return
1442:         shift = dpg.is_key_down(dpg.mvKey_LShift) or dpg.is_key_down(dpg.mvKey_RShift)
1443:         step = 1440 if shift else 15
1444:         if app_data == dpg.mvKey_Up:
1445:             self._shift_timestamp(step)
1446:         else:
1447:             self._shift_timestamp(-step)
1448: 
1449:     def _on_mouse_wheel(self, sender, app_data):
1450:         # app_data is positive when scrolling up, negative when down.
1451:         # We invert so scroll-up = higher value.
1452:         delta = 1 if app_data > 0 else -1
1453: 
1454:         # ── Timestamp scroll ──────────────────────────────────────────
1455:         if (dpg.does_item_exist("event_timestamp_input")
1456:                 and dpg.is_item_hovered("event_timestamp_input")):
1457:             shift = dpg.is_key_down(dpg.mvKey_LShift) or dpg.is_key_down(dpg.mvKey_RShift)
1458:             step = 1440 if shift else 15
1459:             self._shift_timestamp(delta * step)
1460:             return
1461: 
1462:         for tag, (items, cb) in list(getattr(self, '_scroll_combos', {}).items()):
1463:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag) and dpg.is_item_hovered(tag):
1464:                 itype = dpg.get_item_info(tag).get("type", "")
1465:                 if "Button" in itype:
1466:                     cur = dpg.get_item_configuration(tag).get("label")
1467:                 else:
1468:                     cur = str(dpg.get_value(tag))
1469:                 try:
1470:                     idx = items.index(str(cur))
1471:                 except ValueError:
1472:                     idx = 0
1473:                 new_idx = max(0, min(len(items) - 1, idx - delta))
1474:                 if "Button" in itype:
1475:                     dpg.configure_item(tag, label=items[new_idx])
1476:                 else:
1477:                     dpg.set_value(tag, items[new_idx])
1478:                 cb()
1479:                 return
1480:         for tag, (mn, mx, cb) in list(getattr(self, '_scroll_ints', {}).items()):
1481:             if dpg.does_item_exist(tag) and dpg.is_item_hovered(tag):
1482:                 try:
1483:                     cur = int(dpg.get_value(tag))
1484:                 except (ValueError, TypeError):
1485:                     cur = mn
1486:                 new_val = max(mn, min(mx, cur + delta))
1487:                 dpg.set_value(tag, str(new_val))
1488:                 if cb:
1489:                     cb()
1490:                 return
1491: 
1492:     def _is_over_slots_panel(self, x_root: int, y_root: int) -> bool:
1493:         try:
1494:             mn = dpg.get_item_rect_min("slots_scroll")
1495:             mx = dpg.get_item_rect_max("slots_scroll")
1496:         except Exception:
1497:             return False
1498:         return mn[0] <= x_root <= mx[0] and mn[1] <= y_root <= mx[1]
1499: 
1500:     # ── Resize handle logic ───────────────────────────────────────────
1501: 
1502:     _resize_dragging = False
1503:     _resize_start_y = 0
1504:     _resize_start_h = 360
1505: 
1506:     def _resize_handle_click(self, sender, app_data):
1507:         self._resize_dragging = True
1508:         self._resize_start_y = dpg.get_mouse_pos(local=False)[1]
1509:         try:
1510:             self._resize_start_h = dpg.get_item_height("right_tabs_content")
1511:         except Exception:
1512:             self._resize_start_h = 360
1513: 
1514:     def _resize_handle_drag(self, sender, app_data):
1515:         if not self._resize_dragging:
1516:             return
1517:         current_y = dpg.get_mouse_pos(local=False)[1]
1518:         delta = current_y - self._resize_start_y
1519:         new_h = max(80, self._resize_start_h + int(delta))
1520:         max_h = dpg.get_viewport_height() - 200
1521:         new_h = min(new_h, max_h)
1522:         dpg.configure_item("right_tabs_content", height=new_h)
1523: 
1524:     def _resize_handle_release(self, sender, app_data):
1525:         self._resize_dragging = False
1526:         # Update base dimensions so viewport resize scales from the user-set height
1527:         if dpg.does_item_exist("right_tabs_content"):
1528:             self._base_tabs_height = dpg.get_item_height("right_tabs_content")
1529:             self._base_vp_height = dpg.get_viewport_height()
1530:             # Persist divider position
1531:             self.settings["divider_height"] = self._base_tabs_height
1532:             self.save_settings()
1533: 
1534:     def _on_viewport_resize(self, sender=None, app_data=None):
1535:         """Scale right panel contents when the viewport is resized."""
1536:         vp_h = dpg.get_viewport_height()
1537:         base_vp = getattr(self, "_base_vp_height", 600)
1538:         base_tabs = getattr(self, "_base_tabs_height", 360)
1539: 
1540:         if base_vp <= 0 or not dpg.does_item_exist("right_tabs_content"):
1541:             return
1542:         new_h = max(80, int(base_tabs * vp_h / base_vp))
1543:         max_h = vp_h - 200
1544:         new_h = min(new_h, max_h)
1545:         dpg.configure_item("right_tabs_content", height=new_h)
</file>

<file path=".github/copilot-instructions.md">
  1: # Lineup Builder — Copilot Instructions
  2: 
  3: ## Architecture
  4: 
  5: Mixin-composition desktop app. A single `App` class (`src/frontend/app.py`) inherits **11 functional mixins**:
  6: 
  7: | # | Mixin | File | Responsibility |
  8: |---|-------|------|----------------|
  9: | 1 | `UISetupMixin` | `src/frontend/ui_builder.py` | DPG viewport, left/right panels, all widget construction, scroll/arrow-key handlers |
 10: | 2 | `RosterMixin` | `src/frontend/roster.py` | DJ roster CRUD, bulk link import, drag payload creation |
 11: | 3 | `DragDropMixin` | `src/frontend/drag_drop.py` | Slot reordering, DJ-card→slot drop targets, flash highlight |
 12: | 4 | `EventsMixin` | `src/frontend/events_manager.py` | Save/load/delete/duplicate event lineups (YAML-backed) |
 13: | 5 | `GenreMixin` | `src/frontend/genre_manager.py` | Genre tag add/delete/toggle, active-genre filtering |
 14: | 6 | `SlotMixin` | `src/frontend/slot_manager.py` | Slot CRUD, reorder, apply master duration |
 15: | 7 | `OutputMixin` | `src/backend/output_builder.py` | `_build_snapshot()` → `EventSnapshot`; `update_output()` drives preview |
 16: | 8 | `DataMixin` | `src/backend/data_manager.py` | YAML/JSON I/O, window geometry persistence, crash-recovery auto-save |
 17: | 9 | `SettingsMixin` | `src/frontend/settings_manager.py` | `load_settings()`, `apply_theme()`, preset management |
 18: | 10 | `DebounceMixin` | `src/backend/debounce.py` | Timer helpers + per-frame work queue (`process_queue()`) |
 19: | 11 | `ImportMixin` | `src/frontend/import_parser.py` | `_parse_event_text()` for Discord markdown and plain-text formats |
 20: 
 21: > **Rule:** `src/backend/` is **100% GUI-free** (no DearPyGui imports). Tests run against backend only.
 22: 
 23: | Layer | Location | Rule |
 24: |-------|----------|------|
 25: | Backend | `src/backend/` | Pure Python — **no DearPyGui imports** |
 26: | Frontend | `src/frontend/` | DearPyGui widgets, themes, fonts |
 27: | Tests | `tests/` | pytest, backend-only |
 28: 
 29: ---
 30: 
 31: ## State Flow
 32: 
 33: ```
 34: UI widgets
 35:   → DPGVar / DPGBoolVar wrappers (src/frontend/slot_ui.py)
 36:   → debounced callback (_schedule_update / _schedule_save_library)
 37:   → OutputMixin._build_snapshot()   →   EventSnapshot (frozen dataclass)
 38:   → OutputGenerator.generate(snap)  →   formatted string
 39:   → dpg.set_value(preview_tag, text)
 40: ```
 41: 
 42: Cross-module events use `EventBus` pub/sub (`src/backend/event_bus.py`).  
 43: `LineupModel` publishes `"model_changed"` on every state mutation.
 44: 
 45: ---
 46: 
 47: ## GUI Framework
 48: 
 49: **DearPyGui** (`import dearpygui.dearpygui as dpg`) — **NOT CustomTkinter**.
 50: 
 51: - Widgets use tag-based identity: `dpg.add_input_text(tag="event_title_input")`
 52: - Read/write state: `dpg.get_value(tag)` / `dpg.set_value(tag, value)`
 53: - State wrappers: `DPGVar` / `DPGBoolVar` (defined in `src/frontend/slot_ui.py`) replace `tk.StringVar` / `tk.BooleanVar`
 54: - `SlotState` (also in `slot_ui.py`) holds `name_var`, `genre_var`, `duration_var` per slot plus `row_tag` and `_id`
 55: - Main loop: `while dpg.is_dearpygui_running(): process_queue(); dpg.render_dearpygui_frame()`
 56: - All popup/modal windows inherit the **global theme** — **never** override with per-window themes
 57: - Viewport default: 1050×768, minimum 800×600
 58: 
 59: ---
 60: 
 61: ## Theme & Styling
 62: 
 63: - **Color tokens:** `src/frontend/theme.py` — hex string constants (`PANEL_BG`, `ACCENT`, etc.) + `hex_to_dpg()` converter + reusable style dicts (`ENTRY`, `COMBO`, `BTN_PRIMARY`, `BTN_DANGER`, `CARD`, etc.)
 64: - **Global theme:** Built dynamically in `SettingsMixin.apply_theme()` from `self.settings` color tokens. Maps 30+ DPG color slots + rounded-corner styles. Bound globally with `dpg.bind_theme(global_theme)`.
 65: - **Built-in presets** (8 total, in `BUILTIN_PRESETS` list in `settings_manager.py`):
 66:   - **Slate (Default)** — Dark slate blue
 67:   - **Midnight Blue** — Deep midnight blue with blue accents
 68:   - **OLED Black** — Near-pure-black for OLED displays
 69:   - **Crimson** — Deep red/rose tones
 70:   - **Amber** — Warm amber/gold tones
 71:   - **Forest** — Dark emerald green
 72:   - **Ocean** — Deep teal/cyan
 73:   - **Violet** — Rich purple
 74: - **User presets:** Saved to `settings.json` under `"user_presets"` array; managed via `save_current_as_preset()` / `delete_preset()`
 75: - **Fonts/icons:** `src/frontend/fonts.py` — `styled_text(label, style)` helper, `Icon` class (Material Symbols Rounded). Text styles: `HEADER`, `LABEL`, `BODY`, `MUTED`, `ERROR`, `SUCCESS`, `HINT`
 76: - **Widget factory:** `src/frontend/widgets.py` — `add_icon_button()`, `add_primary_button()`, `add_danger_button()`, `add_styled_input()`, `add_styled_combo()` — all apply correct theme automatically
 77: 
 78: ---
 79: 
 80: ## Debounce Timings
 81: 
 82: All mutations must go through `DebounceMixin` helpers — **never call `update_output()` or `_save_library()` directly from a callback**.
 83: 
 84: | Method | Delay | Purpose |
 85: |--------|-------|---------|
 86: | `_schedule_update()` | 150 ms | Refresh output preview after input changes |
 87: | `_schedule_roster_refresh()` | 120 ms | Redraw DJ roster panel |
 88: | `_schedule_genre_refresh()` | 120 ms | Redraw genre tags |
 89: | `_schedule_save_library()` | 500 ms | Persist DJ/genre library to YAML |
 90: | `_schedule_auto_save()` | 5 000 ms | Write crash-recovery state to `auto_save.json` |
 91: | `_schedule_auto_event_save()` | 1 500 ms | Write current event snapshot to `auto_save.json` |
 92: 
 93: `process_queue()` is called every DPG frame and drains the `self._work_queue` (SimpleQueue) for thread-safe main-thread execution.
 94: 
 95: ---
 96: 
 97: ## Output Generation
 98: 
 99: `OutputGenerator` (`src/backend/output_generator.py`) is **pure** — no GUI imports, no side effects.
100: 
101: ```python
102: # Entry point
103: OutputGenerator.generate(snapshot: EventSnapshot) -> str
104: 
105: # Helpers
106: OutputGenerator.compute_slot_times(snapshot) -> list[str]   # ["20:00", "20:30", ...]
107: OutputGenerator.vrcdn_convert(link: str, fmt: str) -> str   # "quest" | "pc"
108: ```
109: 
110: Supported `output_format` values on `EventSnapshot`: `"discord"` | `"local"` | `"quest"` | `"pc"`
111: 
112: `EventSnapshot` is a frozen dataclass with: `title`, `vol`, `timestamp`, `genres`, `slots` (`list[SlotData]`), `names_only`, `output_format`, `saved_djs` (`list[DJInfo]`). Computed properties: `start_datetime`, `full_title`.
113: 
114: ---
115: 
116: ## Conventions
117: 
118: - **Popup windows:** `modal=True`, `autosize=True`, `no_resize=True`, `no_scrollbar=True`. They inherit the global theme automatically. Examples: `save_warn_win`, `overwrite_confirm_win`, `dj_edit_win_{idx}`, `dt_picker_modal`, `import_dialog_win`.
119: - **File I/O:** YAML for events/library (`lineup_events.yaml`, `lineup_library.yaml`), JSON for settings/state (`settings.json`, `window_state.json`, `auto_save.json`). All paths via `get_data_dir()` from `src/frontend/utils.py`.
120: - **Thread safety:** Long-running work goes through `self._work_queue` (SimpleQueue), drained each frame by `process_queue()`.
121: - **Output generation is pure:** `OutputGenerator.generate(snapshot)` takes an immutable `EventSnapshot` and returns a string. No side effects.
122: - **Tag naming:** Widget tags use snake_case strings, e.g. `"event_title_input"`, `"slot_row_{id}"`, `"dj_card_{idx}"`.
123: - **`get_data_dir()`:** Returns `sys.executable`'s directory when frozen by PyInstaller, project root otherwise.
124: 
125: ---
126: 
127: ## Build & Test
128: 
129: ```bash
130: # Setup
131: python -m venv .venv && .venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1
132: pip install -r requirements.txt
133: 
134: # Run
135: python main.py          # production
136: python dev.py           # auto-restart on src/ file changes (watchdog)
137: 
138: # Test
139: pytest tests/ -v
140: 
141: # Build exe
142: pip install pyinstaller
143: python -m PyInstaller lineup_builder.spec --clean
144: ```
145: 
146: ---
147: 
148: ## Code Style
149: 
150: - Linter: **Ruff** (E, F, W, I rules; `line-length = 100`; E501 ignored)
151: - Python 3.11+ (type unions with `|`, f-strings, `match` statements allowed)
152: - No type annotations required on existing code unless adding new public APIs
153: - `pyproject.toml` is the single source for tool config (Ruff, pytest)
</file>

 1: import sys
 2: from pathlib import Path
 3: 
 4: # Add desktop/ to sys.path so internal src.* imports resolve
 5: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent / "desktop"))
 6: 
 7: from src.frontend.app import App
 8: 
 9: 
10: def main():
11:     # Set Windows AppUserModelID so the taskbar shows our icon, not Python's
12:     if sys.platform == "win32":
13:         import ctypes
14:         ctypes.windll.shell32.SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID(
15:             "baebu.lineupbuilder"
16:         )
17: 
18:     app = App()
19:     app.run()
20: 
21: 
22: if __name__ == "__main__":
23:     main()
